Option Explicit

Private Const CLASS_NAME As String = "modPdfForensicCompare"
Private Const TARGET_X_FIRST As Double = 468#
Private Const TARGET_X_SECOND As Double = 639#
Private Const COORD_TOLERANCE As Double = 0.1
Private Const TARGET_TOLERANCE As Double = 0.25
Private Const MAX_DETAIL_LINES As Long = 500
Private Const ERR_BASE As Long = vbObjectError + 4200
Private Const BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE As Double = 0.15
Private Const BREAKS_GEOMETRY_TEXT_X_TOLERANCE As Double = 25#
Private Const BREAKS_GEOMETRY_TEXT_Y_TOLERANCE As Double = 2#
Private Const BREAKS_LINE_MATCH_TOLERANCE As Double = 0.75
Private Const BREAKS_LINE_NEAR_TOLERANCE As Double = 6#
Private Const BREAKS_FULL_WIDTH_MIN As Double = 1200#
Private Const BREAKS_BASELINE_TOLERANCE As Double = 1#
Private Const BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE As Long = 120
Private Const BREAKS_IGNORE_INPUT_CONTENT_DIFFERENCES As Boolean = True
Private Const ORACLE_FIRST_PAGE_MAX_TRADES As Long = 11
Private Const ORACLE_CONTINUATION_PAGE_MAX_TRADES As Long = 12
Private Const ORACLE_MIN_DATA_ROW_STEP As Double = 16#

Private Type tPdfLine
    PageNo As Long
    X1 As Double
    Y1 As Double
    X2 As Double
    Y2 As Double
    LineWidth As Double
    PaintOperator As String
    SourceOperator As String
    StrokeColor As String
End Type

Private Type tPdfText
    PageNo As Long

    Y As Double
    FontSize As Double
    FontName As String
    TextValue As String
End Type

Private Type tPdfImage
    PageNo As Long
    ResourceName As String
    A As Double
    B As Double
    C As Double
    D As Double
    E As Double
    F As Double
End Type

Private Type tPdfPage
    ObjectNo As Long
    MediaBox As String
    CropBox As String
    RotateValue As String
    FontDictionary As String
    ResolvedFonts As String
    ContentFilters As String
    ContentText As String
    ContentLength As Long
    ImageStart As Long
    ImageCount As Long
    LineStart As Long
    LineCount As Long
    TextStart As Long
    TextCount As Long
End Type

Private Type tPdfDocument
    PdfPath As String
    PdfVersion As String
    FileSize As Long
    ObjectCount As Long
    XrefKind As String
    PageCount As Long
    Pages() As tPdfPage
    Lines() As tPdfLine
    LineCount As Long
    Images() As tPdfImage
    ImageCount As Long
    Texts() As tPdfText
    TextCount As Long
End Type

Private m_objNum() As Long
Private m_objPos() As Long
Private m_objCount As Long

Private m_pendingX1() As Double
Private m_pendingY1() As Double
Private m_pendingX2() As Double
Private m_pendingY2() As Double

Private m_currentStrokeColor As String

Private m_stackB() As Double
Private m_stackC() As Double

Private m_subPathStartY As Double

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Lets the user select one PDF and creates a forensic geometry report.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub RunPdfForensicAnalysisBrowse()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "RunPdfForensicAnalysisBrowse"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strOutputPath As String
    Dim strPdfPath As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strPdfPath = SelectPdfFile("Select the PDF to analyse")
    If Len(strPdfPath) = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    strOutputPath = BuildForensicOutputPath(strPdfPath)
    If Not PdfForensicAnalyse(strPdfPath, strOutputPath) Then
        Err.Raise ERR_BASE + 1, METHOD_NAME, "The forensic PDF analysis could not be completed."
    End If

    MsgBox _
        "Forensic PDF analysis completed:" & vbCrLf & strOutputPath, _
        vbInformation, _
        "PDF forensic analysis"

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pdfPath;outputPath", _
        strPdfPath, _
        strOutputPath

    errorManager.save
    errorManager.display "The forensic PDF analysis failed."
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Lets the user select an old and a new PDF and creates a comparison report.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub RunPdfComparisonBrowse()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "RunPdfComparisonBrowse"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strAuditPath As String
    Dim strBreaksPath As String
    Dim strBreaksSummaryPath As String
    Dim strDetailPath As String
    Dim strNewPdfPath As String
    Dim strOldPdfPath As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strOldPdfPath = SelectPdfFile("Select the OLD Oracle reference PDF")
    If Len(strOldPdfPath) = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    strNewPdfPath = SelectPdfFile("Select the NEW generated PDF")
    If Len(strNewPdfPath) = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    strAuditPath = BuildOracleAuditOutputPath(strOldPdfPath, strNewPdfPath)
    strDetailPath = BuildComparisonOutputPath(strOldPdfPath, strNewPdfPath)
    strBreaksPath = BuildBreaksOnlyOutputPath(strOldPdfPath, strNewPdfPath)
    strBreaksSummaryPath = BuildBreaksSummaryOutputPath(strOldPdfPath, strNewPdfPath)

    If Not PdfOracleCloneAudit( _
        strOldPdfPath, _
        strNewPdfPath, _
        strAuditPath, _
        strDetailPath, _
        strBreaksPath, _
        strBreaksSummaryPath) Then

        Err.Raise ERR_BASE + 2, METHOD_NAME, "The Oracle clone PDF audit could not be completed."
    End If

    MsgBox _
        "Oracle clone audit completed." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "SEND / SCREENSHOT THIS FILE:" & vbCrLf & strBreaksSummaryPath & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Full remaining breaks:" & vbCrLf & strBreaksPath & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Main audit:" & vbCrLf & strAuditPath & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Detailed vector comparison:" & vbCrLf & strDetailPath, _
        vbInformation, _
        "Oracle PDF audit"

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldPdfPath;newPdfPath;auditPath;detailPath;breaksPath;breaksSummaryPath", _
        strOldPdfPath, _
        strNewPdfPath, _
        strAuditPath, _
        strDetailPath, _
        strBreaksPath, _
        strBreaksSummaryPath

    errorManager.save
    errorManager.display "The Oracle PDF audit failed."
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfPath As String, outputPath As String
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Analyses one PDF and writes detailed vector and text diagnostics.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function PdfForensicAnalyse( _
    ByVal pdfPath As String, _
    ByVal outputPath As String) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PdfForensicAnalyse"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim pdfDoc As tPdfDocument

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Len(Trim$(pdfPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1001, METHOD_NAME, "The PDF path is required."
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(outputPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1020, METHOD_NAME, "The output path is required."
    End If

    Call LoadPdfDocument(pdfPath, pdfDoc)
    If pdfDoc.PageCount = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "The PDF could not be parsed into page content."
    End If
    Call WriteForensicReport(pdfDoc, outputPath)
    PdfForensicAnalyse = True

ExitFunction:
    Call ResetParserState
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    PdfForensicAnalyse = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pdfPath;outputPath", _
        pdfPath, _
        outputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    oldPdfPath As String, newPdfPath As String, outputPath As String
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Compares two PDFs and writes structural and targeted separator differences.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function PdfCompare( _
    ByVal oldPdfPath As String, _
    ByVal newPdfPath As String, _
    ByVal outputPath As String) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PdfCompare"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim newDoc As tPdfDocument
    Dim oldDoc As tPdfDocument

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Len(Trim$(oldPdfPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1001, METHOD_NAME, "The old PDF path is required."
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(newPdfPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1001, METHOD_NAME, "The new PDF path is required."
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(outputPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1020, METHOD_NAME, "The output path is required."
    End If

    Call LoadPdfDocument(oldPdfPath, oldDoc)
    If oldDoc.PageCount = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "The old PDF could not be parsed into page content."
    End If
    Call LoadPdfDocument(newPdfPath, newDoc)
    If newDoc.PageCount = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "The new PDF could not be parsed into page content."
    End If
    Call WriteComparisonReport(oldDoc, newDoc, outputPath)
    PdfCompare = True

ExitFunction:
    Call ResetParserState
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    PdfCompare = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldPdfPath;newPdfPath;outputPath", _
        oldPdfPath, _
        newPdfPath, _
        outputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    dialogTitle As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Displays a file picker and returns the selected PDF path.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function SelectPdfFile(ByVal dialogTitle As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "SelectPdfFile"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim fileDialog As Office.FileDialog

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set fileDialog = Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFilePicker)
    With fileDialog
        .Title = dialogTitle
        .AllowMultiSelect = False
        .Filters.Clear
        .Filters.Add "PDF files", "*.pdf"
        If .Show = -1 Then
            SelectPdfFile = .SelectedItems(1)
        End If
    End With

ExitFunction:
    Set fileDialog = Nothing
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    SelectPdfFile = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "dialogTitle", _
        dialogTitle

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfPath As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Builds the default forensic report path beside the PDF.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildForensicOutputPath(ByVal pdfPath As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildForensicOutputPath"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngDotPosition As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngDotPosition = InStrRev(pdfPath, ".")
    If lngDotPosition > InStrRev(pdfPath, "\") Then
        BuildForensicOutputPath = Left$(pdfPath, lngDotPosition - 1) & "_forensic_geometry.txt"
    Else
        BuildForensicOutputPath = pdfPath & "_forensic_geometry.txt"
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BuildForensicOutputPath = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pdfPath", _
        pdfPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    oldPdfPath As String, newPdfPath As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Builds the default PDF comparison report path beside the new PDF.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildComparisonOutputPath( _
    ByVal oldPdfPath As String, _
    ByVal newPdfPath As String) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildComparisonOutputPath"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strNewBase As String
    Dim strNewFolder As String
    Dim strOldBase As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strNewFolder = Left$(newPdfPath, InStrRev(newPdfPath, "\"))
    strNewBase = FileBaseName(newPdfPath)
    strOldBase = FileBaseName(oldPdfPath)
    BuildComparisonOutputPath = strNewFolder & strOldBase & "_VS_" & strNewBase & "_pdf_compare.txt"

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BuildComparisonOutputPath = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldPdfPath;newPdfPath", _
        oldPdfPath, _
        newPdfPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfPath As String, pdfDoc As tPdfDocument
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Loads and parses a PDF into a normalized vector and text model.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub LoadPdfDocument( _
    ByVal pdfPath As String, _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "LoadPdfDocument"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrPageRefs() As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngPageCount As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Len(Dir$(pdfPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1010, METHOD_NAME, "The PDF file was not found: " & pdfPath
    End If

    Call ResetParserState
    m_pdf = ReadBytes(pdfPath)
    Call BuildObjectIndex

    pdfDoc.PdfPath = pdfPath
    pdfDoc.FileSize = UBound(m_pdf) + 1
    pdfDoc.PdfVersion = DetectPdfVersion
    pdfDoc.ObjectCount = m_objCount
    pdfDoc.XrefKind = DetectXrefKind
    ReDim pdfDoc.Pages(1 To 8)
    ReDim pdfDoc.Lines(0 To 2047)
    ReDim pdfDoc.Images(0 To 255)
    ReDim pdfDoc.Texts(0 To 2047)

    lngPageCount = PageObjectOrder(arrPageRefs)
    If lngPageCount = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "No PDF page objects were found."
    End If

    For lngIndex = 1 To lngPageCount
        Call ParsePage(arrPageRefs(lngIndex), lngIndex, pdfDoc)
    Next lngIndex

    If pdfDoc.LineCount = 0 And pdfDoc.TextCount = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "No vector lines or text runs were extracted from the PDF page streams."
    End If

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    pdfDoc.PageCount = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pdfPath", _
        pdfPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Clears all module-level PDF parsing state.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub ResetParserState()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ResetParserState"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Erase m_pdf
    Erase m_objNum
    Erase m_objPos
    Erase m_pendingX1
    Erase m_pendingY1
    Erase m_pendingX2
    Erase m_pendingY2
    Erase m_pendingSource
    Erase m_stackA
    Erase m_stackB
    Erase m_stackC
    Erase m_stackD
    Erase m_stackE
    Erase m_stackF
    Erase m_stackLineWidth
    Erase m_stackStrokeColor
    m_objCount = 0
    m_pendingCount = 0
    m_stackCount = 0

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    filePath As String
' Returns:       Byte array
' Description:   Reads the complete PDF file into a byte array.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function ReadBytes(ByVal filePath As String) As Byte()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ReadBytes"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrBuffer() As Byte
    Dim blnFileOpen As Boolean
    Dim lngFileNumber As Long
    Dim lngFileSize As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngFileNumber = FreeFile
    Open filePath For Binary Access Read As #lngFileNumber
    blnFileOpen = True
    lngFileSize = LOF(lngFileNumber)
    If lngFileSize <= 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1011, METHOD_NAME, "The PDF file is empty."
    End If

    ReDim arrBuffer(0 To lngFileSize - 1)
    Get #lngFileNumber, 1, arrBuffer
    ReadBytes = arrBuffer

ExitFunction:
    If blnFileOpen Then
        Close #lngFileNumber
    End If
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "filePath", _
        filePath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Builds an index of indirect PDF objects.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub BuildObjectIndex()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildObjectIndex"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrParts() As String
    Dim blnGenerationFound As Boolean
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngPosition As Long
    Dim strHeader As String
    Dim strObjectNumber As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    m_objCount = 0
    ReDim m_objNum(0 To 255)
    ReDim m_objPos(0 To 255)
    lngPosition = 0

    Do
        lngPosition = FindBytes(m_pdf, lngPosition, " obj")
        If lngPosition < 0 Then
            Exit Do
        End If

        strHeader = BytesText(m_pdf, IIf(lngPosition - 24 < 0, 0, lngPosition - 24), lngPosition - 1)
        strHeader = Replace(Replace(strHeader, vbCr, " "), vbLf, " ")
        arrParts = Split(Trim$(strHeader), " ")
        strObjectNumber = vbNullString
        blnGenerationFound = False

        For lngIndex = UBound(arrParts) To LBound(arrParts) Step -1
            If Len(arrParts(lngIndex)) > 0 Then
                If IsNumeric(arrParts(lngIndex)) Then
                    If Not blnGenerationFound Then
                        blnGenerationFound = True
                    Else
                        strObjectNumber = arrParts(lngIndex)
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Else
                    Exit For
                End If
            End If
        Next lngIndex

        If Len(strObjectNumber) > 0 Then
            If m_objCount > UBound(m_objNum) Then
                ReDim Preserve m_objNum(0 To m_objCount * 2)
                ReDim Preserve m_objPos(0 To m_objCount * 2)
            End If
            m_objNum(m_objCount) = CLng(strObjectNumber)
            m_objPos(m_objCount) = lngPosition + 4
            m_objCount = m_objCount + 1
        End If

        lngPosition = lngPosition + 4
    Loop

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    objectNo As Long
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Returns the index of a PDF object or minus one when absent.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function IndexOfObject(ByVal objectNo As Long) As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IndexOfObject"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    IndexOfObject = -1
    For lngIndex = 0 To m_objCount - 1
        If m_objNum(lngIndex) = objectNo Then
            IndexOfObject = lngIndex
            Exit For
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    IndexOfObject = -1

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "objectNo", _
        objectNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    objectPosition As Long
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns the dictionary text of an indirect PDF object.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function ObjectDict(ByVal objectPosition As Long) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ObjectDict"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngEndObject As Long
    Dim lngEndStream As Long
    Dim lngStopPosition As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngEndStream = FindBytes(m_pdf, objectPosition, "stream")
    lngEndObject = FindBytes(m_pdf, objectPosition, "endobj")

    If lngEndStream < 0 Then
        lngStopPosition = lngEndObject
    ElseIf lngEndObject < 0 Then
        lngStopPosition = lngEndStream
    ElseIf lngEndStream < lngEndObject Then
        lngStopPosition = lngEndStream
    Else
        lngStopPosition = lngEndObject
    End If

    If lngStopPosition < 0 Then
        lngStopPosition = objectPosition + 4000
    End If
    If lngStopPosition - objectPosition > 8000 Then
        lngStopPosition = objectPosition + 8000
    End If

    ObjectDict = Replace(Replace(BytesText(m_pdf, objectPosition, lngStopPosition - 1), vbCr, " "), vbLf, " ")

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    ObjectDict = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "objectPosition", _
        objectPosition

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    objectPosition As Long, dataStart As Long, dataEnd As Long
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Locates the byte bounds of a PDF stream.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function StreamBounds( _
    ByVal objectPosition As Long, _
    ByRef dataStart As Long, _
    ByRef dataEnd As Long) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "StreamBounds"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngDeclaredLength As Long
    Dim lngEndObject As Long
    Dim lngEndStream As Long
    Dim lngStreamPosition As Long
    Dim strProbe As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngStreamPosition = FindBytes(m_pdf, objectPosition, "stream")
    lngEndObject = FindBytes(m_pdf, objectPosition, "endobj")
    If lngStreamPosition < 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If
    If lngEndObject >= 0 And lngStreamPosition > lngEndObject Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    dataStart = lngStreamPosition + 6
    If dataStart > UBound(m_pdf) Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If
    If m_pdf(dataStart) = 13 Then
        dataStart = dataStart + 1
    End If
    If m_pdf(dataStart) = 10 Then
        dataStart = dataStart + 1
    End If

    lngDeclaredLength = DeclaredLength(ObjectDict(objectPosition))
    If lngDeclaredLength > 0 Then
        If dataStart + lngDeclaredLength + 20 <= UBound(m_pdf) Then
            strProbe = BytesText(m_pdf, dataStart + lngDeclaredLength, dataStart + lngDeclaredLength + 20)
            If InStr(1, strProbe, "endstream", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
                dataEnd = dataStart + lngDeclaredLength - 1
                StreamBounds = True
                GoTo ExitFunction
            End If
        End If
    End If

    lngEndStream = FindBytes(m_pdf, dataStart, "endstream")
    If lngEndStream < 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    dataEnd = lngEndStream - 1
    Do While dataEnd >= dataStart
        If m_pdf(dataEnd) <> 10 And m_pdf(dataEnd) <> 13 Then
            Exit Do
        End If
        dataEnd = dataEnd - 1
    Loop

    StreamBounds = True

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    StreamBounds = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "objectPosition", _
        objectPosition

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    dictionaryText As String
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Returns a direct numeric stream length from a PDF dictionary.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DeclaredLength(ByVal dictionaryText As String) As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DeclaredLength"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngKeyPosition As Long
    Dim strCharacter As String
    Dim strNumber As String
    Dim strTail As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngKeyPosition = InStr(1, dictionaryText, "/Length", vbBinaryCompare)
    If lngKeyPosition = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    lngIndex = lngKeyPosition + 7
    Do While lngIndex <= Len(dictionaryText)
        If Mid$(dictionaryText, lngIndex, 1) <> " " Then
            Exit Do
        End If
        lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
    Loop

    Do While lngIndex <= Len(dictionaryText)
        strCharacter = Mid$(dictionaryText, lngIndex, 1)
        If strCharacter < "0" Or strCharacter > "9" Then
            Exit Do
        End If
        strNumber = strNumber & strCharacter
        lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
    Loop

    If Len(strNumber) = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    strTail = LTrim$(Mid$(dictionaryText, lngIndex, 8))
    If InStr(1, strTail, " R", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    DeclaredLength = CLng(strNumber)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    DeclaredLength = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    dataStart As Long
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Returns the zlib header size before a DEFLATE stream.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function ZlibSkip(ByVal dataStart As Long) As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ZlibSkip"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngFirst As Long
    Dim lngSecond As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If dataStart + 1 > UBound(m_pdf) Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    lngFirst = m_pdf(dataStart)
    lngSecond = m_pdf(dataStart + 1)
    If (lngFirst And &HF) = 8 Then
        If ((lngFirst * 256 + lngSecond) Mod 31) = 0 Then
            ZlibSkip = 2
        End If
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    ZlibSkip = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "dataStart", _
        dataStart

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pageRefs As Long array
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Resolves the document page tree in display order.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function PageObjectOrder(ByRef pageRefs() As Long) As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PageObjectOrder"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngPagesObject As Long
    Dim lngPageCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strDictionary As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    ReDim pageRefs(1 To 8)
    lngPagesObject = FindCatalogPagesObject
    If lngPagesObject > 0 Then
        Call CollectPageTree(lngPagesObject, pageRefs, lngPageCount)
    End If

    If lngPageCount = 0 Then
        For lngIndex = 0 To m_objCount - 1
            strDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngIndex))
            If IsPageDictionary(strDictionary) Then
                Call AppendPageReference(pageRefs, lngPageCount, m_objNum(lngIndex))
            End If
        Next lngIndex
    End If

    PageObjectOrder = lngPageCount

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    PageObjectOrder = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Finds the root Pages object referenced by the PDF catalog.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindCatalogPagesObject() As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindCatalogPagesObject"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrReferences() As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strDictionary As String
    Dim strReferences As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To m_objCount - 1
        strDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngIndex))
        If InStr(1, Replace(strDictionary, " ", vbNullString), "/Type/Catalog", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
            strReferences = RefList(strDictionary, "/Pages")
            If Len(strReferences) > 0 Then
                arrReferences = Split(strReferences, ",")
                FindCatalogPagesObject = CLng(arrReferences(0))
                Exit For
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FindCatalogPagesObject = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    objectNo As Long, pageRefs As Long array, pageCount As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Recursively expands a Pages tree into ordered page object references.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub CollectPageTree( _
    ByVal objectNo As Long, _
    ByRef pageRefs() As Long, _
    ByRef pageCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CollectPageTree"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrReferences() As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngObjectIndex As Long
    Dim strDictionary As String
    Dim strReferenceList As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngObjectIndex = IndexOfObject(objectNo)
    If lngObjectIndex < 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    strDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngObjectIndex))
    If IsPageDictionary(strDictionary) Then
        Call AppendPageReference(pageRefs, pageCount, objectNo)
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    strReferenceList = RefList(strDictionary, "/Kids")
    If Len(strReferenceList) = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    arrReferences = Split(strReferenceList, ",")
    For lngIndex = LBound(arrReferences) To UBound(arrReferences)
        Call CollectPageTree(CLng(arrReferences(lngIndex)), pageRefs, pageCount)
    Next lngIndex

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "objectNo;pageCount", _
        objectNo, _
        pageCount

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    dictionaryText As String
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Returns whether a dictionary represents one page rather than a Pages node.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function IsPageDictionary(ByVal dictionaryText As String) As Boolean
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IsPageDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strCompact As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strCompact = Replace(dictionaryText, " ", vbNullString)
    IsPageDictionary = _
        InStr(1, strCompact, "/Type/Page", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 And _
        InStr(1, strCompact, "/Type/Pages", vbBinaryCompare) = 0

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    IsPageDictionary = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pageRefs As Long array, pageCount As Long, objectNo As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Adds one page object reference to a dynamic array.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AppendPageReference( _
    ByRef pageRefs() As Long, _
    ByRef pageCount As Long, _
    ByVal objectNo As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AppendPageReference"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    pageCount = pageCount + 1
    If pageCount > UBound(pageRefs) Then
        ReDim Preserve pageRefs(1 To UBound(pageRefs) * 2)
    End If
    pageRefs(pageCount) = objectNo

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageCount;objectNo", _
        pageCount, _
        objectNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pageDictionary As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns the concatenated and decompressed content streams of a page.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function PageContent(ByVal pageDictionary As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PageContent"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrReferences() As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngObjectIndex As Long
    Dim strContent As String
    Dim strReferenceList As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strReferenceList = RefList(pageDictionary, "/Contents")
    If Len(strReferenceList) = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    arrReferences = Split(strReferenceList, ",")
    For lngIndex = LBound(arrReferences) To UBound(arrReferences)
        lngObjectIndex = IndexOfObject(CLng(arrReferences(lngIndex)))
        If lngObjectIndex >= 0 Then
            strContent = strContent & StreamText(m_objPos(lngObjectIndex)) & vbLf
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    PageContent = strContent

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    PageContent = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    objectPosition As Long
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns an uncompressed text representation of a PDF stream.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function StreamText(ByVal objectPosition As Long) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "StreamText"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrDecoded() As Byte
    Dim arrWorking() As Byte
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngDataEnd As Long
    Dim lngDataStart As Long
    Dim lngDecodedLength As Long
    Dim lngWorkingLength As Long
    Dim strDecodeError As String
    Dim strDictionary As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Not StreamBounds(objectPosition, lngDataStart, lngDataEnd) Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    strDictionary = ObjectDict(objectPosition)
    Call CopyByteRange(m_pdf, lngDataStart, lngDataEnd, arrWorking, lngWorkingLength)

    If InStr(1, strDictionary, "/ASCII85Decode", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Or _
       InStr(1, strDictionary, "/A85", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then

        If Not DecodeAscii85Bytes(arrWorking, lngWorkingLength, arrDecoded, lngDecodedLength) Then
            Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "The PDF ASCII85 stream could not be decoded."
        End If
        Call ReplaceWorkingBytes(arrWorking, lngWorkingLength, arrDecoded, lngDecodedLength)
    End If

    If InStr(1, strDictionary, "/ASCIIHexDecode", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Or _
       InStr(1, strDictionary, "/AHx", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then

        If Not DecodeAsciiHexBytes(arrWorking, lngWorkingLength, arrDecoded, lngDecodedLength) Then
            Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "The PDF ASCIIHex stream could not be decoded."
        End If
        Call ReplaceWorkingBytes(arrWorking, lngWorkingLength, arrDecoded, lngDecodedLength)
    End If

    If InStr(1, strDictionary, "/FlateDecode", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Or _
       InStr(1, strDictionary, "/Fl", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then

        If Not TryInflateBytes( _
            arrWorking, _
            lngWorkingLength, _
            arrDecoded, _
            lngDecodedLength, _
            strDecodeError) Then

            Err.Raise _
                1027, _
                METHOD_NAME, _
                "The PDF Flate stream could not be decoded. " & strDecodeError
        End If
        Call ReplaceWorkingBytes(arrWorking, lngWorkingLength, arrDecoded, lngDecodedLength)
    End If

    If InStr(1, strDictionary, "/LZWDecode", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "LZWDecode is not supported by the forensic comparison module."
    End If

    StreamText = ByteArrayText(arrWorking, lngWorkingLength)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    StreamText = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "objectPosition;filterDictionary", _
        objectPosition, _
        Left$(strDictionary, 500)

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    sourceBytes, sourceStart, sourceEnd, resultBytes, resultLength
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Copies one inclusive byte range into a zero-based byte array.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub CopyByteRange( _
    ByRef sourceBytes() As Byte, _
    ByVal sourceStart As Long, _
    ByVal sourceEnd As Long, _
    ByRef resultBytes() As Byte, _
    ByRef resultLength As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CopyByteRange"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    resultLength = 0
    If sourceEnd < sourceStart Then
        ReDim resultBytes(0 To 0)
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    resultLength = sourceEnd - sourceStart + 1
    ReDim resultBytes(0 To resultLength - 1)
    For lngIndex = 0 To resultLength - 1
        resultBytes(lngIndex) = sourceBytes(sourceStart + lngIndex)
    Next lngIndex

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    resultLength = 0
    ReDim resultBytes(0 To 0)

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "sourceStart;sourceEnd", _
        sourceStart, _
        sourceEnd

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    workingBytes, workingLength, newBytes, newLength
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Replaces the current decoding buffer with another byte array.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub ReplaceWorkingBytes( _
    ByRef workingBytes() As Byte, _
    ByRef workingLength As Long, _
    ByRef newBytes() As Byte, _
    ByVal newLength As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ReplaceWorkingBytes"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    workingLength = newLength
    If newLength <= 0 Then
        ReDim workingBytes(0 To 0)
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    ReDim workingBytes(0 To newLength - 1)
    For lngIndex = 0 To newLength - 1
        workingBytes(lngIndex) = newBytes(lngIndex)
    Next lngIndex

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    workingLength = 0
    ReDim workingBytes(0 To 0)

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "newLength", _
        newLength

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    sourceBytes, sourceLength, resultBytes, resultLength, decodeError
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Inflates either zlib-wrapped or raw DEFLATE bytes without logging expected retries.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function TryInflateBytes( _
    ByRef sourceBytes() As Byte, _
    ByVal sourceLength As Long, _
    ByRef resultBytes() As Byte, _
    ByRef resultLength As Long, _
    ByRef decodeError As String) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "TryInflateBytes"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngAttemptError As Long
    Dim lngSkip As Long
    Dim strAttemptError As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    resultLength = 0
    decodeError = vbNullString
    If sourceLength <= 0 Then
        decodeError = "The stream is empty."
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    lngSkip = ZlibSkipBytes(sourceBytes, sourceLength)

    On Error Resume Next
    VBA.Err.Clear
    resultLength = modPdfReport.InflateStream( _
        sourceBytes, _
        lngSkip, _
        sourceLength - 1, _
        resultBytes)
    lngAttemptError = VBA.Err.Number
    strAttemptError = VBA.Err.Description
    VBA.Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If lngAttemptError = 0 And resultLength > 0 Then
        TryInflateBytes = True
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    If lngSkip <> 0 Then
        On Error Resume Next
        VBA.Err.Clear
        resultLength = modPdfReport.InflateStream( _
            sourceBytes, _
            0, _
            sourceLength - 1, _
            resultBytes)
        lngAttemptError = VBA.Err.Number
        strAttemptError = VBA.Err.Description
        VBA.Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

        If lngAttemptError = 0 And resultLength > 0 Then
            TryInflateBytes = True
            GoTo ExitFunction
        End If
    End If

    decodeError = "DEFLATE decoder error " & CStr(lngAttemptError) & ": " & strAttemptError

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    resultLength = 0
    TryInflateBytes = False
    decodeError = errDescription

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "sourceLength;zlibSkip", _
        sourceLength, _
        lngSkip

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    sourceBytes, sourceLength
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Returns two when the byte array starts with a valid zlib header.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function ZlibSkipBytes( _
    ByRef sourceBytes() As Byte, _
    ByVal sourceLength As Long) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ZlibSkipBytes"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngFirst As Long
    Dim lngSecond As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If sourceLength < 2 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    lngFirst = CLng(sourceBytes(0))
    lngSecond = CLng(sourceBytes(1))
    If (lngFirst And &HF) = 8 Then
        If ((lngFirst * 256 + lngSecond) Mod 31) = 0 Then
            ZlibSkipBytes = 2
        End If
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    ZlibSkipBytes = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "sourceLength", _
        sourceLength

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    sourceBytes, sourceLength, resultBytes, resultLength
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Decodes a PDF ASCII85 stream, including z shorthand and partial groups.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DecodeAscii85Bytes( _
    ByRef sourceBytes() As Byte, _
    ByVal sourceLength As Long, _
    ByRef resultBytes() As Byte, _
    ByRef resultLength As Long) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DecodeAscii85Bytes"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrGroup(0 To 4) As Long
    Dim dblValue As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngByte As Long
    Dim lngGroupCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngOriginalCount As Long
    Dim lngOutputIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    resultLength = 0
    ReDim resultBytes(0 To sourceLength + 3)

    For lngIndex = 0 To sourceLength - 1
        lngByte = CLng(sourceBytes(lngIndex))

        If IsPdfWhiteSpaceByte(lngByte) Then
            GoTo ContinueLoop
        End If

        If lngByte = 60 Then
            If lngIndex + 1 < sourceLength Then
                If sourceBytes(lngIndex + 1) = 126 Then
                    lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
                    GoTo ContinueLoop
                End If
            End If
        End If

        If lngByte = 126 Then
            Exit For
        End If

        If lngByte = 122 Then
            If lngGroupCount <> 0 Then
                Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "ASCII85 z shorthand appeared inside a partial group."
            End If
            resultBytes(lngOutputIndex) = 0
            resultBytes(lngOutputIndex + 1) = 0
            resultBytes(lngOutputIndex + 2) = 0
            resultBytes(lngOutputIndex + 3) = 0
            lngOutputIndex = lngOutputIndex + 4
            GoTo ContinueLoop
        End If

        If lngByte < 33 Or lngByte > 117 Then
            Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "Invalid ASCII85 character code: " & CStr(lngByte)
        End If

        arrGroup(lngGroupCount) = lngByte - 33
        lngGroupCount = lngGroupCount + 1

        If lngGroupCount = 5 Then
            dblValue = Ascii85GroupValue(arrGroup)
            Call AppendAscii85Value(resultBytes, lngOutputIndex, dblValue, 4)
            lngGroupCount = 0
        End If
ContinueLoop:
    Next lngIndex

    If lngGroupCount = 1 Then
        Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "ASCII85 final group contains only one character."
    End If

    If lngGroupCount > 1 Then
        lngOriginalCount = lngGroupCount
        Do While lngGroupCount < 5
            arrGroup(lngGroupCount) = 84
            lngGroupCount = lngGroupCount + 1
        Loop
        dblValue = Ascii85GroupValue(arrGroup)
        Call AppendAscii85Value(resultBytes, lngOutputIndex, dblValue, lngOriginalCount - 1)
    End If

    resultLength = lngOutputIndex
    If resultLength = 0 Then
        ReDim resultBytes(0 To 0)
    Else
        ReDim Preserve resultBytes(0 To resultLength - 1)
    End If
    DecodeAscii85Bytes = True

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    resultLength = 0
    ReDim resultBytes(0 To 0)
    DecodeAscii85Bytes = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "sourceLength;inputIndex", _
        sourceLength, _
        lngIndex

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    groupValues
' Returns:       Double
' Description:   Converts five ASCII85 digits into one unsigned 32-bit value.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function Ascii85GroupValue(ByRef groupValues() As Long) As Double
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "Ascii85GroupValue"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblValue As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To 4
        dblValue = dblValue * 85# + CDbl(groupValues(lngIndex))
    Next lngIndex
    If dblValue > 4294967295# Then
        Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "ASCII85 group exceeds the unsigned 32-bit range."
    End If
    Ascii85GroupValue = dblValue

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Ascii85GroupValue = 0#

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    resultBytes, outputIndex, unsignedValue, byteCount
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Appends up to four big-endian bytes from an unsigned 32-bit value.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AppendAscii85Value( _
    ByRef resultBytes() As Byte, _
    ByRef outputIndex As Long, _
    ByVal unsignedValue As Double, _
    ByVal byteCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AppendAscii85Value"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrValues(0 To 3) As Long
    Dim dblRemainder As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    dblRemainder = unsignedValue
    arrValues(0) = Fix(dblRemainder / 16777216#)
    dblRemainder = dblRemainder - CDbl(arrValues(0)) * 16777216#
    arrValues(1) = Fix(dblRemainder / 65536#)
    dblRemainder = dblRemainder - CDbl(arrValues(1)) * 65536#
    arrValues(2) = Fix(dblRemainder / 256#)
    dblRemainder = dblRemainder - CDbl(arrValues(2)) * 256#
    arrValues(3) = Fix(dblRemainder)

    For lngIndex = 0 To byteCount - 1
        resultBytes(outputIndex) = CByte(arrValues(lngIndex))
        outputIndex = outputIndex + 1
    Next lngIndex

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "unsignedValue;byteCount;outputIndex", _
        unsignedValue, _
        byteCount, _
        outputIndex

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    sourceBytes, sourceLength, resultBytes, resultLength
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Decodes a PDF ASCIIHex stream.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DecodeAsciiHexBytes( _
    ByRef sourceBytes() As Byte, _
    ByVal sourceLength As Long, _
    ByRef resultBytes() As Byte, _
    ByRef resultLength As Long) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DecodeAsciiHexBytes"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngHighNibble As Long
    Dim lngValue As Long
    Dim lngByte As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngOutputIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngHighNibble = -1
    ReDim resultBytes(0 To sourceLength \ 2 + 1)

    For lngIndex = 0 To sourceLength - 1
        lngByte = CLng(sourceBytes(lngIndex))
        If IsPdfWhiteSpaceByte(lngByte) Then
            GoTo ContinueLoop
        End If
        If lngByte = 62 Then
            Exit For
        End If

        lngValue = HexByteValue(lngByte)
        If lngValue < 0 Then
            Err.Raise 1027, METHOD_NAME, "Invalid ASCIIHex character code: " & CStr(lngByte)
        End If

        If lngHighNibble < 0 Then
            lngHighNibble = lngValue
        Else
            resultBytes(lngOutputIndex) = CByte(lngHighNibble * 16 + lngValue)
            lngOutputIndex = lngOutputIndex + 1
            lngHighNibble = -1
        End If
ContinueLoop:
    Next lngIndex

    If lngHighNibble >= 0 Then
        resultBytes(lngOutputIndex) = CByte(lngHighNibble * 16)
        lngOutputIndex = lngOutputIndex + 1
    End If

    resultLength = lngOutputIndex
    If resultLength = 0 Then
        ReDim resultBytes(0 To 0)
    Else
        ReDim Preserve resultBytes(0 To resultLength - 1)
    End If
    DecodeAsciiHexBytes = True

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    resultLength = 0
    ReDim resultBytes(0 To 0)
    DecodeAsciiHexBytes = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "sourceLength;inputIndex", _
        sourceLength, _
        lngIndex

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    byteValue
' Returns:       Integer
' Description:   Converts one ASCII hexadecimal character to a nibble.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function HexByteValue(ByVal byteValue As Long) As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "HexByteValue"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Select Case byteValue
        Case 48 To 57
            HexByteValue = CInt(byteValue - 48)
        Case 65 To 70
            HexByteValue = CInt(byteValue - 55)
        Case 97 To 102
            HexByteValue = CInt(byteValue - 87)
        Case Else
            HexByteValue = -1
    End Select

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    HexByteValue = -1

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "byteValue", _
        byteValue

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    byteValue
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Returns True for PDF whitespace characters.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function IsPdfWhiteSpaceByte(ByVal byteValue As Long) As Boolean
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IsPdfWhiteSpaceByte"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Select Case byteValue
        Case 0, 9, 10, 12, 13, 32
            IsPdfWhiteSpaceByte = True
    End Select

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    IsPdfWhiteSpaceByte = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "byteValue", _
        byteValue

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    sourceBytes, sourceLength
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Converts a byte array prefix into a VBA string.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function ByteArrayText( _
    ByRef sourceBytes() As Byte, _
    ByVal sourceLength As Long) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ByteArrayText"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrTextBytes() As Byte
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If sourceLength <= 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    ReDim arrTextBytes(0 To sourceLength - 1)
    For lngIndex = 0 To sourceLength - 1
        arrTextBytes(lngIndex) = sourceBytes(lngIndex)
    Next lngIndex
    ByteArrayText = StrConv(arrTextBytes, vbUnicode)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    ByteArrayText = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "sourceLength", _
        sourceLength

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    dictionaryText As String, keyName As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns all indirect object references following a PDF dictionary key.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function RefList( _
    ByVal dictionaryText As String, _
    ByVal keyName As String) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "RefList"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrParts() As String
    Dim lngEndPosition As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngKeyPosition As Long
    Dim lngStartPosition As Long
    Dim strResult As String
    Dim strSegment As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngKeyPosition = InStr(1, dictionaryText, keyName, vbBinaryCompare)
    If lngKeyPosition = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    lngStartPosition = lngKeyPosition + Len(keyName)
    Do While lngStartPosition <= Len(dictionaryText)
        If Mid$(dictionaryText, lngStartPosition, 1) <> " " Then
            Exit Do
        End If
        lngStartPosition = lngStartPosition + 1
    Loop

    If Mid$(dictionaryText, lngStartPosition, 1) = "[" Then
        lngEndPosition = InStr(lngStartPosition, dictionaryText, "]", vbBinaryCompare)
        If lngEndPosition = 0 Then
            lngEndPosition = lngStartPosition + 1000
        End If
        strSegment = Mid$(dictionaryText, lngStartPosition + 1, lngEndPosition - lngStartPosition - 1)
    Else
        strSegment = Mid$(dictionaryText, lngStartPosition, 80)
        lngEndPosition = InStr(1, strSegment, "/", vbBinaryCompare)
        If lngEndPosition > 0 Then
            strSegment = Left$(strSegment, lngEndPosition - 1)
        End If
    End If

    Do While InStr(1, strSegment, "  ", vbBinaryCompare) > 0
        strSegment = Replace(strSegment, "  ", " ")
    Loop

    arrParts = Split(Trim$(strSegment), " ")
    For lngIndex = LBound(arrParts) To UBound(arrParts)
        If arrParts(lngIndex) = "R" And lngIndex >= 2 Then
            If Len(strResult) > 0 Then
                strResult = strResult & ","
            End If
            strResult = strResult & arrParts(lngIndex - 2)
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    RefList = strResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    RefList = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "keyName", _
        keyName

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    dictionaryText As String, keyName As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns the compact value following a PDF dictionary key.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DictValue( _
    ByVal dictionaryText As String, _
    ByVal keyName As String) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DictValue"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngDepth As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngKeyPosition As Long
    Dim strCharacter As String
    Dim strValue As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngKeyPosition = InStr(1, dictionaryText, keyName, vbBinaryCompare)
    If lngKeyPosition = 0 Then
        DictValue = "(absent)"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    lngIndex = lngKeyPosition + Len(keyName)
    Do While lngIndex <= Len(dictionaryText)
        If Mid$(dictionaryText, lngIndex, 1) <> " " Then
            Exit Do
        End If
        lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
    Loop

    Do While lngIndex <= Len(dictionaryText)
        strCharacter = Mid$(dictionaryText, lngIndex, 1)
        strValue = strValue & strCharacter
        If strCharacter = "[" Or strCharacter = "<" Then
            lngDepth = lngDepth + 1
        End If
        If strCharacter = "]" Or strCharacter = ">" Then
            lngDepth = lngDepth - 1
            If lngDepth <= 0 Then
                Exit Do
            End If
        End If
        If lngDepth = 0 And Len(strValue) > 1 Then
            If strCharacter = " " Or strCharacter = "/" Then
                Exit Do
            End If
        End If
        If Len(strValue) > 1000 Then
            Exit Do
        End If
        lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
    Loop

    DictValue = Trim$(strValue)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    DictValue = "(error)"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "keyName", _
        keyName

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    objectNo As Long, pageNo As Long, pdfDoc As tPdfDocument
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Parses one page content stream into normalized line and text records.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub ParsePage( _
    ByVal objectNo As Long, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ParsePage"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngObjectIndex As Long
    Dim strContent As String
    Dim strDictionary As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngObjectIndex = IndexOfObject(objectNo)
    If lngObjectIndex < 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "The page object was not found."
    End If

    strDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngObjectIndex))
    strContent = PageContent(strDictionary)

    pdfDoc.PageCount = pdfDoc.PageCount + 1
    If pdfDoc.PageCount > UBound(pdfDoc.Pages) Then
        ReDim Preserve pdfDoc.Pages(1 To UBound(pdfDoc.Pages) * 2)
    End If

    With pdfDoc.Pages(pdfDoc.PageCount)
        .ObjectNo = objectNo
        .MediaBox = DictValue(strDictionary, "/MediaBox")
        .CropBox = DictValue(strDictionary, "/CropBox")
        .RotateValue = DictValue(strDictionary, "/Rotate")
        .FontDictionary = DictValue(strDictionary, "/Font")
        .ResolvedFonts = ResolvePageFontSignature(strDictionary)
        .ContentFilters = PageContentFilterSignature(strDictionary)
        .ContentText = strContent
        .ContentLength = Len(strContent)
        .ImageStart = pdfDoc.ImageCount
        .LineStart = pdfDoc.LineCount
        .TextStart = pdfDoc.TextCount
    End With

    Call AnalysePageContent(strContent, pageNo, pdfDoc)

    pdfDoc.Pages(pdfDoc.PageCount).ImageCount = pdfDoc.ImageCount - pdfDoc.Pages(pdfDoc.PageCount).ImageStart
    pdfDoc.Pages(pdfDoc.PageCount).LineCount = pdfDoc.LineCount - pdfDoc.Pages(pdfDoc.PageCount).LineStart
    pdfDoc.Pages(pdfDoc.PageCount).TextCount = pdfDoc.TextCount - pdfDoc.Pages(pdfDoc.PageCount).TextStart

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "objectNo;pageNo", _
        objectNo, _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    content As String, pageNo As Long, pdfDoc As tPdfDocument
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Interprets relevant PDF graphics and text operators for one page.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AnalysePageContent( _
    ByVal content As String, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AnalysePageContent"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrTokens() As String
    Dim blnInArray As Boolean
    Dim dblFontSize As Double
    Dim dblLineX As Double
    Dim dblLineY As Double
    Dim dblX1 As Double
    Dim dblX2 As Double
    Dim dblY1 As Double
    Dim dblY2 As Double
    Dim h0 As String
    Dim h1 As String
    Dim h2 As String
    Dim h3 As String
    Dim h4 As String
    Dim h5 As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngTokenCount As Long
    Dim strArrayText As String
    Dim strFontName As String
    Dim strLastText As String
    Dim strToken As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Call ResetPageGraphicsState
    lngTokenCount = Tokenize(content, arrTokens)

    For lngIndex = 0 To lngTokenCount - 1
        strToken = arrTokens(lngIndex)

        Select Case strToken
            Case "q"
                Call PushGraphicsState

            Case "Q"
                Call PopGraphicsState

            Case "cm"
                Call ApplyConcatMatrix(Val(h5), Val(h4), Val(h3), Val(h2), Val(h1), Val(h0))

            Case "w"
                m_currentLineWidth = Val(h0)

            Case "G"
                m_currentStrokeColor = "G:" & NumTxt(Val(h0))

            Case "RG"
                m_currentStrokeColor = "RGB:" & NumTxt(Val(h2)) & "," & NumTxt(Val(h1)) & "," & NumTxt(Val(h0))

            Case "K"
                m_currentStrokeColor = "CMYK:" & NumTxt(Val(h3)) & "," & NumTxt(Val(h2)) & "," & NumTxt(Val(h1)) & "," & NumTxt(Val(h0))

            Case "m"
                Call TransformPoint(Val(h1), Val(h0), dblX1, dblY1)
                m_moveX = dblX1
                m_moveY = dblY1
                m_subPathStartX = dblX1
                m_subPathStartY = dblY1
                m_hasMovePoint = True

            Case "l"
                Call TransformPoint(Val(h1), Val(h0), dblX2, dblY2)
                If m_hasMovePoint Then
                    Call AppendPendingLine(m_moveX, m_moveY, dblX2, dblY2, "m/l")
                End If
                m_moveX = dblX2
                m_moveY = dblY2

            Case "h"
                If m_hasMovePoint Then
                    Call AppendPendingLine(m_moveX, m_moveY, m_subPathStartX, m_subPathStartY, "h")
                    m_moveX = m_subPathStartX
                    m_moveY = m_subPathStartY
                End If

            Case "re"
                Call AddRectanglePath(Val(h3), Val(h2), Val(h1), Val(h0))

            Case "S"
                Call FlushPendingPath(pageNo, "S", pdfDoc)

            Case "s"
                If m_hasMovePoint Then
                    Call AppendPendingLine(m_moveX, m_moveY, m_subPathStartX, m_subPathStartY, "h")
                End If
                Call FlushPendingPath(pageNo, "s", pdfDoc)

            Case "B", "B*", "b", "b*"
                Call FlushPendingPath(pageNo, strToken, pdfDoc)

            Case "f", "f*", "F", "n"
                Call ClearPendingPath

            Case "Do"
                Call AppendDocumentImage(pageNo, h0, m_ctmA, m_ctmB, m_ctmC, m_ctmD, m_ctmE, m_ctmF, pdfDoc)

            Case "BT"
                dblLineX = 0
                dblLineY = 0

            Case "Tf"
                dblFontSize = Val(h0)
                strFontName = h1

            Case "Tm"
                dblLineX = Val(h1)
                dblLineY = Val(h0)

            Case "Td", "TD"
                dblLineX = dblLineX + Val(h1)
                dblLineY = dblLineY + Val(h0)

            Case "T*"
                dblLineY = dblLineY - dblFontSize * 1.2

            Case "Tj", "'"
                Call AppendDocumentText(pageNo, dblLineX, dblLineY, dblFontSize, strFontName, strLastText, pdfDoc)

            Case "TJ"
                Call AppendDocumentText(pageNo, dblLineX, dblLineY, dblFontSize, strFontName, strArrayText, pdfDoc)
                strArrayText = vbNullString

            Case "["
                blnInArray = True
                strArrayText = vbNullString

            Case "]"
                blnInArray = False
        End Select

        If Len(strToken) > 0 Then
            If Left$(strToken, 1) = "(" Then
                strLastText = Mid$(strToken, 2)
                If blnInArray Then
                    strArrayText = strArrayText & strLastText
                End If
            End If
        End If

        h5 = h4
        h4 = h3
        h3 = h2
        h2 = h1
        h1 = h0
        h0 = strToken
    Next lngIndex

    Call ClearPendingPath

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;contentLength", _
        pageNo, _
        Len(content)

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Resets the graphics state before parsing one page.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub ResetPageGraphicsState()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ResetPageGraphicsState"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    m_pendingCount = 0
    ReDim m_pendingX1(0 To 255)
    ReDim m_pendingY1(0 To 255)
    ReDim m_pendingX2(0 To 255)
    ReDim m_pendingY2(0 To 255)
    ReDim m_pendingSource(0 To 255)
    m_currentLineWidth = 1
    m_currentStrokeColor = "G:0"
    m_ctmA = 1
    m_ctmB = 0
    m_ctmC = 0
    m_ctmD = 1
    m_ctmE = 0
    m_ctmF = 0
    m_stackCount = 0
    ReDim m_stackA(0 To 63)
    ReDim m_stackB(0 To 63)
    ReDim m_stackC(0 To 63)
    ReDim m_stackD(0 To 63)
    ReDim m_stackE(0 To 63)
    ReDim m_stackF(0 To 63)
    ReDim m_stackLineWidth(0 To 63)
    ReDim m_stackStrokeColor(0 To 63)
    m_hasMovePoint = False

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Saves the current graphics state on the q/Q stack.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub PushGraphicsState()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PushGraphicsState"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If m_stackCount > UBound(m_stackA) Then
        Err.Raise ERR_BASE + 3, METHOD_NAME, "The PDF graphics state stack is too deep."
    End If

    m_stackA(m_stackCount) = m_ctmA
    m_stackB(m_stackCount) = m_ctmB
    m_stackC(m_stackCount) = m_ctmC
    m_stackD(m_stackCount) = m_ctmD
    m_stackE(m_stackCount) = m_ctmE
    m_stackF(m_stackCount) = m_ctmF
    m_stackLineWidth(m_stackCount) = m_currentLineWidth
    m_stackStrokeColor(m_stackCount) = m_currentStrokeColor
    m_stackCount = m_stackCount + 1

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "stackCount", _
        m_stackCount

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Restores the latest graphics state from the q/Q stack.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub PopGraphicsState()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PopGraphicsState"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If m_stackCount <= 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    m_stackCount = m_stackCount - 1
    m_ctmA = m_stackA(m_stackCount)
    m_ctmB = m_stackB(m_stackCount)
