    m_ctmC = m_stackC(m_stackCount)
    m_ctmD = m_stackD(m_stackCount)
    m_ctmE = m_stackE(m_stackCount)
    m_ctmF = m_stackF(m_stackCount)
    m_currentLineWidth = m_stackLineWidth(m_stackCount)
    m_currentStrokeColor = m_stackStrokeColor(m_stackCount)

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "stackCount", _
        m_stackCount

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    a As Double, b As Double, c As Double, d As Double, e As Double, f As Double
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Concatenates one PDF transformation matrix with the current matrix.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub ApplyConcatMatrix( _
    ByVal a As Double, _
    ByVal b As Double, _
    ByVal c As Double, _
    ByVal d As Double, _
    ByVal e As Double, _
    ByVal f As Double)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ApplyConcatMatrix"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dblNewA As Double
    Dim dblNewB As Double
    Dim dblNewC As Double
    Dim dblNewD As Double
    Dim dblNewE As Double
    Dim dblNewF As Double

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    dblNewA = a * m_ctmA + b * m_ctmC
    dblNewB = a * m_ctmB + b * m_ctmD
    dblNewC = c * m_ctmA + d * m_ctmC
    dblNewD = c * m_ctmB + d * m_ctmD
    dblNewE = e * m_ctmA + f * m_ctmC + m_ctmE
    dblNewF = e * m_ctmB + f * m_ctmD + m_ctmF

    m_ctmA = dblNewA
    m_ctmB = dblNewB
    m_ctmC = dblNewC
    m_ctmD = dblNewD
    m_ctmE = dblNewE
    m_ctmF = dblNewF

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    inputX As Double, inputY As Double, outputX As Double, outputY As Double
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Applies the current PDF transformation matrix to one point.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub TransformPoint( _
    ByVal inputX As Double, _
    ByVal inputY As Double, _
    ByRef outputX As Double, _
    ByRef outputY As Double)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "TransformPoint"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    outputX = inputX * m_ctmA + inputY * m_ctmC + m_ctmE
    outputY = inputX * m_ctmB + inputY * m_ctmD + m_ctmF

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "inputX;inputY", _
        inputX, _
        inputY

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    x As Double, y As Double, width As Double, height As Double
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Adds the four transformed edges of one rectangle to the pending path.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AddRectanglePath( _
    ByVal x As Double, _
    ByVal y As Double, _
    ByVal width As Double, _
    ByVal height As Double)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AddRectanglePath"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dblX1 As Double
    Dim dblX2 As Double
    Dim dblX3 As Double
    Dim dblX4 As Double
    Dim dblY1 As Double
    Dim dblY2 As Double
    Dim dblY3 As Double
    Dim dblY4 As Double

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Call TransformPoint(x, y, dblX1, dblY1)
    Call TransformPoint(x + width, y, dblX2, dblY2)
    Call TransformPoint(x + width, y + height, dblX3, dblY3)
    Call TransformPoint(x, y + height, dblX4, dblY4)

    Call AppendPendingLine(dblX1, dblY1, dblX2, dblY2, "re")
    Call AppendPendingLine(dblX2, dblY2, dblX3, dblY3, "re")
    Call AppendPendingLine(dblX3, dblY3, dblX4, dblY4, "re")
    Call AppendPendingLine(dblX4, dblY4, dblX1, dblY1, "re")

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "x;y;width;height", _
        x, _
        y, _
        width, _
        height

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    x1 As Double, y1 As Double, x2 As Double, y2 As Double, sourceOperator As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Adds one line segment to the current unpainted PDF path.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AppendPendingLine( _
    ByVal x1 As Double, _
    ByVal y1 As Double, _
    ByVal x2 As Double, _
    ByVal y2 As Double, _
    ByVal sourceOperator As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AppendPendingLine"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If m_pendingCount > UBound(m_pendingX1) Then
        ReDim Preserve m_pendingX1(0 To m_pendingCount * 2)
        ReDim Preserve m_pendingY1(0 To m_pendingCount * 2)
        ReDim Preserve m_pendingX2(0 To m_pendingCount * 2)
        ReDim Preserve m_pendingY2(0 To m_pendingCount * 2)
        ReDim Preserve m_pendingSource(0 To m_pendingCount * 2)
    End If

    m_pendingX1(m_pendingCount) = x1
    m_pendingY1(m_pendingCount) = y1
    m_pendingX2(m_pendingCount) = x2
    m_pendingY2(m_pendingCount) = y2
    m_pendingSource(m_pendingCount) = sourceOperator
    m_pendingCount = m_pendingCount + 1

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "sourceOperator", _
        sourceOperator

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pageNo As Long, paintOperator As String, pdfDoc As tPdfDocument
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Commits the current path as stroked vector lines.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub FlushPendingPath( _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal paintOperator As String, _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FlushPendingPath"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To m_pendingCount - 1
        Call AppendDocumentLine( _
            pageNo, _
            m_pendingX1(lngIndex), _
            m_pendingY1(lngIndex), _
            m_pendingX2(lngIndex), _
            m_pendingY2(lngIndex), _
            m_currentLineWidth, _
            paintOperator, _
            m_pendingSource(lngIndex), _
            m_currentStrokeColor, _
            pdfDoc)
    Next lngIndex

    Call ClearPendingPath

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;paintOperator;pendingCount", _
        pageNo, _
        paintOperator, _
        m_pendingCount

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Clears the current unpainted PDF path.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub ClearPendingPath()
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ClearPendingPath"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    m_pendingCount = 0
    m_hasMovePoint = False

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pageNo As Long, x1 As Double, y1 As Double, x2 As Double, y2 As Double, lineWidth As Double, paintOperator As String, sourceOperator As String, strokeColor As String, pdfDoc As tPdfDocument
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Adds one normalized stroked vector line to a parsed PDF document.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AppendDocumentLine( _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal x1 As Double, _
    ByVal y1 As Double, _
    ByVal x2 As Double, _
    ByVal y2 As Double, _
    ByVal lineWidth As Double, _
    ByVal paintOperator As String, _
    ByVal sourceOperator As String, _
    ByVal strokeColor As String, _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AppendDocumentLine"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Abs(x1 - x2) > COORD_TOLERANCE And Abs(y1 - y2) > COORD_TOLERANCE Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    If pdfDoc.LineCount > UBound(pdfDoc.Lines) Then
        ReDim Preserve pdfDoc.Lines(0 To pdfDoc.LineCount * 2)
    End If

    With pdfDoc.Lines(pdfDoc.LineCount)
        .PageNo = pageNo
        .X1 = x1
        .Y1 = y1
        .X2 = x2
        .Y2 = y2
        .LineWidth = lineWidth
        .PaintOperator = paintOperator
        .SourceOperator = sourceOperator
        .StrokeColor = strokeColor
    End With
    pdfDoc.LineCount = pdfDoc.LineCount + 1

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;sourceOperator;paintOperator", _
        pageNo, _
        sourceOperator, _
        paintOperator

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pageNo As Long, resourceName As String, matrixA As Double, matrixB As Double, matrixC As Double, matrixD As Double, matrixE As Double, matrixF As Double, pdfDoc As tPdfDocument
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Stores one PDF XObject placement matrix for page-level image and form-placement auditing.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AppendDocumentImage( _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal resourceName As String, _
    ByVal matrixA As Double, _
    ByVal matrixB As Double, _
    ByVal matrixC As Double, _
    ByVal matrixD As Double, _
    ByVal matrixE As Double, _
    ByVal matrixF As Double, _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AppendDocumentImage"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If pdfDoc.ImageCount > UBound(pdfDoc.Images) Then
        ReDim Preserve pdfDoc.Images(0 To pdfDoc.ImageCount * 2)
    End If

    With pdfDoc.Images(pdfDoc.ImageCount)
        .PageNo = pageNo
        .ResourceName = resourceName
        .A = matrixA
        .B = matrixB
        .C = matrixC
        .D = matrixD
        .E = matrixE
        .F = matrixF
    End With
    pdfDoc.ImageCount = pdfDoc.ImageCount + 1

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;resourceName", _
        pageNo, _
        resourceName

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pageNo As Long, x As Double, y As Double, fontSize As Double, fontName As String, textValue As String, pdfDoc As tPdfDocument
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Adds one text run with an absolute page coordinate.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub AppendDocumentText( _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal x As Double, _
    ByVal y As Double, _
    ByVal fontSize As Double, _
    ByVal fontName As String, _
    ByVal textValue As String, _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "AppendDocumentText"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dblAbsoluteX As Double
    Dim dblAbsoluteY As Double

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Len(Trim$(textValue)) = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    Call TransformPoint(x, y, dblAbsoluteX, dblAbsoluteY)
    If pdfDoc.TextCount > UBound(pdfDoc.Texts) Then
        ReDim Preserve pdfDoc.Texts(0 To pdfDoc.TextCount * 2)
    End If

    With pdfDoc.Texts(pdfDoc.TextCount)
        .PageNo = pageNo
        .X = dblAbsoluteX
        .Y = dblAbsoluteY
        .FontSize = fontSize
        .FontName = fontName
        .TextValue = textValue
    End With
    pdfDoc.TextCount = pdfDoc.TextCount + 1

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;fontName;fontSize", _
        pageNo, _
        fontName, _
        fontSize

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    content As String, tokens As String array
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Tokenizes PDF page content while preserving literal strings.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function Tokenize( _
    ByVal content As String, _
    ByRef tokens() As String) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "Tokenize"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngDepth As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngLength As Long
    Dim lngNext As Long
    Dim strBuffer As String
    Dim strCharacter As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngLength = Len(content)
    ReDim tokens(0 To 4095)
    lngIndex = 1

    Do While lngIndex <= lngLength
        strCharacter = Mid$(content, lngIndex, 1)

        If strCharacter = " " Or strCharacter = vbCr Or strCharacter = vbLf Or strCharacter = vbTab Or strCharacter = Chr$(0) Then
            lngIndex = lngIndex + 1

        ElseIf strCharacter = "(" Then
            lngDepth = 1
            lngNext = lngIndex + 1
            strBuffer = vbNullString
            Do While lngNext <= lngLength And lngDepth > 0
                strCharacter = Mid$(content, lngNext, 1)
                If strCharacter = "\" Then
                    strBuffer = strBuffer & Mid$(content, lngNext + 1, 1)
                    lngNext = lngNext + 2
                Else
                    If strCharacter = "(" Then
                        lngDepth = lngDepth + 1
                    ElseIf strCharacter = ")" Then
                        lngDepth = lngDepth - 1
                        If lngDepth = 0 Then
                            Exit Do
                        End If
                    End If
                    strBuffer = strBuffer & strCharacter
                    lngNext = lngNext + 1
                End If
            Loop
            Call PushToken(tokens, Tokenize, "(" & strBuffer)
            lngIndex = lngNext + 1

        ElseIf strCharacter = "/" Then
            lngNext = lngIndex + 1
            Do While lngNext <= lngLength
                If IsDelimiter(Mid$(content, lngNext, 1)) Then
                    Exit Do
                End If
                lngNext = lngNext + 1
            Loop
            Call PushToken(tokens, Tokenize, Mid$(content, lngIndex, lngNext - lngIndex))
            lngIndex = lngNext

        ElseIf strCharacter = "[" Or strCharacter = "]" Then
            Call PushToken(tokens, Tokenize, strCharacter)
            lngIndex = lngIndex + 1

        ElseIf strCharacter = "<" Or strCharacter = ">" Or strCharacter = "{" Or strCharacter = "}" Then
            lngIndex = lngIndex + 1

        ElseIf strCharacter = "%" Then
            lngNext = InStr(lngIndex, content, vbLf, vbBinaryCompare)
            If lngNext = 0 Then
                Exit Do
            End If
            lngIndex = lngNext + 1

        Else
            lngNext = lngIndex
            Do While lngNext <= lngLength
                If IsDelimiter(Mid$(content, lngNext, 1)) Then
                    Exit Do
                End If
                lngNext = lngNext + 1
            Loop
            If lngNext = lngIndex Then
                lngNext = lngIndex + 1
            End If
            Call PushToken(tokens, Tokenize, Mid$(content, lngIndex, lngNext - lngIndex))
            lngIndex = lngNext
        End If
    Loop

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Tokenize = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "contentLength", _
        Len(content)

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    tokens As String array, tokenCount As Long, tokenValue As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Appends one token to a dynamic token array.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub PushToken( _
    ByRef tokens() As String, _
    ByRef tokenCount As Long, _
    ByVal tokenValue As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PushToken"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If tokenCount > UBound(tokens) Then
        ReDim Preserve tokens(0 To tokenCount * 2)
    End If
    tokens(tokenCount) = tokenValue
    tokenCount = tokenCount + 1

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "tokenCount", _
        tokenCount

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    characterValue As String
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Returns whether a character terminates a PDF token.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function IsDelimiter(ByVal characterValue As String) As Boolean
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IsDelimiter"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Select Case characterValue
        Case " ", vbCr, vbLf, vbTab, "(", ")", "<", ">", "[", "]", "{", "}", "/", "%"
            IsDelimiter = True
    End Select

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    IsDelimiter = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, outputPath As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes a detailed forensic report for one parsed PDF document.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteForensicReport( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal outputPath As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteForensicReport"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngFileNumber As Long
    Dim lngPage As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngFileNumber = OpenNew(outputPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "PDF FORENSIC GEOMETRY REPORT V2")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "SOURCE|" & pdfDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "DOCUMENT|version=" & pdfDoc.PdfVersion & "|bytes=" & CStr(pdfDoc.FileSize) & "|objects=" & CStr(pdfDoc.ObjectCount) & "|xref=" & pdfDoc.XrefKind & "|pages=" & CStr(pdfDoc.PageCount))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "TARGET_X|" & NumTxt(TARGET_X_FIRST) & "|" & NumTxt(TARGET_X_SECOND))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "NOTE|CROSSING_TEXT uses an estimated Times font bounding width and is diagnostic, not a PDF rendering measurement.")

    For lngPage = 1 To pdfDoc.PageCount
        Call WritePageForensic(pdfDoc, lngPage, lngFileNumber)
    Next lngPage

ExitSub:
    If lngFileNumber > 0 Then
        Close #lngFileNumber
    End If
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "outputPath", _
        outputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, fileNumber As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes one page section of the forensic report.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WritePageForensic( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WritePageForensic"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE|" & CStr(pageNo) & "|object=" & CStr(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).ObjectNo))
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DICT|MediaBox=" & pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox & "|CropBox=" & pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).CropBox & "|Rotate=" & pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue & "|Fonts=" & pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).FontDictionary)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "COUNTS|contentChars=" & CStr(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentLength) & "|strokedLines=" & CStr(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).LineCount) & "|textRuns=" & CStr(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount))
    Call WriteDuplicateSection(pdfDoc, pageNo, fileNumber)
    Call WriteTargetSeparatorSection(pdfDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_FIRST, fileNumber)
    Call WriteTargetSeparatorSection(pdfDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_SECOND, fileNumber)
    Call WriteCrossingSection(pdfDoc, pageNo, fileNumber)

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, fileNumber As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes duplicated geometric line groups for one page.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteDuplicateSection( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteDuplicateSection"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictCounts As Object
    Dim lngDetailCount As Long
    Dim lngDuplicateGroups As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictCounts = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            strKey = GeometryKey(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex))
            If dictCounts.Exists(strKey) Then
                dictCounts(strKey) = CLng(dictCounts(strKey)) + 1
            Else
                dictCounts.Add strKey, 1
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    For Each varKey In dictCounts.Keys
        If CLng(dictCounts(varKey)) > 1 Then
            lngDuplicateGroups = lngDuplicateGroups + 1
        End If
    Next varKey

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DUPLICATE_STROKES|groups=" & CStr(lngDuplicateGroups))
    For Each varKey In dictCounts.Keys
        If CLng(dictCounts(varKey)) > 1 Then
            lngDetailCount = lngDetailCount + 1
            If lngDetailCount <= MAX_DETAIL_LINES Then
                Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DUPLICATE|count=" & CStr(dictCounts(varKey)) & "|" & CStr(varKey))
            End If
        End If
    Next varKey

ExitSub:
    Set dictCounts = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, targetX As Double, fileNumber As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes all vertical strokes and text intersections at one target X coordinate.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteTargetSeparatorSection( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteTargetSeparatorSection"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictGeometry As Object
    Dim lngCrossingCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngOccurrenceCount As Long
    Dim strKey As String
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictGeometry = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If IsVerticalAtX(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex), targetX) Then
                lngOccurrenceCount = lngOccurrenceCount + 1
                strKey = GeometryKey(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & "|width=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).LineWidth) & "|paint=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PaintOperator & "|source=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).SourceOperator & "|color=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).StrokeColor
                If dictGeometry.Exists(strKey) Then
                    dictGeometry(strKey) = CLng(dictGeometry(strKey)) + 1
                Else
                    dictGeometry.Add strKey, 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    lngCrossingCount = CountTargetTextCrossings(pdfDoc, pageNo, targetX, fileNumber, True)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TARGET_SEPARATOR|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & "|occurrences=" & CStr(lngOccurrenceCount) & "|uniqueStyled=" & CStr(dictGeometry.Count) & "|estimatedTextCrossings=" & CStr(lngCrossingCount))

    For Each varKey In dictGeometry.Keys
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TARGET_RULE|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & "|count=" & CStr(dictGeometry(varKey)) & "|" & CStr(varKey))
    Next varKey

ExitSub:
    Set dictGeometry = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, fileNumber As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes all estimated intersections between vertical rules and text runs.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteCrossingSection( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteCrossingSection"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictCrossings As Object
    Dim dblLineX As Double
    Dim dblTextWidth As Double
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngTextIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictCrossings = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Abs(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X1 - pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
                dblLineX = (pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X1 + pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X2) / 2
                For lngTextIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
                    If pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
                        dblTextWidth = EstimateTextWidth(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).TextValue, pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontName, pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontSize)
                        If dblLineX > pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).X + 0.5 And dblLineX < pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).X + dblTextWidth - 0.5 Then
                            If LineOverlapsTextVertically(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex), pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex)) Then
                                strKey = "x=" & NumTxt(dblLineX) & "|lineY=" & NumTxt(MinDouble(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1, pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2)) & ".." & NumTxt(MaxDouble(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1, pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2)) & "|textX=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).X) & "|textY=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).Y) & "|font=" & pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontName & "@" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontSize) & "|text=" & SanitizeReportText(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).TextValue)
                                If Not dictCrossings.Exists(strKey) Then
                                    dictCrossings.Add strKey, True
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                Next lngTextIndex
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "ESTIMATED_TEXT_CROSSINGS|count=" & CStr(dictCrossings.Count))
    For Each varKey In dictCrossings.Keys
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "CROSSING_TEXT|" & CStr(varKey))
    Next varKey

ExitSub:
    Set dictCrossings = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, outputPath As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes a focused old-versus-new PDF vector comparison report.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteComparisonReport( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal outputPath As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteComparisonReport"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngFileNumber As Long
    Dim lngCommonPages As Long
    Dim lngPage As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngFileNumber = OpenNew(outputPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "PDF VECTOR COMPARISON V2 NORMALIZED TARGET ORDER")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "OLD|" & oldDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "NEW|" & newDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "OLD_DOCUMENT|version=" & oldDoc.PdfVersion & "|bytes=" & CStr(oldDoc.FileSize) & "|objects=" & CStr(oldDoc.ObjectCount) & "|xref=" & oldDoc.XrefKind & "|pages=" & CStr(oldDoc.PageCount))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "NEW_DOCUMENT|version=" & newDoc.PdfVersion & "|bytes=" & CStr(newDoc.FileSize) & "|objects=" & CStr(newDoc.ObjectCount) & "|xref=" & newDoc.XrefKind & "|pages=" & CStr(newDoc.PageCount))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "IMPORTANT|Use matching reports with the same transactions for meaningful text and page-level comparison.")

    lngCommonPages = oldDoc.PageCount
    If newDoc.PageCount < lngCommonPages Then
        lngCommonPages = newDoc.PageCount
    End If

    For lngPage = 1 To lngCommonPages
        Call ComparePage(oldDoc, newDoc, lngPage, lngFileNumber)
    Next lngPage

    If oldDoc.PageCount <> newDoc.PageCount Then
        Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "PAGE_COUNT_DIFFERENCE|old=" & CStr(oldDoc.PageCount) & "|new=" & CStr(newDoc.PageCount))
    End If

ExitSub:
    If lngFileNumber > 0 Then
        Close #lngFileNumber
    End If
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "outputPath", _
        outputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, fileNumber As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Compares dictionary, line multiplicity, style, and target separators for one page.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub ComparePage( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ComparePage"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim newGeometry As Object
    Dim newStyles As Object
    Dim oldGeometry As Object
    Dim oldStyles As Object

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE_COMPARE|" & CStr(pageNo))
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "OLD_DICT|MediaBox=" & oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox & "|CropBox=" & oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).CropBox & "|Rotate=" & oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue & "|Fonts=" & oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).FontDictionary)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "NEW_DICT|MediaBox=" & newDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox & "|CropBox=" & newDoc.Pages(pageNo).CropBox & "|Rotate=" & newDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue & "|Fonts=" & newDoc.Pages(pageNo).FontDictionary)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "COUNTS|oldLines=" & CStr(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).LineCount) & "|newLines=" & CStr(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).LineCount) & "|oldText=" & CStr(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount) & "|newText=" & CStr(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount))

    Set oldGeometry = BuildGeometryDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo)
    Set newGeometry = BuildGeometryDictionary(newDoc, pageNo)
    Set oldStyles = BuildStyleDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo)
    Set newStyles = BuildStyleDictionary(newDoc, pageNo)

    Call WriteGeometryDifferences(oldGeometry, newGeometry, oldStyles, newStyles, fileNumber)
    Call WriteTargetComparison(oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_FIRST, fileNumber)
    Call WriteTargetComparison(oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_SECOND, fileNumber)

ExitSub:
    Set oldGeometry = Nothing
    Set newGeometry = Nothing
    Set oldStyles = Nothing
    Set newStyles = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long
' Returns:       Object
' Description:   Builds geometry multiplicity counts for all stroked page lines.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildGeometryDictionary( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As Object

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildGeometryDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictResult As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictResult = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            strKey = GeometryKey(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex))
            If dictResult.Exists(strKey) Then
                dictResult(strKey) = CLng(dictResult(strKey)) + 1
            Else
                dictResult.Add strKey, 1
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Set BuildGeometryDictionary = dictResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Set BuildGeometryDictionary = Nothing

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long
' Returns:       Object
' Description:   Builds style signatures grouped by geometric line coordinates.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildStyleDictionary( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As Object

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildStyleDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictResult As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strGeometryKey As String
    Dim strStyle As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictResult = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            strGeometryKey = GeometryKey(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex))
            strStyle = "width=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).LineWidth) & ",paint=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PaintOperator & ",source=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).SourceOperator & ",color=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).StrokeColor
            If dictResult.Exists(strGeometryKey) Then
                If InStr(1, "|" & dictResult(strGeometryKey) & "|", "|" & strStyle & "|", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then
                    dictResult(strGeometryKey) = dictResult(strGeometryKey) & "|" & strStyle
                End If
            Else
                dictResult.Add strGeometryKey, strStyle
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Set BuildStyleDictionary = dictResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Set BuildStyleDictionary = Nothing

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    oldGeometry As Object, newGeometry As Object, oldStyles As Object, newStyles As Object, fileNumber As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes missing, added, multiplicity, and style differences between line sets.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteGeometryDifferences( _
    ByVal oldGeometry As Object, _
    ByVal newGeometry As Object, _
    ByVal oldStyles As Object, _
    ByVal newStyles As Object, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteGeometryDifferences"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngAdded As Long
    Dim lngChanged As Long
    Dim lngDetailCount As Long
    Dim lngMissing As Long
    Dim lngStyleChanged As Long
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For Each varKey In oldGeometry.Keys
        If Not newGeometry.Exists(varKey) Then
            lngMissing = lngMissing + 1
            If lngDetailCount < MAX_DETAIL_LINES Then
                lngDetailCount = lngDetailCount + 1
                Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "LINE_ONLY_OLD|count=" & CStr(oldGeometry(varKey)) & "|" & CStr(varKey))
            End If
        ElseIf CLng(oldGeometry(varKey)) <> CLng(newGeometry(varKey)) Then
            lngChanged = lngChanged + 1
            If lngDetailCount < MAX_DETAIL_LINES Then
                lngDetailCount = lngDetailCount + 1
                Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "LINE_MULTIPLICITY_CHANGED|old=" & CStr(oldGeometry(varKey)) & "|new=" & CStr(newGeometry(varKey)) & "|" & CStr(varKey))
            End If
        End If

        If newStyles.Exists(varKey) Then
            If CStr(oldStyles(varKey)) <> CStr(newStyles(varKey)) Then
                lngStyleChanged = lngStyleChanged + 1
                If lngDetailCount < MAX_DETAIL_LINES Then
                    lngDetailCount = lngDetailCount + 1
                    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "LINE_STYLE_CHANGED|old=" & CStr(oldStyles(varKey)) & "|new=" & CStr(newStyles(varKey)) & "|" & CStr(varKey))
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next varKey

    For Each varKey In newGeometry.Keys
        If Not oldGeometry.Exists(varKey) Then
            lngAdded = lngAdded + 1
            If lngDetailCount < MAX_DETAIL_LINES Then
                lngDetailCount = lngDetailCount + 1
                Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "LINE_ONLY_NEW|count=" & CStr(newGeometry(varKey)) & "|" & CStr(varKey))
            End If
        End If
    Next varKey

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "VECTOR_DIFF_SUMMARY|onlyOld=" & CStr(lngMissing) & "|onlyNew=" & CStr(lngAdded) & "|multiplicityChanged=" & CStr(lngChanged) & "|styleChanged=" & CStr(lngStyleChanged))

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, targetX As Double, fileNumber As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Compares stroke multiplicity, style, vertical span, and text crossings at one X coordinate.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteTargetComparison( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteTargetComparison"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngNewCrossings As Long
    Dim lngNewOccurrences As Long
    Dim lngNewUnique As Long
    Dim lngOldCrossings As Long
    Dim lngOldOccurrences As Long
    Dim lngOldUnique As Long
    Dim strFinding As String
    Dim strNewDetails As String
    Dim strOldDetails As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Call TargetLineStats(oldDoc, pageNo, targetX, lngOldOccurrences, lngOldUnique, lngOldCrossings, strOldDetails)
    Call TargetLineStats(newDoc, pageNo, targetX, lngNewOccurrences, lngNewUnique, lngNewCrossings, strNewDetails)

    If lngNewOccurrences > lngOldOccurrences Then
        strFinding = strFinding & "NEW_HAS_MORE_STROKES;"
    End If
    If lngNewUnique > lngOldUnique Then
        strFinding = strFinding & "NEW_HAS_MORE_DISTINCT_SPANS_OR_STYLES;"
    End If
    If lngNewCrossings > lngOldCrossings Then
        strFinding = strFinding & "NEW_INTERSECTS_MORE_TEXT;"
    End If
    If strOldDetails <> strNewDetails Then
        strFinding = strFinding & "SPAN_OR_STYLE_DIFFERS;"
    End If
    If Len(strFinding) = 0 Then
        strFinding = "NO_TARGET_VECTOR_DIFFERENCE_DETECTED"
    End If

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TARGET_COMPARE|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & "|oldOccurrences=" & CStr(lngOldOccurrences) & "|newOccurrences=" & CStr(lngNewOccurrences) & "|oldUnique=" & CStr(lngOldUnique) & "|newUnique=" & CStr(lngNewUnique) & "|oldCrossings=" & CStr(lngOldCrossings) & "|newCrossings=" & CStr(lngNewCrossings) & "|finding=" & strFinding)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TARGET_OLD_DETAIL|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & "|" & strOldDetails)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TARGET_NEW_DETAIL|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & "|" & strNewDetails)

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, targetX As Double, occurrenceCount As Long, uniqueCount As Long, crossingCount As Long, details As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Summarizes all stroked vertical rules at one target coordinate.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub TargetLineStats( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double, _
    ByRef occurrenceCount As Long, _
    ByRef uniqueCount As Long, _
    ByRef crossingCount As Long, _
    ByRef details As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "TargetLineStats"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrSortedKeys As Variant
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictDetails As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngSortedIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictDetails = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    occurrenceCount = 0
    uniqueCount = 0
    crossingCount = 0
    details = vbNullString

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If IsVerticalAtX(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex), targetX) Then
                occurrenceCount = occurrenceCount + 1
                strKey = "y=" & NumTxt(MinDouble(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1, pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2)) & ".." & NumTxt(MaxDouble(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1, pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2)) & ",width=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).LineWidth) & ",paint=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PaintOperator & ",source=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).SourceOperator & ",color=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).StrokeColor
                Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictDetails, strKey)
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    uniqueCount = dictDetails.Count
    crossingCount = CountTargetTextCrossings(pdfDoc, pageNo, targetX, 0, False)
    If dictDetails.Count > 0 Then
        arrSortedKeys = SortedDictionaryKeys(dictDetails)
        For lngSortedIndex = LBound(arrSortedKeys) To UBound(arrSortedKeys)
            strKey = CStr(arrSortedKeys(lngSortedIndex))
            If Len(details) > 0 Then
                details = details & "|"
            End If
            details = details & "count=" & CStr(dictDetails(strKey)) & "," & strKey
        Next lngSortedIndex
    End If
    If Len(details) = 0 Then
        details = "none"
    End If

ExitSub:
    Set dictDetails = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, targetX As Double, fileNumber As Long, writeDetails As Boolean
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Counts estimated text bounding boxes intersected by a target vertical rule.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function CountTargetTextCrossings( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal writeDetails As Boolean) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CountTargetTextCrossings"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictCrossings As Object
    Dim dblTextWidth As Double
    Dim lngLineIndex As Long
    Dim lngTextIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictCrossings = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngLineIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngLineIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If IsVerticalAtX(pdfDoc.Lines(lngLineIndex), targetX) Then
                For lngTextIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
                    If pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
                        dblTextWidth = EstimateTextWidth(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).TextValue, pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontName, pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontSize)
                        If targetX > pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).X + 0.5 And targetX < pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).X + dblTextWidth - 0.5 Then
                            If LineOverlapsTextVertically(pdfDoc.Lines(lngLineIndex), pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex)) Then
                                strKey = "textX=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).X) & "|textY=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).Y) & "|font=" & pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontName & "@" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).FontSize) & "|text=" & SanitizeReportText(pdfDoc.Texts(lngTextIndex).TextValue)
                                If Not dictCrossings.Exists(strKey) Then
                                    dictCrossings.Add strKey, True
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                Next lngTextIndex
            End If
        End If
    Next lngLineIndex

    CountTargetTextCrossings = dictCrossings.Count
    If writeDetails And fileNumber > 0 Then
        For Each varKey In dictCrossings.Keys
            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TARGET_CROSSING_TEXT|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & "|" & CStr(varKey))
        Next varKey
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Set dictCrossings = Nothing
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    CountTargetTextCrossings = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    lineInfo As tPdfLine, targetX As Double
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Returns whether a line is vertical at the requested X coordinate.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function IsVerticalAtX( _
    ByRef lineInfo As tPdfLine, _
    ByVal targetX As Double) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IsVerticalAtX"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dblLineX As Double

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Abs(lineInfo.X1 - lineInfo.X2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
        dblLineX = (lineInfo.X1 + lineInfo.X2) / 2
        IsVerticalAtX = Abs(dblLineX - targetX) <= TARGET_TOLERANCE
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    IsVerticalAtX = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "targetX", _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    lineInfo As tPdfLine, textInfo As tPdfText
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Returns whether a line vertically overlaps an estimated text box.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function LineOverlapsTextVertically( _
    ByRef lineInfo As tPdfLine, _
    ByRef textInfo As tPdfText) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "LineOverlapsTextVertically"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dblLineBottom As Double
    Dim dblLineTop As Double
    Dim dblTextBottom As Double
    Dim dblTextTop As Double

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    dblLineBottom = MinDouble(lineInfo.Y1, lineInfo.Y2)
    dblLineTop = MaxDouble(lineInfo.Y1, lineInfo.Y2)
    dblTextBottom = textInfo.Y - textInfo.FontSize * 0.25
    dblTextTop = textInfo.Y + textInfo.FontSize * 0.85
    LineOverlapsTextVertically = dblLineTop >= dblTextBottom And dblLineBottom <= dblTextTop

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    LineOverlapsTextVertically = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    textValue As String, fontName As String, fontSize As Double
' Returns:       Double
' Description:   Estimates a Times-family text run width for separator diagnostics.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function EstimateTextWidth( _
    ByVal textValue As String, _
    ByVal fontName As String, _
    ByVal fontSize As Double) As Double

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "EstimateTextWidth"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dblFactor As Double
    Dim dblUnits As Double
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strCharacter As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 1 To Len(textValue)
        strCharacter = Mid$(textValue, lngIndex, 1)
        Select Case strCharacter
            Case " "
                dblUnits = dblUnits + 0.25
            Case "i", "l", "I", ".", ",", ":", ";", "!", "'", "|"
                dblUnits = dblUnits + 0.25
            Case "M", "W", "m", "w", "@", "%"
                dblUnits = dblUnits + 0.85
            Case "0" To "9"
                dblUnits = dblUnits + 0.5
            Case "A" To "Z"
                dblUnits = dblUnits + 0.65
            Case Else
                dblUnits = dblUnits + 0.48
        End Select
    Next lngIndex

    dblFactor = 1
    If InStr(1, fontName, "Bold", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
        dblFactor = 1.03
    End If
    EstimateTextWidth = dblUnits * fontSize * dblFactor

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    EstimateTextWidth = Len(textValue) * fontSize * 0.5

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "fontName;fontSize", _
        fontName, _
        fontSize

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldPdfPath As String, newPdfPath As String, auditOutputPath As String, detailOutputPath As String
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Compares a generated PDF with an Oracle reference and writes normalized audit and detailed reports.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function PdfOracleCloneAudit( _
    ByVal oldPdfPath As String, _
    ByVal newPdfPath As String, _
    ByVal auditOutputPath As String, _
    ByVal detailOutputPath As String, _
    Optional ByVal breaksOutputPath As String = vbNullString, _
    Optional ByVal breaksSummaryOutputPath As String = vbNullString) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PdfOracleCloneAudit"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim newDoc As tPdfDocument
    Dim oldDoc As tPdfDocument

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Len(Trim$(oldPdfPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1001, METHOD_NAME, "The old PDF path is required."
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(newPdfPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1001, METHOD_NAME, "The new PDF path is required."
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(auditOutputPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1020, METHOD_NAME, "The audit output path is required."
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(detailOutputPath)) = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1020, METHOD_NAME, "The detail output path is required."
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(breaksOutputPath)) = 0 Then
        breaksOutputPath = BuildBreaksOnlyOutputPath(oldPdfPath, newPdfPath)
    End If

    If Len(Trim$(breaksSummaryOutputPath)) = 0 Then
        breaksSummaryOutputPath = BuildBreaksSummaryOutputPath(oldPdfPath, newPdfPath)
    End If

    Call LoadPdfDocument(oldPdfPath, oldDoc)
    If oldDoc.PageCount = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "The old PDF could not be parsed into page content."
    End If

    Call LoadPdfDocument(newPdfPath, newDoc)
    If newDoc.PageCount = 0 Then
        Err.Raise 1014, METHOD_NAME, "The new PDF could not be parsed into page content."
    End If

    Call WriteOracleCloneAuditReport(oldDoc, newDoc, auditOutputPath)
    Call WriteComparisonReport(oldDoc, newDoc, detailOutputPath)
    Call WriteBreaksOnlyReport(oldDoc, newDoc, breaksOutputPath)
    Call WriteBreaksExecutiveSummary( _
        oldDoc, _
        newDoc, _
        breaksOutputPath, _
        breaksSummaryOutputPath)
    PdfOracleCloneAudit = True

ExitFunction:
    Call ResetParserState
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    PdfOracleCloneAudit = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldPdfPath;newPdfPath;auditOutputPath;detailOutputPath;breaksOutputPath;breaksSummaryOutputPath", _
        oldPdfPath, _
        newPdfPath, _
        auditOutputPath, _
        detailOutputPath, _
        breaksOutputPath, _
        breaksSummaryOutputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldPdfPath As String, newPdfPath As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Builds the output path for the normalized Oracle clone audit.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildOracleAuditOutputPath( _
    ByVal oldPdfPath As String, _
    ByVal newPdfPath As String) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildOracleAuditOutputPath"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngSlashPosition As Long
    Dim strFolder As String
    Dim strNewBase As String
    Dim strOldBase As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngSlashPosition = InStrRev(newPdfPath, "\")
    If lngSlashPosition > 0 Then
        strFolder = Left$(newPdfPath, lngSlashPosition)
    End If

    strOldBase = FileBaseName(oldPdfPath)
    strNewBase = FileBaseName(newPdfPath)
    BuildOracleAuditOutputPath = strFolder & strOldBase & "_VS_" & strNewBase & "_oracle_clone_audit.txt"

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BuildOracleAuditOutputPath = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldPdfPath;newPdfPath", _
        oldPdfPath, _
        newPdfPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

 '-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldPdfPath As String, newPdfPath As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Builds the path for the concise actionable Oracle compatibility report.
