'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, outputPath As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes a normalized parser-sensitive audit focused on meaningful Oracle compatibility differences.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteOracleCloneAuditReport( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal outputPath As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteOracleCloneAuditReport"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngCommonPages As Long
    Dim lngCriticalTotal As Long
    Dim lngFileNumber As Long
    Dim lngInfoTotal As Long
    Dim lngPage As Long
    Dim lngPageCritical As Long
    Dim lngPageInfo As Long
    Dim lngPageStructural As Long
    Dim lngStructuralTotal As Long
    Dim strStatus As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngFileNumber = OpenNew(outputPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "ORACLE_CLONE_AUDIT_V3")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "OLD|" & oldDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "NEW|" & newDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "PURPOSE|Normalize ordering and object-number noise, then report parser-sensitive geometry, text, font, filter, and operator differences.")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "IMPORTANT|Use matching PDFs when possible. This version can ignore business-value text differences while still auditing text anchors, fonts, geometry, and reading-order topology.")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "INPUT_CONTENT_POLICY|ignoreBusinessValueDifferences=" & BoolText(BREAKS_IGNORE_INPUT_CONTENT_DIFFERENCES))
    Call WriteDocumentAudit(oldDoc, newDoc, lngFileNumber, lngCriticalTotal, lngStructuralTotal, lngInfoTotal)

    lngCommonPages = oldDoc.PageCount
    If newDoc.PageCount < lngCommonPages Then
        lngCommonPages = newDoc.PageCount
    End If

    For lngPage = 1 To lngCommonPages
        lngPageCritical = 0
        lngPageStructural = 0
        lngPageInfo = 0
        Call WriteOraclePageAudit(oldDoc, newDoc, lngPage, lngFileNumber, lngPageCritical, lngPageStructural, lngPageInfo)
        lngCriticalTotal = lngCriticalTotal + lngPageCritical
        lngStructuralTotal = lngStructuralTotal + lngPageStructural
        lngInfoTotal = lngInfoTotal + lngPageInfo
    Next lngPage

    If lngCriticalTotal = 0 And lngStructuralTotal = 0 Then
        strStatus = "PARSER_SENSITIVE_MATCH"
    ElseIf lngCriticalTotal = 0 Then
        strStatus = "GEOMETRY_AND_TEXT_MATCH_STRUCTURAL_DIFFERENCES_REMAIN"
    Else
        strStatus = "PARSER_SENSITIVE_DIFFERENCES_REMAIN"
    End If

    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "AUDIT_SUMMARY|status=" & strStatus & "|critical=" & CStr(lngCriticalTotal) & "|structural=" & CStr(lngStructuralTotal) & "|informational=" & CStr(lngInfoTotal))
    If lngCriticalTotal = 0 Then
        Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "NEXT_ACTION|Do not change table coordinates or target separators unless the client still reports a parsing error. Review STRUCTURAL entries only when pursuing a closer Oracle stream clone.")
    Else
        Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "NEXT_ACTION|Resolve CRITICAL entries first. These are geometry, page-box, text-content, or text-position differences that can change table extraction.")
    End If
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "LIMITATION|This audit cannot execute the proprietary client parser. A zero critical count is strong structural evidence, not a guarantee of identical proprietary-parser behavior.")

ExitSub:
    If lngFileNumber > 0 Then
        Close #lngFileNumber
    End If
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "outputPath", _
        outputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, fileNumber As Long, criticalCount As Long, structuralCount As Long, infoCount As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes document-level compatibility checks and classifies meaningful differences.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteDocumentAudit( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long, _
    ByRef structuralCount As Long, _
    ByRef infoCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteDocumentAudit"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strMatch As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strMatch = BoolText(oldDoc.PdfVersion = newDoc.PdfVersion)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DOCUMENT_VERSION|old=" & oldDoc.PdfVersion & "|new=" & newDoc.PdfVersion & "|match=" & strMatch)
    If oldDoc.PdfVersion <> newDoc.PdfVersion Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
    End If

    strMatch = BoolText(oldDoc.PageCount = newDoc.PageCount)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DOCUMENT_PAGES|old=" & CStr(oldDoc.PageCount) & "|new=" & CStr(newDoc.PageCount) & "|match=" & strMatch)
    If oldDoc.PageCount <> newDoc.PageCount Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
    End If

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DOCUMENT_XREF|old=" & oldDoc.XrefKind & "|new=" & newDoc.XrefKind & "|match=" & BoolText(oldDoc.XrefKind = newDoc.XrefKind))
    If oldDoc.XrefKind <> newDoc.XrefKind Then
        infoCount = infoCount + 1
    End If

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DOCUMENT_OBJECTS|old=" & CStr(oldDoc.ObjectCount) & "|new=" & CStr(newDoc.ObjectCount) & "|classification=INFORMATIONAL")
    If oldDoc.ObjectCount <> newDoc.ObjectCount Then
        infoCount = infoCount + 1
    End If

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "DOCUMENT_BYTES|old=" & CStr(oldDoc.FileSize) & "|new=" & CStr(newDoc.FileSize) & "|classification=INFORMATIONAL")

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, fileNumber As Long, criticalCount As Long, structuralCount As Long, infoCount As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes normalized page-level geometry, text, font, stream, and target-separator comparisons.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteOraclePageAudit( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long, _
    ByRef structuralCount As Long, _
    ByRef infoCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteOraclePageAudit"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictNew As Object
    Dim dictOld As Object
    Dim lngChanged As Long
    Dim lngNewOnly As Long
    Dim lngOldOnly As Long
    Dim strNewCrop As String
    Dim strNewRotate As String
    Dim strNewTextSequence As String
    Dim strOldCrop As String
    Dim strOldRotate As String
    Dim strOldTextSequence As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE_AUDIT|" & CStr(pageNo))

    strOldCrop = EffectiveCropBox(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo))
    strNewCrop = EffectiveCropBox(newDoc.Pages(pageNo))
    strOldRotate = NormalizeRotateValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue)
    strNewRotate = NormalizeRotateValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue)

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE_BOX|MediaBoxOld=" & NormalizePdfValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) & "|MediaBoxNew=" & NormalizePdfValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) & "|mediaMatch=" & BoolText(NormalizePdfValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) = NormalizePdfValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox)) & "|CropBoxOld=" & strOldCrop & "|CropBoxNew=" & strNewCrop & "|cropMatch=" & BoolText(strOldCrop = strNewCrop) & "|RotateOld=" & strOldRotate & "|RotateNew=" & strNewRotate & "|rotateMatch=" & BoolText(strOldRotate = strNewRotate))
    If NormalizePdfValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) <> NormalizePdfValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
    End If
    If strOldCrop <> strNewCrop Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
    End If
    If strOldRotate <> strNewRotate Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
    End If

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE_FONTS|old=" & oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts & "|new=" & newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts & "|match=" & BoolText(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts = newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts))
    If oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts <> newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
    End If

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE_FILTERS|old=" & oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters & "|new=" & newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters & "|match=" & BoolText(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters = newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters))
    If oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters <> newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
    End If

    Set dictOld = BuildImageAuditDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo)
    Set dictNew = BuildImageAuditDictionary(newDoc, pageNo)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "IMAGE_PLACEMENT_AUDIT|oldCount=" & CStr(DictionaryValueTotal(dictOld)) & "|newCount=" & CStr(DictionaryValueTotal(dictNew)) & "|oldOnly=" & CStr(lngOldOnly) & "|newOnly=" & CStr(lngNewOnly) & "|multiplicity=" & CStr(lngChanged) & "|match=" & BoolText(lngOldOnly = 0 And lngNewOnly = 0 And lngChanged = 0))
    If lngOldOnly > 0 Or lngNewOnly > 0 Or lngChanged > 0 Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples("IMAGE", dictOld, dictNew, fileNumber, 12)
    End If
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing

    Set dictOld = BuildLineAuditDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo, False)
    Set dictNew = BuildLineAuditDictionary(newDoc, pageNo, False)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "GEOMETRY_AUDIT|oldOnly=" & CStr(lngOldOnly) & "|newOnly=" & CStr(lngNewOnly) & "|multiplicity=" & CStr(lngChanged) & "|match=" & BoolText(lngOldOnly = 0 And lngNewOnly = 0 And lngChanged = 0))
    If lngOldOnly > 0 Or lngNewOnly > 0 Or lngChanged > 0 Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples("GEOMETRY", dictOld, dictNew, fileNumber, 24)
    End If
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing

    Set dictOld = BuildLineAuditDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo, True)
    Set dictNew = BuildLineAuditDictionary(newDoc, pageNo, True)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "STYLED_VECTOR_AUDIT|oldOnly=" & CStr(lngOldOnly) & "|newOnly=" & CStr(lngNewOnly) & "|multiplicity=" & CStr(lngChanged) & "|match=" & BoolText(lngOldOnly = 0 And lngNewOnly = 0 And lngChanged = 0))
    If lngOldOnly > 0 Or lngNewOnly > 0 Or lngChanged > 0 Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples("STYLED_VECTOR", dictOld, dictNew, fileNumber, 24)
    End If
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing

    Set dictOld = BuildTextAuditDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo, False)
    Set dictNew = BuildTextAuditDictionary(newDoc, pageNo, False)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TEXT_CONTENT_AUDIT|oldOnly=" & CStr(lngOldOnly) & "|newOnly=" & CStr(lngNewOnly) & "|multiplicity=" & CStr(lngChanged) & "|match=" & BoolText(lngOldOnly = 0 And lngNewOnly = 0 And lngChanged = 0))
    If lngOldOnly > 0 Or lngNewOnly > 0 Or lngChanged > 0 Then
        If BREAKS_IGNORE_INPUT_CONTENT_DIFFERENCES Then
            infoCount = infoCount + 1
            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TEXT_CONTENT_CLASSIFICATION|INFORMATIONAL_EXPECTED_INPUT_DIFFERENCE")
        Else
            structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        End If
        Call WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples("TEXT_CONTENT", dictOld, dictNew, fileNumber, 20)
    End If
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing

    Set dictOld = BuildTextAuditDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo, True)
    Set dictNew = BuildTextAuditDictionary(newDoc, pageNo, True)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TEXT_POSITION_AUDIT|oldOnly=" & CStr(lngOldOnly) & "|newOnly=" & CStr(lngNewOnly) & "|multiplicity=" & CStr(lngChanged) & "|match=" & BoolText(lngOldOnly = 0 And lngNewOnly = 0 And lngChanged = 0))
    If lngOldOnly > 0 Or lngNewOnly > 0 Or lngChanged > 0 Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples("TEXT_POSITION", dictOld, dictNew, fileNumber, 20)
    End If
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing

    strOldTextSequence = BuildNormalizedTextSequence(oldDoc, pageNo)
    strNewTextSequence = BuildNormalizedTextSequence(newDoc, pageNo)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TEXT_READING_ORDER|match=" & BoolText(strOldTextSequence = strNewTextSequence))
    If strOldTextSequence <> strNewTextSequence Then
        If BREAKS_IGNORE_INPUT_CONTENT_DIFFERENCES Then
            infoCount = infoCount + 1
            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TEXT_READING_ORDER_CLASSIFICATION|INFORMATIONAL_WHEN_INPUT_VALUES_DIFFER")
        Else
            criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        End If
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TEXT_READING_ORDER_OLD|" & Left$(strOldTextSequence, 1500))
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TEXT_READING_ORDER_NEW|" & Left$(strNewTextSequence, 1500))
    End If

    Set dictOld = BuildOperatorProfileDictionary(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentText)
    Set dictNew = BuildOperatorProfileDictionary(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentText)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "OPERATOR_PROFILE|oldOnly=" & CStr(lngOldOnly) & "|newOnly=" & CStr(lngNewOnly) & "|countChanged=" & CStr(lngChanged) & "|match=" & BoolText(lngOldOnly = 0 And lngNewOnly = 0 And lngChanged = 0))
    If lngOldOnly > 0 Or lngNewOnly > 0 Or lngChanged > 0 Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples("OPERATOR", dictOld, dictNew, fileNumber, 30)
    End If
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing

    Call WriteNormalizedTargetAudit(oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_FIRST, fileNumber, criticalCount)
    Call WriteNormalizedTargetAudit(oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_SECOND, fileNumber, criticalCount)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE_CLASSIFICATION|critical=" & CStr(criticalCount) & "|structural=" & CStr(structuralCount) & "|informational=" & CStr(infoCount))

ExitSub:
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, targetX As Double, fileNumber As Long, criticalCount As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Compares one parser-sensitive separator using sorted styled-line multisets and text-crossing counts.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteNormalizedTargetAudit( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteNormalizedTargetAudit"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictNew As Object
    Dim dictOld As Object
    Dim lngChanged As Long
    Dim lngNewCrossings As Long
    Dim lngNewOnly As Long
    Dim lngOldCrossings As Long
    Dim lngOldOnly As Long
    Dim strMatch As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictOld = BuildTargetLineAuditDictionary(oldDoc, pageNo, targetX)
    Set dictNew = BuildTargetLineAuditDictionary(newDoc, pageNo, targetX)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)
    lngOldCrossings = CountTargetTextCrossings(oldDoc, pageNo, targetX, 0, False)
    lngNewCrossings = CountTargetTextCrossings(newDoc, pageNo, targetX, 0, False)
    strMatch = BoolText(lngOldOnly = 0 And lngNewOnly = 0 And lngChanged = 0 And lngOldCrossings = lngNewCrossings)

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "TARGET_AUDIT|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & "|oldOccurrences=" & CStr(DictionaryValueTotal(dictOld)) & "|newOccurrences=" & CStr(DictionaryValueTotal(dictNew)) & "|oldUnique=" & CStr(dictOld.Count) & "|newUnique=" & CStr(dictNew.Count) & "|oldCrossings=" & CStr(lngOldCrossings) & "|newCrossings=" & CStr(lngNewCrossings) & "|match=" & strMatch)
    If strMatch = "FALSE" Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples("TARGET_" & Replace(NumTxt(targetX), ".", "_"), dictOld, dictNew, fileNumber, 20)
    End If

ExitSub:
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub


'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long
' Returns:       Object
' Description:   Builds a resource-name-independent multiset of XObject placement matrices for one page.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildImageAuditDictionary( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As Object

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildImageAuditDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictResult As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictResult = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.ImageCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Images(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            strKey = "matrix=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Images(lngIndex).A) & "," & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Images(lngIndex).B) & "," & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Images(lngIndex).C) & "," & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Images(lngIndex).D) & "," & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Images(lngIndex).E) & "," & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Images(lngIndex).F)
            Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictResult, strKey)
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Set BuildImageAuditDictionary = dictResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Set BuildImageAuditDictionary = Nothing

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, includeStyle As Boolean
' Returns:       Object
' Description:   Builds a canonical line multiset with optional style and source-operator information.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildLineAuditDictionary( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal includeStyle As Boolean) As Object

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildLineAuditDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictResult As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictResult = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            strKey = CanonicalLineGeometryKey(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex))
            If includeStyle Then
                strKey = strKey & "|width=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).LineWidth) & "|paint=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PaintOperator & "|source=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).SourceOperator & "|color=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).StrokeColor
            End If
            Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictResult, strKey)
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Set BuildLineAuditDictionary = dictResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Set BuildLineAuditDictionary = Nothing

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;includeStyle", _
        pageNo, _
        includeStyle

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, targetX As Double
' Returns:       Object
' Description:   Builds a sorted-order-independent styled multiset for one vertical target separator.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildTargetLineAuditDictionary( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double) As Object

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildTargetLineAuditDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictResult As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictResult = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If IsVerticalAtX(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex), targetX) Then
                strKey = CanonicalLineGeometryKey(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & "|width=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).LineWidth) & "|paint=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PaintOperator & "|source=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).SourceOperator & "|color=" & pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).StrokeColor
                Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictResult, strKey)
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Set BuildTargetLineAuditDictionary = dictResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Set BuildTargetLineAuditDictionary = Nothing

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long, includePosition As Boolean
' Returns:       Object
' Description:   Builds a text multiset using either content only or exact anchor, font, size, and content.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildTextAuditDictionary( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal includePosition As Boolean) As Object

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildTextAuditDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictResult As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictResult = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If includePosition Then
                strKey = "x=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) & _
                         "|y=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) & _
                         "|font=" & pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontName & _
                         "|size=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontSize)

                If Not BREAKS_IGNORE_INPUT_CONTENT_DIFFERENCES Then
                    strKey = strKey & "|text=" & SanitizeReportText(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue)
                End If
            Else
                strKey = "text=" & SanitizeReportText(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue)
            End If
            Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictResult, strKey)
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Set BuildTextAuditDictionary = dictResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Set BuildTextAuditDictionary = Nothing

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;includePosition", _
        pageNo, _
        includePosition

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    content As String
' Returns:       Object
' Description:   Counts parser-relevant PDF graphics and text operators in a page content stream.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildOperatorProfileDictionary(ByVal content As String) As Object
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildOperatorProfileDictionary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrTokens() As String
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictResult As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngTokenCount As Long
    Dim strToken As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictResult = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    lngTokenCount = Tokenize(content, arrTokens)
    For lngIndex = 0 To lngTokenCount - 1
        strToken = arrTokens(lngIndex)
        If IsAuditOperator(strToken) Then
            Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictResult, strToken)
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    Set BuildOperatorProfileDictionary = dictResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    Set BuildOperatorProfileDictionary = Nothing

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "contentLength", _
        Len(content)

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    operatorValue As String
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Returns whether a token is relevant to Oracle stream-structure comparison.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function IsAuditOperator(ByVal operatorValue As String) As Boolean
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IsAuditOperator"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Select Case operatorValue
        Case "q", "Q", "cm", "w", "G", "RG", "K", "g", "rg", "k", _
             "m", "l", "h", "re", "S", "s", "B", "B*", "b", "b*", _
             "f", "f*", "F", "n", "BT", "ET", "Tf", "Tm", "Td", "TD", _
             "T*", "Tj", "TJ", "'", "Do"
            IsAuditOperator = True
    End Select

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    IsAuditOperator = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "operatorValue", _
        operatorValue

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, pageNo As Long
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Builds a reading-order text signature while normalizing adjacent text-run segmentation.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildNormalizedTextSequence( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildNormalizedTextSequence"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrIndices() As Long
    Dim blnFirst As Boolean
    Dim dblBaseline As Double
    Dim lngCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngInner As Long
    Dim lngSwap As Long
    Dim strResult As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount = 0 Then
        BuildNormalizedTextSequence = "(none)"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    ReDim arrIndices(0 To pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount - 1)
    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            arrIndices(lngCount) = lngIndex
            lngCount = lngCount + 1
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    If lngCount = 0 Then
        BuildNormalizedTextSequence = "(none)"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If
    ReDim Preserve arrIndices(0 To lngCount - 1)

    For lngIndex = 0 To lngCount - 2
        For lngInner = lngIndex + 1 To lngCount - 1
            If TextIndexComesBefore(pdfDoc, arrIndices(lngInner), arrIndices(lngIndex)) Then
                lngSwap = arrIndices(lngIndex)
                arrIndices(lngIndex) = arrIndices(lngInner)
                arrIndices(lngInner) = lngSwap
            End If
        Next lngInner
    Next lngIndex

    blnFirst = True
    For lngIndex = 0 To lngCount - 1
        If blnFirst Then
            dblBaseline = pdfDoc.Texts(arrIndices(lngIndex)).Y
            strResult = "y=" & NumTxt(dblBaseline) & ":"
            blnFirst = False
        ElseIf Abs(pdfDoc.Texts(arrIndices(lngIndex)).Y - dblBaseline) > 0.15 Then
            dblBaseline = pdfDoc.Texts(arrIndices(lngIndex)).Y
            strResult = strResult & "^y=" & NumTxt(dblBaseline) & ":"
        End If
        If BREAKS_IGNORE_INPUT_CONTENT_DIFFERENCES Then
            strResult = strResult & _
                "[x=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(arrIndices(lngIndex)).X) & _
                ",f=" & pdfDoc.Texts(arrIndices(lngIndex)).FontName & _
                ",s=" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(arrIndices(lngIndex)).FontSize) & "]"
        Else
            strResult = strResult & SanitizeReportText(pdfDoc.Texts(arrIndices(lngIndex)).TextValue)
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    BuildNormalizedTextSequence = strResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BuildNormalizedTextSequence = "(error)"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, firstIndex As Long, secondIndex As Long
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Returns whether the first text run sorts before the second in visual reading order.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function TextIndexComesBefore( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal firstIndex As Long, _
    ByVal secondIndex As Long) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "TextIndexComesBefore"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If pdfDoc.Texts(firstIndex).Y > pdfDoc.Texts(secondIndex).Y + 0.01 Then
        TextIndexComesBefore = True
    ElseIf Abs(pdfDoc.Texts(firstIndex).Y - pdfDoc.Texts(secondIndex).Y) <= 0.01 Then
        TextIndexComesBefore = pdfDoc.Texts(firstIndex).X < pdfDoc.Texts(secondIndex).X
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    TextIndexComesBefore = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "firstIndex;secondIndex", _
        firstIndex, _
        secondIndex

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldValues As Object, newValues As Object, oldOnly As Long, newOnly As Long, multiplicityChanged As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Counts canonical multiset differences without depending on dictionary insertion order.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub DictionaryDifferenceCounts( _
    ByVal oldValues As Object, _
    ByVal newValues As Object, _
    ByRef oldOnly As Long, _
    ByRef newOnly As Long, _
    ByRef multiplicityChanged As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DictionaryDifferenceCounts"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    oldOnly = 0
    newOnly = 0
    multiplicityChanged = 0

    For Each varKey In oldValues.Keys
        If Not newValues.Exists(varKey) Then
            oldOnly = oldOnly + 1
        ElseIf CLng(oldValues(varKey)) <> CLng(newValues(varKey)) Then
            multiplicityChanged = multiplicityChanged + 1
        End If
    Next varKey

    For Each varKey In newValues.Keys
        If Not oldValues.Exists(varKey) Then
            newOnly = newOnly + 1
        End If
    Next varKey

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    prefix As String, oldValues As Object, newValues As Object, fileNumber As Long, maxLines As Long
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes deterministic sorted samples of canonical dictionary differences.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples( _
    ByVal prefix As String, _
    ByVal oldValues As Object, _
    ByVal newValues As Object, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal maxLines As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteDictionaryDifferenceSamples"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrKeys As Variant
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngWritten As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    arrKeys = SortedDictionaryKeys(oldValues)
    If oldValues.Count > 0 Then
        For lngIndex = LBound(arrKeys) To UBound(arrKeys)
            strKey = CStr(arrKeys(lngIndex))
            If Not newValues.Exists(strKey) Then
                Call EmitLine(fileNumber, prefix & "_ONLY_OLD|count=" & CStr(oldValues(strKey)) & "|" & strKey)
                lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
            ElseIf CLng(oldValues(strKey)) <> CLng(newValues(strKey)) Then
                Call EmitLine(fileNumber, prefix & "_COUNT_CHANGED|old=" & CStr(oldValues(strKey)) & "|new=" & CStr(newValues(strKey)) & "|" & strKey)
                lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
            End If
            If lngWritten >= maxLines Then
                GoTo ExitSub
            End If
        Next lngIndex
    End If

    arrKeys = SortedDictionaryKeys(newValues)
    If newValues.Count > 0 Then
        For lngIndex = LBound(arrKeys) To UBound(arrKeys)
            strKey = CStr(arrKeys(lngIndex))
            If Not oldValues.Exists(strKey) Then
                Call EmitLine(fileNumber, prefix & "_ONLY_NEW|count=" & CStr(newValues(strKey)) & "|" & strKey)
                lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
            End If
            If lngWritten >= maxLines Then
                GoTo ExitSub
            End If
        Next lngIndex
    End If

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "prefix;maxLines", _
        prefix, _
        maxLines

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    dictValues As Object
' Returns:       Variant
' Description:   Returns dictionary keys sorted alphabetically for deterministic comparison output.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function SortedDictionaryKeys(ByVal dictValues As Object) As Variant
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "SortedDictionaryKeys"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrKeys() As String
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngInner As Long
    Dim strSwap As String
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If dictValues.Count = 0 Then
        SortedDictionaryKeys = Array()
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    ReDim arrKeys(0 To dictValues.Count - 1)
    For Each varKey In dictValues.Keys
        arrKeys(lngIndex) = CStr(varKey)
        lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
    Next varKey

    For lngIndex = LBound(arrKeys) To UBound(arrKeys) - 1
        For lngInner = lngIndex + 1 To UBound(arrKeys)
            If StrComp(arrKeys(lngInner), arrKeys(lngIndex), vbBinaryCompare) < 0 Then
                strSwap = arrKeys(lngIndex)
                arrKeys(lngIndex) = arrKeys(lngInner)
                arrKeys(lngInner) = strSwap
            End If
        Next lngInner
    Next lngIndex

    SortedDictionaryKeys = arrKeys

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    SortedDictionaryKeys = Array()

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    dictValues As Object, keyValue As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Increments a Long count stored in a late-bound dictionary.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub IncrementDictionaryValue( _
    ByVal dictValues As Object, _
    ByVal keyValue As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IncrementDictionaryValue"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If dictValues.Exists(keyValue) Then
        dictValues(keyValue) = CLng(dictValues(keyValue)) + 1
    Else
        dictValues.Add keyValue, 1
    End If

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "keyValue", _
        keyValue

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    dictValues As Object
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Returns the sum of numeric multiplicity values in a dictionary.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DictionaryValueTotal(ByVal dictValues As Object) As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DictionaryValueTotal"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim varKey As Variant

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For Each varKey In dictValues.Keys
        DictionaryValueTotal = DictionaryValueTotal + CLng(dictValues(varKey))
    Next varKey

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    DictionaryValueTotal = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    lineInfo As tPdfLine
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns a canonical geometry key for vertical, horizontal, or diagonal line segments.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function CanonicalLineGeometryKey(ByRef lineInfo As tPdfLine) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CanonicalLineGeometryKey"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblAx As Double
    Dim dblAy As Double
    Dim dblBx As Double
    Dim dblBy As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Abs(lineInfo.X1 - lineInfo.X2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
        CanonicalLineGeometryKey = "V|x=" & NumTxt((lineInfo.X1 + lineInfo.X2) / 2) & "|y=" & NumTxt(MinDouble(lineInfo.Y1, lineInfo.Y2)) & ".." & NumTxt(MaxDouble(lineInfo.Y1, lineInfo.Y2))
    ElseIf Abs(lineInfo.Y1 - lineInfo.Y2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
        CanonicalLineGeometryKey = "H|y=" & NumTxt((lineInfo.Y1 + lineInfo.Y2) / 2) & "|x=" & NumTxt(MinDouble(lineInfo.X1, lineInfo.X2)) & ".." & NumTxt(MaxDouble(lineInfo.X1, lineInfo.X2))
    Else
        dblAx = lineInfo.X1
        dblAy = lineInfo.Y1
        dblBx = lineInfo.X2
        dblBy = lineInfo.Y2
        If dblAx > dblBx Or (Abs(dblAx - dblBx) <= COORD_TOLERANCE And dblAy > dblBy) Then
            dblAx = lineInfo.X2
            dblAy = lineInfo.Y2
            dblBx = lineInfo.X1
            dblBy = lineInfo.Y1
        End If
        CanonicalLineGeometryKey = "D|a=" & NumTxt(dblAx) & "," & NumTxt(dblAy) & "|b=" & NumTxt(dblBx) & "," & NumTxt(dblBy)
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    CanonicalLineGeometryKey = "ERROR"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pageInfo As tPdfPage
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns the effective CropBox, applying the PDF default to MediaBox when CropBox is absent.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function EffectiveCropBox(ByRef pageInfo As tPdfPage) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "EffectiveCropBox"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If pageInfo.CropBox = "(absent)" Or Len(Trim$(pageInfo.CropBox)) = 0 Then
        EffectiveCropBox = NormalizePdfValue(pageInfo.MediaBox)
    Else
        EffectiveCropBox = NormalizePdfValue(pageInfo.CropBox)
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    EffectiveCropBox = "(error)"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

' Description:   Normalizes an absent PDF Rotate entry to its effective zero-degree value.

Private Function NormalizeRotateValue(ByVal rotateValue As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "NormalizeRotateValue"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strValue As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strValue = Trim$(rotateValue)
    If Len(strValue) = 0 Or strValue = "(absent)" Then
        NormalizeRotateValue = "0"
    Else
        NormalizeRotateValue = strValue
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    NormalizeRotateValue = rotateValue

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    valueText As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Removes formatting whitespace from compact PDF dictionary values before semantic comparison.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function NormalizePdfValue(ByVal valueText As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "NormalizePdfValue"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strValue As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strValue = Replace(valueText, vbCr, vbNullString)
    strValue = Replace(strValue, vbLf, vbNullString)
    strValue = Replace(strValue, vbTab, " ")
    Do While InStr(1, strValue, "  ", vbBinaryCompare) > 0
        strValue = Replace(strValue, "  ", " ")
    Loop
    NormalizePdfValue = Trim$(strValue)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    NormalizePdfValue = valueText

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

    If booleanValue Then
        BoolText = "TRUE"
    Else
        BoolText = "FALSE"

ExitFunction:

    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BoolText = "FALSE"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pageDictionary As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Resolves page font resource names to BaseFont, subtype, and encoding without object-number noise.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function ResolvePageFontSignature(ByVal pageDictionary As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "ResolvePageFontSignature"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrReferences() As String
    Dim arrTokens() As String
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictFonts As Object
    Dim lngFontObject As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngObjectIndex As Long
    Dim strBaseFont As String
    Dim strDescriptor As String
    Dim strEncoding As String
    Dim strFontDictionary As String
    Dim strFontObjectDictionary As String
    Dim strFontRefs As String
    Dim strResourceName As String
    Dim strSubtype As String
    Dim strWidths As String
    Dim strWorking As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictFonts = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    strFontDictionary = DictValue(pageDictionary, "/Font")
    If strFontDictionary = "(absent)" Then
        ResolvePageFontSignature = "(none)"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    If InStr(1, strFontDictionary, " R", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then
        strFontRefs = RefList(pageDictionary, "/Font")
        If Len(strFontRefs) > 0 Then
            arrReferences = Split(strFontRefs, ",")
            lngObjectIndex = IndexOfObject(CLng(arrReferences(0)))
            If lngObjectIndex >= 0 Then
                strFontDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngObjectIndex))
            End If
        End If
    End If

    strWorking = Replace(strFontDictionary, "<<", " ")
    strWorking = Replace(strWorking, ">>", " ")
    strWorking = Replace(strWorking, vbCr, " ")
    strWorking = Replace(strWorking, vbLf, " ")
    Do While InStr(1, strWorking, "  ", vbBinaryCompare) > 0
        strWorking = Replace(strWorking, "  ", " ")
    Loop
    arrTokens = Split(Trim$(strWorking), " ")

    For lngIndex = LBound(arrTokens) To UBound(arrTokens) - 3
        If Left$(arrTokens(lngIndex), 1) = "/" Then
            If IsNumeric(arrTokens(lngIndex + 1)) And arrTokens(lngIndex + 3) = "R" Then
                strResourceName = arrTokens(lngIndex)
                If Left$(strResourceName, 2) = "/F" Then
                    lngFontObject = CLng(arrTokens(lngIndex + 1))
                    lngObjectIndex = IndexOfObject(lngFontObject)
                    If lngObjectIndex >= 0 Then
                        strFontObjectDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngObjectIndex))
                        strBaseFont = DictValue(strFontObjectDictionary, "/BaseFont")
                        strSubtype = DictValue(strFontObjectDictionary, "/Subtype")
                        strEncoding = DictValue(strFontObjectDictionary, "/Encoding")
                        strDescriptor = FontDescriptorSignature(strFontObjectDictionary)
                        If InStr(1, strFontObjectDictionary, "/Widths", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
                            strWidths = "present"
                        Else
                            strWidths = "absent"
                        End If
                        dictFonts(strResourceName) = strResourceName & "=" & strBaseFont & ",subtype=" & strSubtype & ",encoding=" & strEncoding & ",widths=" & strWidths & ",descriptor=" & strDescriptor
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    ResolvePageFontSignature = DictionaryEntrySignature(dictFonts)
    If Len(ResolvePageFontSignature) = 0 Then
        ResolvePageFontSignature = "(unresolved)"
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Set dictFonts = Nothing
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    ResolvePageFontSignature = "(error)"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    fontObjectDictionary As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Resolves parser-relevant FontDescriptor metrics while ignoring indirect object numbers.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FontDescriptorSignature(ByVal fontObjectDictionary As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FontDescriptorSignature"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrReferences() As String
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngObjectIndex As Long
    Dim strDescriptorDictionary As String
    Dim strReferences As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strReferences = RefList(fontObjectDictionary, "/FontDescriptor")
    If Len(strReferences) = 0 Then
        FontDescriptorSignature = "absent"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    arrReferences = Split(strReferences, ",")
    lngObjectIndex = IndexOfObject(CLng(arrReferences(0)))
    If lngObjectIndex < 0 Then
        FontDescriptorSignature = "unresolved"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    strDescriptorDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngObjectIndex))
    FontDescriptorSignature = _
        "bbox=" & DictValue(strDescriptorDictionary, "/FontBBox") & _
        ",flags=" & DictValue(strDescriptorDictionary, "/Flags") & _
        ",ascent=" & DictValue(strDescriptorDictionary, "/Ascent") & _
        ",descent=" & DictValue(strDescriptorDictionary, "/Descent") & _
        ",capHeight=" & DictValue(strDescriptorDictionary, "/CapHeight") & _
        ",stemV=" & DictValue(strDescriptorDictionary, "/StemV")

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FontDescriptorSignature = "error"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pageDictionary As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns the page content-stream filter sequence without indirect-object-number noise.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function PageContentFilterSignature(ByVal pageDictionary As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "PageContentFilterSignature"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrReferences() As String
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictFilters As Object
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngObjectIndex As Long
    Dim strDictionary As String
    Dim strFilter As String
    Dim strReferenceList As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictFilters = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    strReferenceList = RefList(pageDictionary, "/Contents")
    If Len(strReferenceList) = 0 Then
        PageContentFilterSignature = "(direct-or-none)"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    arrReferences = Split(strReferenceList, ",")
    For lngIndex = LBound(arrReferences) To UBound(arrReferences)
        lngObjectIndex = IndexOfObject(CLng(arrReferences(lngIndex)))
        If lngObjectIndex >= 0 Then
            strDictionary = ObjectDict(m_objPos(lngObjectIndex))
            strFilter = DictValue(strDictionary, "/Filter")
            If strFilter = "(absent)" Then
                strFilter = "none"
            End If
            Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictFilters, NormalizePdfValue(strFilter))
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    PageContentFilterSignature = DictionaryCountSignature(dictFilters)

ExitFunction:
    Set dictFilters = Nothing
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    PageContentFilterSignature = "(error)"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    dictValues As Object
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns a deterministic key=value signature for dictionary string values.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DictionaryEntrySignature(ByVal dictValues As Object) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DictionaryEntrySignature"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrKeys As Variant
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String
    Dim strResult As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If dictValues.Count = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If
    arrKeys = SortedDictionaryKeys(dictValues)
    For lngIndex = LBound(arrKeys) To UBound(arrKeys)
        strKey = CStr(arrKeys(lngIndex))
        If Len(strResult) > 0 Then
            strResult = strResult & ";"
        End If
        strResult = strResult & CStr(dictValues(strKey))
    Next lngIndex
    DictionaryEntrySignature = strResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    DictionaryEntrySignature = "(error)"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    dictValues As Object
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns a deterministic key=count signature for a counted dictionary.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DictionaryCountSignature(ByVal dictValues As Object) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DictionaryCountSignature"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrKeys As Variant
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String
    Dim strResult As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If dictValues.Count = 0 Then
        DictionaryCountSignature = "(none)"
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If
    arrKeys = SortedDictionaryKeys(dictValues)
    For lngIndex = LBound(arrKeys) To UBound(arrKeys)
        strKey = CStr(arrKeys(lngIndex))
        If Len(strResult) > 0 Then
            strResult = strResult & ";"
        End If
        strResult = strResult & strKey & "=" & CStr(dictValues(strKey))
    Next lngIndex
    DictionaryCountSignature = strResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    DictionaryCountSignature = "(error)"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    lineInfo As tPdfLine
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns a normalized coordinate-only key for one horizontal or vertical line.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function GeometryKey(ByRef lineInfo As tPdfLine) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "GeometryKey"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dblFirst As Double
    Dim dblSecond As Double

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Abs(lineInfo.X1 - lineInfo.X2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
        dblFirst = MinDouble(lineInfo.Y1, lineInfo.Y2)
        dblSecond = MaxDouble(lineInfo.Y1, lineInfo.Y2)
        GeometryKey = "V|x=" & NumTxt((lineInfo.X1 + lineInfo.X2) / 2) & "|y=" & NumTxt(dblFirst) & ".." & NumTxt(dblSecond)
    Else
        dblFirst = MinDouble(lineInfo.X1, lineInfo.X2)
        dblSecond = MaxDouble(lineInfo.X1, lineInfo.X2)
        GeometryKey = "H|y=" & NumTxt((lineInfo.Y1 + lineInfo.Y2) / 2) & "|x=" & NumTxt(dblFirst) & ".." & NumTxt(dblSecond)
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    GeometryKey = "ERROR"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Detects the PDF version from the file header.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DetectPdfVersion() As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DetectPdfVersion"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strHeader As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strHeader = BytesText(m_pdf, 0, IIf(UBound(m_pdf) < 31, UBound(m_pdf), 31))
    If InStr(1, strHeader, "%PDF-", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
        DetectPdfVersion = Mid$(strHeader, InStr(1, strHeader, "%PDF-", vbBinaryCompare) + 5, 3)
    Else
        DetectPdfVersion = "unknown"
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    DetectPdfVersion = "unknown"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    ---
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Detects whether the PDF uses a classic xref table or an xref stream.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function DetectXrefKind() As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "DetectXrefKind"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If FindBytes(m_pdf, 0, "/Type/XRef") >= 0 Or FindBytes(m_pdf, 0, "/Type /XRef") >= 0 Then
        DetectXrefKind = "stream"
    ElseIf FindBytes(m_pdf, 0, vbLf & "xref") >= 0 Or FindBytes(m_pdf, 0, vbCr & "xref") >= 0 Then
        DetectXrefKind = "classic"
    Else
        DetectXrefKind = "unknown"
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    DetectXrefKind = "unknown"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function


'===============================================================================
' CONCISE ACTIONABLE ORACLE COMPATIBILITY REPORT
'===============================================================================

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc As tPdfDocument, newDoc As tPdfDocument, outputPath As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes only remaining parser-sensitive differences with OLD, NEW, DELTA, and FIX hints.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, detailedBreaksPath, outputPath
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes a short, screenshot-friendly summary of the detailed
'                BREAKS_ONLY report. Raw events are grouped into fix families.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteBreaksExecutiveSummary( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal detailedBreaksPath As String, _
    ByVal outputPath As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksExecutiveSummary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dictActionTypes As Object
    Dim dictFixNow As Object
    Dim dictFontPairs As Object
    Dim dictIgnore As Object
    Dim dictPages As Object
    Dim dictShiftPatterns As Object
    Dim dictVerify As Object
    Dim dictWhitespace As Object
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngCurrentPage As Long
    Dim lngFileIn As Long
    Dim lngFileOut As Long
    Dim lngLikelyTextShift As Long
    Dim lngMatchingNoise As Long
    Dim lngPage As Long
    Dim lngPagesCompared As Long
    Dim lngRawCritical As Long
    Dim lngRawInfo As Long
    Dim lngRawStructural As Long
    Dim lngTargetGeometry As Long
    Dim lngTargetStyleOnly As Long
    Dim lngTradeMismatchPages As Long
    Dim lngVectorGeometry As Long
    Dim lngVectorStyle As Long
    Dim oldTradeCount As Long
    Dim newTradeCount As Long
    Dim sampleFooter As Collection
    Dim sampleOperator As Collection
    Dim sampleTarget As Collection
    Dim sampleTextShift As Collection
    Dim sampleVector As Collection
    Dim strFooterOld As String
    Dim strFooterNew As String
    Dim strKey As Variant
    Dim strLine As String
    Dim strRawStatus As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictActionTypes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set dictFixNow = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set dictFontPairs = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set dictIgnore = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set dictPages = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set dictShiftPatterns = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set dictVerify = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set dictWhitespace = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    Set sampleFooter = New Collection
    Set sampleOperator = New Collection
    Set sampleTarget = New Collection
    Set sampleTextShift = New Collection
    Set sampleVector = New Collection

    lngFileIn = FreeFile
    Open detailedBreaksPath For Input Access Read As #lngFileIn

    Do While Not EOF(lngFileIn)
        Line Input #lngFileIn, strLine

        If Left$(strLine, 5) = "PAGE|" Then
            lngCurrentPage = CLng(Val(Mid$(strLine, 6)))
            If lngCurrentPage > lngPagesCompared Then
                lngPagesCompared = lngCurrentPage
            End If

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 12) = "PAGE_RESULT|" Then
            Call BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
                dictPages, _
                lngCurrentPage, _
                "result", _
                BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "result"))

            Call BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
                dictPages, _
                lngCurrentPage, _
                "critical", _
                BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "newCritical"))

            Call BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
                dictPages, _
                lngCurrentPage, _
                "structural", _
                BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "newStructural"))

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 12) = "PAGE_LAYOUT|" Then
            oldTradeCount = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "tradesOld")))
            newTradeCount = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "tradesNew")))

            Call BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
                dictPages, _
                lngCurrentPage, _
                "trades", _
                CStr(oldTradeCount) & ">" & CStr(newTradeCount))

            strFooterOld = BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "footerOld")
            strFooterNew = BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "footerNew")

            Call BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
                dictPages, _
                lngCurrentPage, _
                "footer", _
                strFooterOld & ">" & strFooterNew)

            If oldTradeCount <> newTradeCount Then
                lngTradeMismatchPages = lngTradeMismatchPages + 1
            End If

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 17) = "TARGET_SEPARATOR|" Then
            Call BreaksSummaryRegisterTarget( _
                strLine, _
                lngCurrentPage, _
                dictPages, _
                lngTargetStyleOnly, _
                lngTargetGeometry)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 23) = "TARGET_SEPARATOR_STYLE|" Then
            Call BreaksSummaryAddSample(sampleTarget, strLine, 4)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 26) = "TARGET_SEPARATOR_GEOMETRY|" Or _
               Left$(strLine, 27) = "TARGET_SEPARATOR_EXTRA_NEW|" Then

            Call BreaksSummaryAddSample(sampleTarget, strLine, 4)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 14) = "TEXT_POSITION|" Then
            If BreaksSummaryLikelyPairingNoise(strLine) Then
                lngMatchingNoise = lngMatchingNoise + 1
            Else
                lngLikelyTextShift = lngLikelyTextShift + 1
                Call BreaksSummaryRegisterShiftPattern( _
                    strLine, _
                    dictShiftPatterns)
                Call BreaksSummaryAddSample(sampleTextShift, strLine, 5)
            End If

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 10) = "TEXT_FONT|" Then
            Call BreaksSummaryRegisterFontPair(strLine, dictFontPairs)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 16) = "WHITESPACE_ONLY|" Then
            Call BreaksSummaryRegisterWhitespace(strLine, dictWhitespace)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 12) = "FOOTER_FONT|" Or _
               Left$(strLine, 13) = "FOOTER_SHIFT|" Then

            Call BreaksSummaryAddSample(sampleFooter, strLine, 4)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 18) = "PAGE_STREAM_BREAK|" Then
            If BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "type") = "OPERATOR_PROFILE" Then
                Call BreaksSummaryAddSample(sampleOperator, strLine, 2)
            End If

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 24) = "VECTOR_GEOMETRY_SUMMARY|" Then
            lngVectorGeometry = lngVectorGeometry + _
                CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "breaks")))

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 21) = "VECTOR_STYLE_SUMMARY|" Then
            lngVectorStyle = lngVectorStyle + _
                CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "breaks")))

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 15) = "VECTOR_GEOMETRY|" Or _
               Left$(strLine, 13) = "VECTOR_STYLE|" Then

            Call BreaksSummaryAddSample(sampleVector, strLine, 4)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 7) = "ACTION|" Then
            Call BreaksSummaryRegisterActionType(strLine, dictActionTypes)

        ElseIf Left$(strLine, 15) = "BREAKS_SUMMARY|" Then
            strRawStatus = BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "status")
            lngRawCritical = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "critical")))
            lngRawStructural = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "structural")))
            lngRawInfo = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(strLine, "informational")))
        End If
    Loop

    Close #lngFileIn
    lngFileIn = 0

    '-----------------------------------------------------------------------
    ' Convert raw event families into a small set of code-level decisions.
    '-----------------------------------------------------------------------
    For Each strKey In dictFontPairs.Keys
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictFixNow, _
            "FONT_ROLE|" & CStr(strKey) & _
            "|ACTION=Use the OLD font resource/size for this recurring text role.", _
            CLng(dictFontPairs(strKey)))
    Next strKey

    For Each strKey In dictWhitespace.Keys
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictFixNow, _
            "WHITESPACE|" & CStr(strKey) & _
            "|ACTION=Make Oracle-exporter whitespace match OLD; do not change upstream business formatting.", _
            CLng(dictWhitespace(strKey)))
    Next strKey

    If CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
        dictActionTypes, _
        "PAGE_FONT_RESOURCES")) > 0 Then

        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictFixNow, _
            "PAGE_FONT_RESOURCES|ACTION=Match OLD page font-resource roles/dictionaries.", _
            CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
                dictActionTypes, _
                "PAGE_FONT_RESOURCES")))
    End If

    If lngTargetStyleOnly > 0 Then
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictFixNow, _
            "TARGET_SEPARATOR_STYLE|ACTION=Keep X/Y geometry; reproduce OLD width/paint/source/color.", _
            lngTargetStyleOnly)
    End If

    If lngVectorStyle > 0 Then
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictFixNow, _
            "VECTOR_STYLE|ACTION=Reproduce OLD paint/source operator families; prioritize repeated rectangle/separator roles.", _
            lngVectorStyle)
    End If

    If sampleFooter.Count > 0 Then
        If CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
            dictActionTypes, _
            "FOOTER_FONT")) > 0 Then

            Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
                dictFixNow, _
                "FOOTER_FONT|ACTION=Use the OLD footer font resource and size.", _
                CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
                    dictActionTypes, _
                    "FOOTER_FONT")))
        End If

        If CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
            dictActionTypes, _
            "FOOTER_SHIFT")) > 0 Then

            Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
                dictVerify, _
                "FOOTER_SHIFT|ACTION=Verify on matched pagination before moving footer coordinates.", _
                CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
                    dictActionTypes, _
                    "FOOTER_SHIFT")))
        End If
    End If

    If lngTargetGeometry > 0 Then
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictVerify, _
            "TARGET_SEPARATOR_GEOMETRY|ACTION=Review representative detail before changing X/Y.", _
            lngTargetGeometry)
    End If

    For Each strKey In dictShiftPatterns.Keys
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictVerify, _
            "TEXT_SHIFT_PATTERN|" & CStr(strKey) & _
            "|ACTION=Apply only when the same delta repeats on logically matching fields.", _
            CLng(dictShiftPatterns(strKey)))
    Next strKey

    If CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
        dictActionTypes, _
        "OPERATOR_PROFILE")) > 0 Then

        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictVerify, _
            "OPERATOR_PROFILE|ACTION=Tune after fonts/vector roles are stable; use representative sample below.", _
            CLng(BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
                dictActionTypes, _
                "OPERATOR_PROFILE")))
    End If

    If lngTradeMismatchPages > 0 Then
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictIgnore, _
            "TRADE_COUNT/PAGINATION|" & CStr(lngTradeMismatchPages) & _
            " page(s) differ|ACTION=Treat downstream row/text/vector count differences as input-contaminated unless reproduced on matched input.", _
            lngTradeMismatchPages)
    End If

    If lngMatchingNoise > 0 Then
        Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
            dictIgnore, _
            "TEXT_PAIRING_NOISE|ACTION=Do not change generator coordinates from implausibly large repeated-text deltas.", _
            lngMatchingNoise)
    End If

    Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
        dictIgnore, _
        "EXPECTED_INPUT_VALUES|ACTION=Security description/WKN/business-value differences remain informational when OLD and NEW inputs differ.", _
        1)

    '-----------------------------------------------------------------------
    ' Write compact decision-oriented summary.
    '-----------------------------------------------------------------------
    lngFileOut = OpenNew(outputPath)

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "ORACLE_COMPATIBILITY_ACTIONABLE_SUMMARY_V4")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "OLD|" & oldDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "NEW|" & newDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "PURPOSE|Show this file first. It converts raw events into code-level decisions and includes representative detail samples.")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "IMPORTANT|Raw critical/structural numbers are event counts, NOT the number of unique code fixes.")

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "GLOBAL")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "RAW|status=" & strRawStatus & _
        "|criticalEvents=" & CStr(lngRawCritical) & _
        "|structuralEvents=" & CStr(lngRawStructural) & _
        "|informationalEvents=" & CStr(lngRawInfo) & _
        "|pagesCompared=" & CStr(lngPagesCompared))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "DECISIONS|fixNowFamilies=" & CStr(dictFixNow.Count) & _
        "|verifyFamilies=" & CStr(dictVerify.Count) & _
        "|ignoreFamilies=" & CStr(dictIgnore.Count))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "TEXT_POSITION|likelyRealEvents=" & CStr(lngLikelyTextShift) & _
        "|pairingNoiseEvents=" & CStr(lngMatchingNoise))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "VECTORS|geometryEvents=" & CStr(lngVectorGeometry) & _
        "|styleEvents=" & CStr(lngVectorStyle))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "TARGET_SEPARATORS|styleOnlyEvents=" & CStr(lngTargetStyleOnly) & _
        "|geometryOrCrossingEvents=" & CStr(lngTargetGeometry))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "INPUT_CONTAMINATION|tradeMismatchPages=" & CStr(lngTradeMismatchPages))

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "FIX_NOW_STRONG")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call BreaksSummaryWriteDictionary( _
        dictFixNow, _
        lngFileOut, _
        10, _
        "FIX_NOW")

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "VERIFY_BEFORE_CODE_CHANGE")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call BreaksSummaryWriteDictionary( _
        dictVerify, _
        lngFileOut, _
        8, _
        "VERIFY")

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "IGNORE_OR_DEFER")
