    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call BreaksSummaryWriteDictionary( _
        dictIgnore, _
        lngFileOut, _
        6, _
        "IGNORE")

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "PAGE_HEALTH")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")

    For lngPage = 1 To lngPagesCompared
        Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
            "PAGE|" & CStr(lngPage) & _
            "|result=" & BreaksSummaryGetPageValue( _
                dictPages, lngPage, "result", "?") & _
            "|trades=" & BreaksSummaryGetPageValue( _
                dictPages, lngPage, "trades", "?") & _
            "|expectedMax=" & CStr(BreaksOracleMaxTradesForPage(lngPage)) & _
            "|footer=" & BreaksSummaryGetPageValue( _
                dictPages, lngPage, "footer", "?") & _
            "|x468=" & BreaksSummaryGetPageValue( _
                dictPages, lngPage, "x468", "?") & _
            "|x639=" & BreaksSummaryGetPageValue( _
                dictPages, lngPage, "x639", "?"))
    Next lngPage

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "REPRESENTATIVE_DETAIL_SAMPLES")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")

    Call BreaksSummaryWriteSamples( _
        sampleOperator, _
        lngFileOut, _
        "OPERATOR_PROFILE")

    Call BreaksSummaryWriteSamples( _
        sampleFooter, _
        lngFileOut, _
        "FOOTER")

    Call BreaksSummaryWriteSamples( _
        sampleTarget, _
        lngFileOut, _
        "TARGET_SEPARATOR")

    Call BreaksSummaryWriteSamples( _
        sampleTextShift, _
        lngFileOut, _
        "TEXT_POSITION")

    Call BreaksSummaryWriteSamples( _
        sampleVector, _
        lngFileOut, _
        "VECTOR")

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "NEXT_STEP")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, "============================================================")

    If dictFixNow.Count > 0 Then
        Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
            "NEXT|Apply FIX_NOW_STRONG families first, rerun the audit, then reassess VERIFY families.")
    ElseIf dictVerify.Count > 0 Then
        Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
            "NEXT|No strong automatic fix remains. Use the representative samples above for the next targeted code change.")
    Else
        Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
            "NEXT|No actionable parser-sensitive family was detected in this summary.")
    End If

    If lngTradeMismatchPages > 0 Then
        Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
            "MATCHED_INPUT_TEST|Recommended for final coordinate/pagination proof because OLD and NEW trade counts differ on " & _
            CStr(lngTradeMismatchPages) & " page(s).")
    End If

    Call EmitLine(lngFileOut, _
        "SEND_ME|Normally this ACTIONABLE_SUMMARY file alone is enough. Full BREAKS_ONLY is only needed if I explicitly ask for one unresolved family.")

ExitSub:
    If lngFileIn > 0 Then Close #lngFileIn
    If lngFileOut > 0 Then Close #lngFileOut

    Set dictActionTypes = Nothing
    Set dictFixNow = Nothing
    Set dictFontPairs = Nothing
    Set dictIgnore = Nothing
    Set dictPages = Nothing
    Set dictShiftPatterns = Nothing
    Set dictVerify = Nothing
    Set dictWhitespace = Nothing

    Set sampleFooter = Nothing
    Set sampleOperator = Nothing
    Set sampleTarget = Nothing
    Set sampleTextShift = Nothing
    Set sampleVector = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "detailedBreaksPath;outputPath", _
        detailedBreaksPath, _
        outputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

Private Sub BreaksSummaryRegisterShiftPattern( _
    ByVal lineText As String, _
    ByVal dictionary As Object)

    Dim dblDeltaX As Double
    Dim dblDeltaY As Double
    Dim strDelta As String
    Dim strKey As String

    strDelta = BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "DELTA_NEW_MINUS_OLD")
    If Len(strDelta) = 0 Then Exit Sub

    dblDeltaX = Val(BreaksSummarySubField(strDelta, "x"))
    dblDeltaY = Val(BreaksSummarySubField(strDelta, "y"))

    strKey = _
        "shiftX=" & BreaksSummaryRoundedDelta(dblDeltaX) & _
        "|shiftY=" & BreaksSummaryRoundedDelta(dblDeltaY)

    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictionary, strKey)
End Sub

Private Function BreaksSummaryRoundedDelta(ByVal value As Double) As String
    BreaksSummaryRoundedDelta = _
        Replace(Format$(Round(value, 2), "0.00"), ",", ".")
End Function

Private Function BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue( _
    ByVal dictionary As Object, _
    ByVal keyName As String) As Long

    If dictionary Is Nothing Then Exit Function

    If dictionary.Exists(keyName) Then
        BreaksSummaryDictionaryValue = CLng(dictionary(keyName))
    End If
End Function

Private Sub BreaksSummaryAddSample( _
    ByRef samples As Collection, _
    ByVal lineText As String, _
    ByVal maxSamples As Long)

    If samples Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If samples.Count >= maxSamples Then Exit Sub

    samples.Add BreaksSummaryCompactSample(lineText)
End Sub

Private Function BreaksSummaryCompactSample(ByVal lineText As String) As String
    Const MAX_SAMPLE_LENGTH As Long = 360

    lineText = Replace(lineText, vbCr, " ")
    lineText = Replace(lineText, vbLf, " ")

    If Len(lineText) > MAX_SAMPLE_LENGTH Then
        lineText = Left$(lineText, MAX_SAMPLE_LENGTH) & "..."
    End If

    BreaksSummaryCompactSample = lineText
End Function

Private Sub BreaksSummaryWriteSamples( _
    ByVal samples As Collection, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal sampleType As String)

    Dim lngIndex As Long

    If samples Is Nothing Then
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "SAMPLE|" & sampleType & "|(none)")
        Exit Sub
    End If

    If samples.Count = 0 Then
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "SAMPLE|" & sampleType & "|(none)")
        Exit Sub
    End If

    For lngIndex = 1 To samples.Count
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "SAMPLE|" & sampleType & "|" & CStr(lngIndex) & _
            "|" & CStr(samples.Item(lngIndex)))
    Next lngIndex
End Sub

Private Sub BreaksSummaryRegisterTarget( _
    ByVal lineText As String, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal pageDictionary As Object, _
    ByRef styleOnlyCount As Long, _
    ByRef geometryCount As Long)

    Dim lngExtra As Long
    Dim lngGeometry As Long
    Dim lngNewCrossings As Long
    Dim lngOldCrossings As Long
    Dim lngStyle As Long
    Dim strClassification As String
    Dim strTargetX As String

    strTargetX = BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "x")

    If BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "result") = "PASS" Then
        strClassification = "PASS"
    Else
        lngGeometry = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "geometryBreaks")))
        lngExtra = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "extraNew")))
        lngStyle = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "styleBreaks")))
        lngOldCrossings = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "oldCrossings")))
        lngNewCrossings = CLng(Val(BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "newCrossings")))

        If lngGeometry = 0 And lngExtra = 0 And _
           lngStyle > 0 And lngOldCrossings = lngNewCrossings Then

            strClassification = "STYLE_ONLY"
            styleOnlyCount = styleOnlyCount + lngStyle
        Else
            strClassification = "GEOMETRY/CROSSING"
            geometryCount = geometryCount + 1
        End If
    End If

    If Abs(Val(strTargetX) - 468#) <= 0.01 Then
        Call BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
            pageDictionary, pageNo, "x468", strClassification)
    ElseIf Abs(Val(strTargetX) - 639#) <= 0.01 Then
        Call BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
            pageDictionary, pageNo, "x639", strClassification)
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub BreaksSummaryRegisterWhitespace( _
    ByVal lineText As String, _
    ByVal dictionary As Object)

    Dim strKey As String

    strKey = _
        "leading " & BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "leadingOld") & ">" & _
        BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "leadingNew") & _
        "; trailing " & BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "trailingOld") & ">" & _
        BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "trailingNew") & _
        "; length " & BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "lengthOld") & ">" & _
        BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "lengthNew")

    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictionary, strKey)
End Sub

Private Sub BreaksSummaryRegisterFontPair( _
    ByVal lineText As String, _
    ByVal dictionary As Object)

    Dim strKey As String

    strKey = _
        BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "OLD") & " > " & _
        BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "NEW")

    If Len(Replace(strKey, " > ", vbNullString)) > 0 Then
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictionary, strKey)
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub BreaksSummaryRegisterActionType( _
    ByVal lineText As String, _
    ByVal dictionary As Object)

    Dim arrParts() As String
    Dim lngCount As Long
    Dim strAction As String
    Dim strType As String

    arrParts = Split(lineText, "|")
    If UBound(arrParts) < 2 Then Exit Sub

    lngCount = CLng(Val(Replace(arrParts(1), "count=", vbNullString)))
    strAction = arrParts(2)
    strType = strAction

    If InStr(1, strType, "=", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
        strType = Left$(strType, InStr(1, strType, "=", vbBinaryCompare) - 1)
    End If

    If lngCount <= 0 Then lngCount = 1
    Call BreaksSummaryIncrementBy(dictionary, strType, lngCount)
End Sub

Private Function BreaksSummaryLikelyPairingNoise( _
    ByVal lineText As String) As Boolean

    Dim dblDeltaX As Double
    Dim dblDeltaY As Double
    Dim strDelta As String
    Dim strX As String
    Dim strY As String

    strDelta = BreaksSummaryField(lineText, "DELTA_NEW_MINUS_OLD")
    If Len(strDelta) = 0 Then Exit Function

    strX = BreaksSummarySubField(strDelta, "x")
    strY = BreaksSummarySubField(strDelta, "y")

    dblDeltaX = Val(strX)
    dblDeltaY = Val(strY)

    BreaksSummaryLikelyPairingNoise = _
        Abs(dblDeltaY) > 5# Or Abs(dblDeltaX) > 50#
End Function

Private Function BreaksSummarySubField( _
    ByVal value As String, _
    ByVal keyName As String) As String

    Dim arrParts() As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strPrefix As String

    strPrefix = keyName & "="
    arrParts = Split(value, ",")

    For lngIndex = LBound(arrParts) To UBound(arrParts)
        If Left$(arrParts(lngIndex), Len(strPrefix)) = strPrefix Then
            BreaksSummarySubField = Mid$(arrParts(lngIndex), Len(strPrefix) + 1)
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next lngIndex
End Function

Private Function BreaksSummaryField( _
    ByVal lineText As String, _
    ByVal keyName As String) As String

    Dim arrParts() As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strPrefix As String

    strPrefix = keyName & "="
    arrParts = Split(lineText, "|")

    For lngIndex = LBound(arrParts) To UBound(arrParts)
        If Left$(arrParts(lngIndex), Len(strPrefix)) = strPrefix Then
            BreaksSummaryField = Mid$(arrParts(lngIndex), Len(strPrefix) + 1)
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next lngIndex
End Function

Private Sub BreaksSummarySetPageValue( _
    ByVal dictionary As Object, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fieldName As String, _
    ByVal fieldValue As String)

    dictionary(CStr(pageNo) & "|" & fieldName) = fieldValue
End Sub

Private Function BreaksSummaryGetPageValue( _
    ByVal dictionary As Object, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fieldName As String, _
    ByVal defaultValue As String) As String

    Dim strKey As String

    strKey = CStr(pageNo) & "|" & fieldName

    If dictionary.Exists(strKey) Then
        BreaksSummaryGetPageValue = CStr(dictionary(strKey))
    Else
        BreaksSummaryGetPageValue = defaultValue
    End If
End Function

Private Sub BreaksSummaryIncrementBy( _
    ByVal dictionary As Object, _
    ByVal keyName As String, _
    ByVal incrementValue As Long)

    If dictionary.Exists(keyName) Then
        dictionary(keyName) = CLng(dictionary(keyName)) + incrementValue
    Else
        dictionary.Add keyName, incrementValue
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub BreaksSummaryWriteDictionary( _
    ByVal dictionary As Object, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal maxLines As Long, _
    ByVal prefix As String)

    Dim arrKeys() As String
    Dim arrValues() As Long
    Dim lngCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngInner As Long
    Dim lngSwapValue As Long
    Dim strKey As Variant
    Dim strSwapKey As String

    If dictionary Is Nothing Then
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, prefix & "|(none)")
        Exit Sub
    End If

    If dictionary.Count = 0 Then
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, prefix & "|(none)")
        Exit Sub
    End If

    lngCount = dictionary.Count
    ReDim arrKeys(0 To lngCount - 1)
    ReDim arrValues(0 To lngCount - 1)

    lngIndex = 0
    For Each strKey In dictionary.Keys
        arrKeys(lngIndex) = CStr(strKey)
        arrValues(lngIndex) = CLng(dictionary(strKey))
        lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
    Next strKey

    For lngIndex = 0 To lngCount - 2
        For lngInner = lngIndex + 1 To lngCount - 1
            If arrValues(lngInner) > arrValues(lngIndex) Then
                lngSwapValue = arrValues(lngIndex)
                arrValues(lngIndex) = arrValues(lngInner)
                arrValues(lngInner) = lngSwapValue

                strSwapKey = arrKeys(lngIndex)
                arrKeys(lngIndex) = arrKeys(lngInner)
                arrKeys(lngInner) = strSwapKey
            End If
        Next lngInner
    Next lngIndex

    For lngIndex = 0 To lngCount - 1
        If lngIndex >= maxLines Then
            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                prefix & "|... " & CStr(lngCount - maxLines) & _
                " additional grouped item(s) omitted")
            Exit For
        End If

        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            prefix & "|count=" & CStr(arrValues(lngIndex)) & _
            "|" & arrKeys(lngIndex))
    Next lngIndex
End Sub

Private Sub WriteBreaksOnlyReport( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal outputPath As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksOnlyReport"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim dictActions As Object
    Dim lngCommonPages As Long
    Dim lngCritical As Long
    Dim lngFileNumber As Long
    Dim lngInfo As Long
    Dim lngPage As Long
    Dim lngStructural As Long
    Dim strStatus As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    Set dictActions = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    lngFileNumber = OpenNew(outputPath)

    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "ORACLE_COMPATIBILITY_BREAKS_ONLY_V2")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "OLD|" & oldDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "NEW|" & newDoc.PdfPath)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
        "POLICY|Geometry, text position, fonts, vector style, page structure, pagination, and target separators are actionable.")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
        "POLICY|Different business values caused by different input files are INFORMATIONAL and do not create a parser-critical break.")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
        "POLICY|Whitespace-only text changes are reported separately. WKN or security-description value differences may therefore remain informational.")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
        "TOLERANCE|textPosition=" & NumTxt(BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE) & _
        "|lineMatch=" & NumTxt(BREAKS_LINE_MATCH_TOLERANCE) & _
        "|baselineCluster=" & NumTxt(BREAKS_BASELINE_TOLERANCE))

    If oldDoc.PdfVersion <> newDoc.PdfVersion Then
        lngCritical = lngCritical + 1
        Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
            "DOCUMENT_BREAK|type=PDF_VERSION|OLD=" & oldDoc.PdfVersion & _
            "|NEW=" & newDoc.PdfVersion & _
            "|FIX=Generate NEW with the OLD PDF version.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictActions, _
            "PDF_VERSION|old=" & oldDoc.PdfVersion & "|new=" & newDoc.PdfVersion)
    End If

    If oldDoc.PageCount <> newDoc.PageCount Then
        lngCritical = lngCritical + 1
        Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
            "DOCUMENT_BREAK|type=PAGE_COUNT|OLD=" & CStr(oldDoc.PageCount) & _
            "|NEW=" & CStr(newDoc.PageCount) & _
            "|FIX=Match Oracle pagination and trades-per-page.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(dictActions, _
            "PAGE_COUNT|old=" & CStr(oldDoc.PageCount) & "|new=" & CStr(newDoc.PageCount))
    End If

    If oldDoc.XrefKind <> newDoc.XrefKind Then
        lngInfo = lngInfo + 1
        Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
            "DOCUMENT_INFO|type=XREF|OLD=" & oldDoc.XrefKind & _
            "|NEW=" & newDoc.XrefKind)
    End If

    lngCommonPages = oldDoc.PageCount
    If newDoc.PageCount < lngCommonPages Then
        lngCommonPages = newDoc.PageCount
    End If

    For lngPage = 1 To lngCommonPages
        Call WriteBreaksPageReport( _
            oldDoc, _
            newDoc, _
            lngPage, _
            lngFileNumber, _
            dictActions, _
            lngCritical, _
            lngStructural, _
            lngInfo)
    Next lngPage

    If lngCritical = 0 And lngStructural = 0 Then
        strStatus = "READY_FOR_CLIENT_PARSER_TEST"
    ElseIf lngCritical = 0 Then
        strStatus = "NO_CRITICAL_BREAKS_STRUCTURAL_DIFFERENCES_REMAIN"
    Else
        strStatus = "ACTION_REQUIRED"
    End If

    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "ACTION_SUMMARY")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, "============================================================")
    Call WriteBreaksActionSummary(dictActions, lngFileNumber)

    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
        "BREAKS_SUMMARY|status=" & strStatus & _
        "|critical=" & CStr(lngCritical) & _
        "|structural=" & CStr(lngStructural) & _
        "|informational=" & CStr(lngInfo) & _
        "|pagesCompared=" & CStr(lngCommonPages))
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
        "NEXT_ACTION|Apply CRITICAL fixes first, rerun the audit, then reduce STRUCTURAL differences. Ignore input-value differences unless the client parser proves they are relevant.")
    Call EmitLine(lngFileNumber, _
        "LIMITATION|The report compares PDF structure and extracted operators; it cannot execute the proprietary client parser.")

ExitSub:
    If lngFileNumber > 0 Then
        Close #lngFileNumber
    End If
    Set dictActions = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "outputPath", _
        outputPath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, fileNumber, actionDictionary, counters
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes one concise page section including pagination, fingerprint, text, vector, and target checks.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksOracleMaxTradesForPage( _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As Long

    If pageNo = 1 Then
        BreaksOracleMaxTradesForPage = ORACLE_FIRST_PAGE_MAX_TRADES
    Else
        BreaksOracleMaxTradesForPage = ORACLE_CONTINUATION_PAGE_MAX_TRADES
    End If
End Function

Private Sub WriteBreaksPageReport( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal actionDictionary As Object, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long, _
    ByRef structuralCount As Long, _
    ByRef infoCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksPageReport"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim blnNewFooter As Boolean
    Dim blnOldFooter As Boolean
    Dim dblNewBottomHeaderY As Double
    Dim dblNewFooterX As Double
    Dim dblNewFooterY As Double
    Dim dblNewTopHeaderY As Double
    Dim dblOldBottomHeaderY As Double
    Dim dblOldFooterX As Double
    Dim dblOldFooterY As Double
    Dim dblOldTopHeaderY As Double
    Dim lngChanged As Long
    Dim lngNewOnly As Long
    Dim lngNewTradeCount As Long
    Dim lngNewUnpaired As Long
    Dim lngOldOnly As Long
    Dim lngOldTradeCount As Long
    Dim lngOldUnpaired As Long
    Dim lngPageCriticalBefore As Long
    Dim lngPageStructuralBefore As Long
    Dim strNewFooterFont As String
    Dim strNewFooterText As String
    Dim strOldFooterFont As String
    Dim strOldFooterText As String
    Dim strResult As String
    Dim dictNew As Object
    Dim dictOld As Object

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngPageCriticalBefore = criticalCount
    lngPageStructuralBefore = structuralCount

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, vbNullString)
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "PAGE|" & CStr(pageNo))
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "============================================================")
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "FINGERPRINT_OLD|" & BuildBreaksPageFingerprint(oldDoc, pageNo))
    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "FINGERPRINT_NEW|" & BuildBreaksPageFingerprint(newDoc, pageNo))

    If NormalizePdfValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) <> _
       NormalizePdfValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) Then

        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=MEDIABOX|OLD=" & NormalizePdfValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) & _
            "|NEW=" & NormalizePdfValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).MediaBox) & _
            "|FIX=Use the OLD MediaBox.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "PAGE_MEDIABOX")
    End If

    If EffectiveCropBox(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo)) <> EffectiveCropBox(newDoc.Pages(pageNo)) Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=CROPBOX|OLD=" & EffectiveCropBox(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo)) & _
            "|NEW=" & EffectiveCropBox(newDoc.Pages(pageNo)) & _
            "|FIX=Use the OLD effective CropBox.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "PAGE_CROPBOX")
    End If

    If NormalizeRotateValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue) <> _
       NormalizeRotateValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue) Then

        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=ROTATE|OLD=" & NormalizeRotateValue(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue) & _
            "|NEW=" & NormalizeRotateValue(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).RotateValue) & _
            "|FIX=Use the OLD page rotation.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "PAGE_ROTATE")
    End If

    dblOldTopHeaderY = FindBreaksTextBaseline(oldDoc, pageNo, "DEPOTNR")
    dblNewTopHeaderY = FindBreaksTextBaseline(newDoc, pageNo, "DEPOTNR")
    dblOldBottomHeaderY = FindBreaksTextBaseline(oldDoc, pageNo, "TRANSAKTIONSNR")
    dblNewBottomHeaderY = FindBreaksTextBaseline(newDoc, pageNo, "TRANSAKTIONSNR")

    lngOldTradeCount = CountBreaksTradePairs(oldDoc, pageNo, dblOldBottomHeaderY, lngOldUnpaired)
    lngNewTradeCount = CountBreaksTradePairs(newDoc, pageNo, dblNewBottomHeaderY, lngNewUnpaired)

    blnOldFooter = FindBreaksFooter( _
        oldDoc, _
        pageNo, _
        strOldFooterText, _
        dblOldFooterX, _
        dblOldFooterY, _
        strOldFooterFont)

    blnNewFooter = FindBreaksFooter( _
        newDoc, _
        pageNo, _
        strNewFooterText, _
        dblNewFooterX, _
        dblNewFooterY, _
        strNewFooterFont)

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
        "PAGE_LAYOUT|topHeaderYOld=" & NumTxt(dblOldTopHeaderY) & _
        "|topHeaderYNew=" & NumTxt(dblNewTopHeaderY) & _
        "|bottomHeaderYOld=" & NumTxt(dblOldBottomHeaderY) & _
        "|bottomHeaderYNew=" & NumTxt(dblNewBottomHeaderY) & _
        "|tradesOld=" & CStr(lngOldTradeCount) & _
        "|tradesNew=" & CStr(lngNewTradeCount) & _
        "|expectedMax=" & CStr(BreaksOracleMaxTradesForPage(pageNo)) & _
        "|unpairedRowsOld=" & CStr(lngOldUnpaired) & _
        "|unpairedRowsNew=" & CStr(lngNewUnpaired) & _
        "|footerOld=" & BoolText(blnOldFooter) & _
        "|footerNew=" & BoolText(blnNewFooter))

    Call WriteBreaksPositionDelta( _
        fileNumber, _
        actionDictionary, _
        criticalCount, _
        pageNo, _
        "HEADER_TOP_BASELINE", _
        dblOldTopHeaderY, _
        dblNewTopHeaderY)

    Call WriteBreaksPositionDelta( _
        fileNumber, _
        actionDictionary, _
        criticalCount, _
        pageNo, _
        "HEADER_BOTTOM_BASELINE", _
        dblOldBottomHeaderY, _
        dblNewBottomHeaderY)

    If lngOldTradeCount <> lngNewTradeCount Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=TRADE_COUNT|OLD=" & CStr(lngOldTradeCount) & _
            "|NEW=" & CStr(lngNewTradeCount) & _
            "|FIX=Match Oracle pagination and two-row trade grouping.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
            "TRADE_COUNT|old=" & CStr(lngOldTradeCount) & "|new=" & CStr(lngNewTradeCount))
    End If

    If lngNewTradeCount > BreaksOracleMaxTradesForPage(pageNo) Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=MAX_TRADES|NEW=" & CStr(lngNewTradeCount) & _
            "|EXPECTED_MAX=" & CStr(BreaksOracleMaxTradesForPage(pageNo)) & _
            "|PAGE_TYPE=" & IIf(pageNo = 1, "FIRST", "CONTINUATION") & _
            "|FIX=Paginate OLD Oracle style: first page max 11, continuation page max 12.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "MAX_TRADES_PER_PAGE")
    End If

    If lngOldUnpaired <> lngNewUnpaired Or lngNewUnpaired > 0 Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=TRADE_ROW_PAIRING|OLD_UNPAIRED=" & CStr(lngOldUnpaired) & _
            "|NEW_UNPAIRED=" & CStr(lngNewUnpaired) & _
            "|FIX=Keep each transaction as one top row plus one bottom row.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "TRADE_ROW_PAIRING")
    End If

    If blnOldFooter <> blnNewFooter Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=FOOTER_PRESENCE|OLD=" & BoolText(blnOldFooter) & _
            "|NEW=" & BoolText(blnNewFooter) & _
            "|FIX=Match Oracle footer placement by page.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "FOOTER_PRESENCE")
    ElseIf blnOldFooter And blnNewFooter Then
        If Abs(dblOldFooterX - dblNewFooterX) > BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE Or _
           Abs(dblOldFooterY - dblNewFooterY) > BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE Then

            criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                "TEXT_POSITION|role=FOOTER|OLD=x=" & NumTxt(dblOldFooterX) & _
                ",y=" & NumTxt(dblOldFooterY) & _
                "|NEW=x=" & NumTxt(dblNewFooterX) & _
                ",y=" & NumTxt(dblNewFooterY) & _
                "|DELTA_NEW_MINUS_OLD=x=" & NumTxt(dblNewFooterX - dblOldFooterX) & _
                ",y=" & NumTxt(dblNewFooterY - dblOldFooterY) & _
                "|FIX=" & BreaksMoveFix(dblOldFooterX - dblNewFooterX, dblOldFooterY - dblNewFooterY))
            Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                "FOOTER_SHIFT|shiftX=" & NumTxt(dblOldFooterX - dblNewFooterX) & _
                "|shiftY=" & NumTxt(dblOldFooterY - dblNewFooterY))
        End If

        If strOldFooterFont <> strNewFooterFont Then
            structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                "TEXT_FONT|role=FOOTER|OLD=" & strOldFooterFont & _
                "|NEW=" & strNewFooterFont & _
                "|FIX=Use the OLD footer font resource and size.")
            Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                "FOOTER_FONT|old=" & strOldFooterFont & "|new=" & strNewFooterFont)
        End If
    End If

    If BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts) <> _
       BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts) Then

        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_STREAM_BREAK|type=FONT_RESOURCES" & _
            "|OLD=" & BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts) & _
            "|NEW=" & BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts) & _
            "|FIX=Match the OLD semantic font set; resource aliases such as F1/F2 are ignored here.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "PAGE_FONT_RESOURCES")
    ElseIf oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts <> newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts Then
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "FONT_RESOURCE_ALIAS_ONLY|result=PASS_SEMANTIC" & _
            "|ACTION=No text-font change required; OLD/NEW resource aliases or serialization differ.")
    End If

    If oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters <> newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_STREAM_BREAK|type=CONTENT_FILTERS|OLD=" & oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters & _
            "|NEW=" & newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentFilters & _
            "|FIX=Match the OLD page-stream filters.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "PAGE_CONTENT_FILTERS")
    End If

    Set dictOld = BuildOperatorProfileDictionary(oldDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentText)
    Set dictNew = BuildOperatorProfileDictionary(newDoc.Pages(pageNo).ContentText)
    Call DictionaryDifferenceCounts(dictOld, dictNew, lngOldOnly, lngNewOnly, lngChanged)

    If lngOldOnly > 0 Or lngNewOnly > 0 Or lngChanged > 0 Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_STREAM_BREAK|type=OPERATOR_PROFILE|oldOnly=" & CStr(lngOldOnly) & _
            "|newOnly=" & CStr(lngNewOnly) & _
            "|countChanged=" & CStr(lngChanged) & _
            "|FIX=Review only after geometry/text-position breaks are removed.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, "OPERATOR_PROFILE")
    End If
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing

    Call WriteBreaksTargetSeparator( _
        oldDoc, _
        newDoc, _
        pageNo, _
        TARGET_X_FIRST, _
        fileNumber, _
        actionDictionary, _
        criticalCount)

    Call WriteBreaksTargetSeparator( _
        oldDoc, _
        newDoc, _
        pageNo, _
        TARGET_X_SECOND, _
        fileNumber, _
        actionDictionary, _
        criticalCount)

    Call WriteBreaksTextDifferences( _
        oldDoc, _
        newDoc, _
        pageNo, _
        fileNumber, _
        actionDictionary, _
        criticalCount, _
        structuralCount, _
        infoCount)

    Call WriteBreaksVectorDifferences( _
        oldDoc, _
        newDoc, _
        pageNo, _
        fileNumber, _
        actionDictionary, _
        criticalCount, _
        structuralCount)

    If criticalCount = lngPageCriticalBefore And _
       structuralCount = lngPageStructuralBefore Then

        strResult = "PASS"
    ElseIf criticalCount = lngPageCriticalBefore Then
        strResult = "WARN"
    Else
        strResult = "FAIL"
    End If

    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
        "PAGE_RESULT|page=" & CStr(pageNo) & _
        "|result=" & strResult & _
        "|newCritical=" & CStr(criticalCount - lngPageCriticalBefore) & _
        "|newStructural=" & CStr(structuralCount - lngPageStructuralBefore))

ExitSub:
    Set dictOld = Nothing
    Set dictNew = Nothing
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Builds a compact page fingerprint for fast multi-page consistency checks.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BuildBreaksPageFingerprint( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BuildBreaksPageFingerprint"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim blnFooter As Boolean
    Dim dblBottomHeaderY As Double
    Dim dblFooterX As Double
    Dim dblFooterY As Double
    Dim lngFullWidthH As Long
    Dim lngH As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngTrades As Long
    Dim lngUnpaired As Long
    Dim lngV As Long
    Dim strFooterFont As String
    Dim strFooterText As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Abs(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1 - pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
                lngH = lngH + 1
                If Abs(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X2 - pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X1) >= BREAKS_FULL_WIDTH_MIN Then
                    lngFullWidthH = lngFullWidthH + 1
                End If
            ElseIf Abs(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X1 - pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
                lngV = lngV + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    dblBottomHeaderY = FindBreaksTextBaseline(pdfDoc, pageNo, "TRANSAKTIONSNR")
    lngTrades = CountBreaksTradePairs(pdfDoc, pageNo, dblBottomHeaderY, lngUnpaired)
    blnFooter = FindBreaksFooter( _
        pdfDoc, _
        pageNo, _
        strFooterText, _
        dblFooterX, _
        dblFooterY, _
        strFooterFont)

    BuildBreaksPageFingerprint = _
        "lines=" & CStr(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).LineCount) & _
        "|H=" & CStr(lngH) & _
        "|V=" & CStr(lngV) & _
        "|fullWidthH=" & CStr(lngFullWidthH) & _
        "|textRuns=" & CStr(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount) & _
        "|baselines=" & CStr(CountBreaksTextBaselines(pdfDoc, pageNo)) & _
        "|images=" & CStr(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).ImageCount) & _
        "|target468=" & CStr(CountBreaksTargetOccurrences(pdfDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_FIRST)) & _
        "|target639=" & CStr(CountBreaksTargetOccurrences(pdfDoc, pageNo, TARGET_X_SECOND)) & _
        "|trades=" & CStr(lngTrades) & _
        "|unpairedRows=" & CStr(lngUnpaired) & _
        "|footer=" & BoolText(blnFooter)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BuildBreaksPageFingerprint = "error"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    fileNumber, actionDictionary, criticalCount, pageNo, roleName, oldY, newY
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Reports a baseline Y shift and gives the exact shift required on NEW.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteBreaksPositionDelta( _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal actionDictionary As Object, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal roleName As String, _
    ByVal oldY As Double, _
    ByVal newY As Double)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksPositionDelta"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If oldY = 0 And newY = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    If oldY = 0 Or newY = 0 Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=" & roleName & "|page=" & CStr(pageNo) & _
            "|OLD=" & NumTxt(oldY) & "|NEW=" & NumTxt(newY) & _
            "|FIX=Restore the missing Oracle header baseline.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, roleName & "|missing")
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    If Abs(newY - oldY) > BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "PAGE_BREAK|type=" & roleName & "|page=" & CStr(pageNo) & _
            "|OLD=" & NumTxt(oldY) & "|NEW=" & NumTxt(newY) & _
            "|DELTA_NEW_MINUS_OLD=" & NumTxt(newY - oldY) & _
            "|FIX=" & BreaksMoveFix(0, oldY - newY))
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
            roleName & "|shiftY=" & NumTxt(oldY - newY))
    End If

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;roleName", _
        pageNo, _
        roleName

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, targetX, fileNumber, actionDictionary, criticalCount
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Gives a concise exact audit for the client-sensitive separators at x=468 and x=639.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteBreaksTargetSeparator( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal actionDictionary As Object, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksTargetSeparator"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim blnNewUsed() As Boolean
    Dim blnFound As Boolean
    Dim dblBestDistance As Double
    Dim dblDistance As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngBestNewIndex As Long
    Dim lngGeometryBreaks As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngNewCrossings As Long
    Dim lngNewIndex As Long
    Dim lngNewOccurrences As Long
    Dim lngOldCrossings As Long
    Dim lngOldOccurrences As Long
    Dim lngStyleBreaks As Long
    Dim lngUnmatchedNew As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngOldOccurrences = CountBreaksTargetOccurrences(oldDoc, pageNo, targetX)
    lngNewOccurrences = CountBreaksTargetOccurrences(newDoc, pageNo, targetX)
    lngOldCrossings = CountTargetTextCrossings(oldDoc, pageNo, targetX, 0, False)
    lngNewCrossings = CountTargetTextCrossings(newDoc, pageNo, targetX, 0, False)

    If newDoc.LineCount > 0 Then
        ReDim blnNewUsed(0 To newDoc.LineCount - 1)
    Else
        ReDim blnNewUsed(0 To 0)
    End If

    For lngIndex = 0 To oldDoc.LineCount - 1
        If oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If IsVerticalAtX(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex), targetX) Then
                dblBestDistance = 1E+30
                lngBestNewIndex = -1

                For lngNewIndex = 0 To newDoc.LineCount - 1
                    If newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
                        If Not blnNewUsed(lngNewIndex) Then
                            If IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex), targetX) Then
                                dblDistance = _
                                    Abs(MinDouble(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1, oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2) - _
                                        MinDouble(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex).Y1, newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex).Y2)) + _
                                    Abs(MaxDouble(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1, oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2) - _
                                        MaxDouble(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex).Y1, newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex).Y2))

                                If dblDistance < dblBestDistance Then
                                    dblBestDistance = dblDistance
                                    lngBestNewIndex = lngNewIndex
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                Next lngNewIndex

                blnFound = lngBestNewIndex >= 0 And _
                    dblBestDistance <= TARGET_TOLERANCE * 2#

                If blnFound Then
                    blnNewUsed(lngBestNewIndex) = True

                    If Not BreaksLineStyleMatches( _
                        oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex), _
                        newDoc.Lines(lngBestNewIndex)) Then

                        lngStyleBreaks = lngStyleBreaks + 1
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "TARGET_SEPARATOR_STYLE" & _
                            "|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & _
                            "|OLD=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|" & BreaksLineStyle(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|NEW=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(newDoc.Lines(lngBestNewIndex)) & _
                            "|" & BreaksLineStyle(newDoc.Lines(lngBestNewIndex)) & _
                            "|FIX=Keep geometry; reproduce OLD width/paint/source/color.")
                    End If
                Else
                    lngGeometryBreaks = lngGeometryBreaks + 1
                    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                        "TARGET_SEPARATOR_GEOMETRY" & _
                        "|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & _
                        "|OLD=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                        "|NEW=(no matching segment)" & _
                        "|FIX=Restore the OLD target-separator y-range.")
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    For lngNewIndex = 0 To newDoc.LineCount - 1
        If newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex), targetX) Then
                If Not blnNewUsed(lngNewIndex) Then
                    lngUnmatchedNew = lngUnmatchedNew + 1
                    Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                        "TARGET_SEPARATOR_EXTRA_NEW" & _
                        "|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & _
                        "|NEW=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex)) & _
                        "|" & BreaksLineStyle(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex)) & _
                        "|FIX=Remove/reconcile the extra NEW target-separator segment.")
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngNewIndex

    If lngOldOccurrences = lngNewOccurrences And _
       lngGeometryBreaks = 0 And _
       lngUnmatchedNew = 0 And _
       lngStyleBreaks = 0 And _
       lngOldCrossings = lngNewCrossings Then

        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "TARGET_SEPARATOR|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & _
            "|result=PASS" & _
            "|occurrences=" & CStr(lngOldOccurrences) & _
            "|crossings=" & CStr(lngOldCrossings))
    Else
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "TARGET_SEPARATOR|x=" & NumTxt(targetX) & _
            "|result=FAIL" & _
            "|oldOccurrences=" & CStr(lngOldOccurrences) & _
            "|newOccurrences=" & CStr(lngNewOccurrences) & _
            "|geometryBreaks=" & CStr(lngGeometryBreaks) & _
            "|extraNew=" & CStr(lngUnmatchedNew) & _
            "|styleBreaks=" & CStr(lngStyleBreaks) & _
            "|oldCrossings=" & CStr(lngOldCrossings) & _
            "|newCrossings=" & CStr(lngNewCrossings) & _
            "|FIX=Match OLD target separator geometry/style exactly.")
        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
            "TARGET_SEPARATOR_X_" & Replace(NumTxt(targetX), ".", "_"))
    End If

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, fileNumber, actionDictionary, counters
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Pairs text runs and separates true position/font breaks from expected input-value and whitespace differences.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteBreaksTextDifferences( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal actionDictionary As Object, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long, _
    ByRef structuralCount As Long, _
    ByRef infoCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksTextDifferences"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim blnNewUsed() As Boolean
    Dim dblShiftX As Double
    Dim dblShiftY As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngNewIndex As Long
    Dim lngWritten As Long
    Dim strNewFontSemantic As String
    Dim strNewText As String
    Dim strOldFontSemantic As String
    Dim strOldText As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If newDoc.TextCount > 0 Then
        ReDim blnNewUsed(0 To newDoc.TextCount - 1)
    Else
        ReDim blnNewUsed(0 To 0)
    End If

    For lngIndex = 0 To oldDoc.TextCount - 1
        If oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            lngNewIndex = FindBreaksExactTextMatch( _
                oldDoc, _
                newDoc, _
                lngIndex, _
                pageNo, _
                blnNewUsed)

            If lngNewIndex < 0 Then
                lngNewIndex = FindBreaksGeometryTextMatch( _
                    oldDoc, _
                    newDoc, _
                    lngIndex, _
                    pageNo, _
                    blnNewUsed)
            End If

            If lngNewIndex >= 0 Then
                blnNewUsed(lngNewIndex) = True
                dblShiftX = oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X - newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).X
                dblShiftY = oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y - newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).Y

                If Abs(dblShiftX) > BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE Or _
                   Abs(dblShiftY) > BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE Then

                    criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "TEXT_POSITION|text=" & BreaksShortText(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                            "|OLD=x=" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) & _
                            ",y=" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) & _
                            "|NEW=x=" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).X) & _
                            ",y=" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).Y) & _
                            "|DELTA_NEW_MINUS_OLD=x=" & NumTxt(-dblShiftX) & _
                            ",y=" & NumTxt(-dblShiftY) & _
                            "|FIX=" & BreaksMoveFix(dblShiftX, dblShiftY))
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                        "TEXT_SHIFT|text=" & BreaksShortText(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                        "|shiftX=" & NumTxt(dblShiftX) & _
                        "|shiftY=" & NumTxt(dblShiftY))
                End If

                strOldFontSemantic = BreaksTextFontSemanticSignature( _
                    oldDoc, _
                    pageNo, _
                    oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontName)

                strNewFontSemantic = BreaksTextFontSemanticSignature( _
                    newDoc, _
                    pageNo, _
                    newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).FontName)

                If strOldFontSemantic <> strNewFontSemantic Or _
                   Abs(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontSize - newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).FontSize) > 0.01 Then

                    structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "TEXT_FONT|text=" & BreaksShortText(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                            "|OLD=" & strOldFontSemantic & "@" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontSize) & _
                            "|NEW=" & strNewFontSemantic & "@" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).FontSize) & _
                            "|OLD_ALIAS=" & oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontName & _
                            "|NEW_ALIAS=" & newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).FontName & _
                            "|FIX=Match the OLD semantic font and size; do not change font only because F1/F2 aliases differ.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                        "TEXT_FONT|old=" & strOldFontSemantic & "@" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontSize) & _
                        "|new=" & strNewFontSemantic & "@" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).FontSize))
                ElseIf oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontName <> newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).FontName Then
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "TEXT_FONT_ALIAS_ONLY|text=" & BreaksShortText(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                            "|OLD_ALIAS=" & oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontName & _
                            "|NEW_ALIAS=" & newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).FontName & _
                            "|semantic=" & strOldFontSemantic & _
                            "|ACTION=No text-font code change required from this alias difference.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                End If

                strOldText = oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue
                strNewText = newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).TextValue

                If StrComp(strOldText, strNewText, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
                    If NormalizeBreaksWhitespace(strOldText) = NormalizeBreaksWhitespace(strNewText) Then
                        infoCount = infoCount + 1
                        If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                                "WHITESPACE_ONLY" & _
                                "|OLD_VISIBLE=" & BreaksVisibleWhitespace(strOldText) & _
                                "|NEW_VISIBLE=" & BreaksVisibleWhitespace(strNewText) & _
                                "|leadingOld=" & CStr(BreaksLeadingSpaceCount(strOldText)) & _
                                "|leadingNew=" & CStr(BreaksLeadingSpaceCount(strNewText)) & _
                                "|trailingOld=" & CStr(BreaksTrailingSpaceCount(strOldText)) & _
                                "|trailingNew=" & CStr(BreaksTrailingSpaceCount(strNewText)) & _
                                "|lengthOld=" & CStr(Len(strOldText)) & _
                                "|lengthNew=" & CStr(Len(strNewText)) & _
                                "|ACTION=Whitespace differs. Use the counts above before changing text coordinates.")
                            lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                        End If
                    ElseIf IsBreaksFixedTextRole(oldDoc, pageNo, lngIndex) Or _
                           IsBreaksFixedTextRole(newDoc, pageNo, lngNewIndex) Then

                        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
                        If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                                "FIXED_TEXT_CONTENT|OLD=" & BreaksShortText(strOldText) & _
                                "|NEW=" & BreaksShortText(strNewText) & _
                                "|FIX=Use the OLD fixed Oracle label/footer text.")
                            lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                        End If
                        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                            "FIXED_TEXT_CONTENT|old=" & BreaksShortText(strOldText) & _
                            "|new=" & BreaksShortText(strNewText))
                    ElseIf BREAKS_IGNORE_INPUT_CONTENT_DIFFERENCES Then
                        infoCount = infoCount + 1
                        If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                                "EXPECTED_INPUT_CONTENT|OLD=" & BreaksShortText(strOldText) & _
                                "|NEW=" & BreaksShortText(strNewText) & _
                                "|OLD_XY=" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) & "," & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) & _
                                "|NEW_XY=" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).X) & "," & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngNewIndex).Y) & _
                                "|ACTION=Ignored as business-value difference; position/font are audited separately.")
                            lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                        End If
                    Else
                        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                If IsBreaksFixedTextRole(oldDoc, pageNo, lngIndex) Then
                    criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "FIXED_TEXT_ONLY_OLD|text=" & BreaksShortText(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                            "|x=" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) & _
                            "|y=" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) & _
                            "|FIX=Restore this fixed Oracle label/footer text.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                        "FIXED_TEXT_MISSING_NEW|text=" & BreaksShortText(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue))
                Else
                    infoCount = infoCount + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "EXPECTED_INPUT_ONLY_OLD|text=" & BreaksShortText(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                            "|x=" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) & _
                            "|y=" & NumTxt(oldDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) & _
                            "|ACTION=Ignored as missing business value.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    For lngIndex = 0 To newDoc.TextCount - 1
        If newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Not blnNewUsed(lngIndex) Then
                If IsBreaksFixedTextRole(newDoc, pageNo, lngIndex) Then
                    criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "FIXED_TEXT_ONLY_NEW|text=" & BreaksShortText(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                            "|x=" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) & _
                            "|y=" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) & _
                            "|FIX=Remove or reconcile this extra fixed label with OLD.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                        "FIXED_TEXT_EXTRA_NEW|text=" & BreaksShortText(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue))
                Else
                    infoCount = infoCount + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "EXPECTED_INPUT_ONLY_NEW|text=" & BreaksShortText(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) & _
                            "|x=" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) & _
                            "|y=" & NumTxt(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) & _
                            "|ACTION=Ignored as additional business value.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, oldIndex, pageNo, newUsed
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Finds the nearest unused NEW run with the same whitespace-normalized text.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindBreaksExactTextMatch( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal oldIndex As Long, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByRef newUsed() As Boolean) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindBreaksExactTextMatch"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblBestScore As Double
    Dim dblScore As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strOldNormalized As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    FindBreaksExactTextMatch = -1
    dblBestScore = 1E+30
    strOldNormalized = NormalizeBreaksWhitespace(oldDoc.Texts(oldIndex).TextValue)

    For lngIndex = 0 To newDoc.TextCount - 1
        If newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Not newUsed(lngIndex) Then
                If StrComp( _
                    strOldNormalized, _
                    NormalizeBreaksWhitespace(newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue), _
                    vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then

                    dblScore = _
                        Abs(oldDoc.Texts(oldIndex).X - newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X) + _
                        (Abs(oldDoc.Texts(oldIndex).Y - newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y) * 5#) + _
                        (Abs(oldDoc.Texts(oldIndex).FontSize - newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontSize) * 2#)

                    If dblScore < dblBestScore Then
                        dblBestScore = dblScore
                        FindBreaksExactTextMatch = lngIndex
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FindBreaksExactTextMatch = -1

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldIndex;pageNo", _
        oldIndex, _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, oldIndex, pageNo, newUsed
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Pairs different business values only when their anchors occupy the same logical cell.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindBreaksGeometryTextMatch( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal oldIndex As Long, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByRef newUsed() As Boolean) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindBreaksGeometryTextMatch"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblBestScore As Double
    Dim dblDx As Double
    Dim dblDy As Double
    Dim dblScore As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    FindBreaksGeometryTextMatch = -1
    dblBestScore = 1E+30

    For lngIndex = 0 To newDoc.TextCount - 1
        If newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Not newUsed(lngIndex) Then
                dblDx = Abs(oldDoc.Texts(oldIndex).X - newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X)
                dblDy = Abs(oldDoc.Texts(oldIndex).Y - newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y)

                If dblDx <= BREAKS_GEOMETRY_TEXT_X_TOLERANCE And _
                   dblDy <= BREAKS_GEOMETRY_TEXT_Y_TOLERANCE And _
                   Abs(oldDoc.Texts(oldIndex).FontSize - newDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontSize) <= 2# Then

                    dblScore = dblDx + (dblDy * 5#)
                    If dblScore < dblBestScore Then
                        dblBestScore = dblScore
                        FindBreaksGeometryTextMatch = lngIndex
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FindBreaksGeometryTextMatch = -1

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldIndex;pageNo", _
        oldIndex, _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, pageNo, fileNumber, actionDictionary, counters
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Pairs non-target vector segments by geometry and reports style or coordinate changes.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteBreaksVectorDifferences( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal actionDictionary As Object, _
    ByRef criticalCount As Long, _
    ByRef structuralCount As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksVectorDifferences"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim blnNewUsed() As Boolean
    Dim dblDistance As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngGeometryBreaks As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngNewIndex As Long
    Dim lngStyleBreaks As Long
    Dim lngWritten As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If newDoc.LineCount > 0 Then
        ReDim blnNewUsed(0 To newDoc.LineCount - 1)
    Else
        ReDim blnNewUsed(0 To 0)
    End If

    For lngIndex = 0 To oldDoc.LineCount - 1
        If oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Not IsVerticalAtX(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_FIRST) And _
               Not IsVerticalAtX(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_SECOND) Then

                lngNewIndex = FindBreaksNearestLine( _
                    oldDoc, _
                    newDoc, _
                    lngIndex, _
                    pageNo, _
                    blnNewUsed, _
                    dblDistance)

                If lngNewIndex >= 0 And dblDistance <= BREAKS_LINE_MATCH_TOLERANCE Then
                    blnNewUsed(lngNewIndex) = True

                    If Not BreaksLineStyleMatches(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex), newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex)) Then
                        lngStyleBreaks = lngStyleBreaks + 1
                        If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                            Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                                "VECTOR_STYLE|geometry=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                                "|OLD=" & BreaksLineStyle(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                                "|NEW=" & BreaksLineStyle(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex)) & _
                                "|FIX=Use OLD width, paint/source operator, and stroke color.")
                            lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                        End If
                        Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                            "VECTOR_STYLE|old=" & BreaksLineStyle(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|new=" & BreaksLineStyle(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex)))
                    End If

                ElseIf lngNewIndex >= 0 And dblDistance <= BREAKS_LINE_NEAR_TOLERANCE Then
                    blnNewUsed(lngNewIndex) = True
                    lngGeometryBreaks = lngGeometryBreaks + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "VECTOR_GEOMETRY|OLD=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|NEW=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(newDoc.Lines(lngNewIndex)) & _
                            "|distance=" & NumTxt(dblDistance) & _
                            "|FIX=Move/resize NEW segment to the OLD coordinates.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                        "VECTOR_GEOMETRY|" & BreaksLineOrientation(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)))
                Else
                    lngGeometryBreaks = lngGeometryBreaks + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "VECTOR_ONLY_OLD|OLD=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|style=" & BreaksLineStyle(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|FIX=Restore the corresponding Oracle vector segment.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                        "VECTOR_MISSING_NEW|" & BreaksLineOrientation(oldDoc.Lines(lngIndex)))
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    For lngIndex = 0 To newDoc.LineCount - 1
        If newDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Not IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_FIRST) And _
               Not IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_SECOND) Then

                If Not blnNewUsed(lngIndex) Then
                    lngGeometryBreaks = lngGeometryBreaks + 1
                    If lngWritten < BREAKS_MAX_DETAILS_PER_PAGE Then
                        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
                            "VECTOR_ONLY_NEW|NEW=" & CanonicalLineGeometryKey(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|style=" & BreaksLineStyle(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) & _
                            "|FIX=Remove or reconcile this extra segment with OLD.")
                        lngWritten = lngWritten + 1
                    End If
                    Call IncrementDictionaryValue(actionDictionary, _
                        "VECTOR_EXTRA_NEW|" & BreaksLineOrientation(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex)))
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    If lngGeometryBreaks > 0 Then
        criticalCount = criticalCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "VECTOR_GEOMETRY_SUMMARY|breaks=" & CStr(lngGeometryBreaks) & _
            "|classification=CRITICAL")
    End If

    If lngStyleBreaks > 0 Then
        structuralCount = structuralCount + 1
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "VECTOR_STYLE_SUMMARY|breaks=" & CStr(lngStyleBreaks) & _
            "|classification=STRUCTURAL")
    End If

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    oldDoc, newDoc, oldIndex, pageNo, newUsed, distance
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Finds the nearest unused NEW line with the same orientation.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindBreaksNearestLine( _
    ByRef oldDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByRef newDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal oldIndex As Long, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByRef newUsed() As Boolean, _
    ByRef distance As Double) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindBreaksNearestLine"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblCandidate As Double
    Dim dblExactBest As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngExactIndex As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    FindBreaksNearestLine = -1
    distance = 1E+30
    dblExactBest = 1E+30
    lngExactIndex = -1

    ' First pass: among geometrically matching candidates, prefer the one
    ' whose style already matches OLD. This prevents duplicate rectangle
    ' edges from creating false VECTOR_STYLE breaks.
    For lngIndex = 0 To newDoc.LineCount - 1
        If newDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Not newUsed(lngIndex) Then
                If Not IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_FIRST) And _
                   Not IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_SECOND) Then

                    dblCandidate = BreaksLineGeometryDistance( _
                        oldDoc.Lines(oldIndex), _
                        newDoc.Lines(lngIndex))

                    If dblCandidate <= BREAKS_LINE_MATCH_TOLERANCE Then
                        If BreaksLineStyleMatches( _
                            oldDoc.Lines(oldIndex), _
                            newDoc.Lines(lngIndex)) Then

                            If dblCandidate < dblExactBest Then
                                dblExactBest = dblCandidate
                                lngExactIndex = lngIndex
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    If lngExactIndex >= 0 Then
        FindBreaksNearestLine = lngExactIndex
        distance = dblExactBest
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    ' Second pass: no exact-style partner exists; use nearest geometry so a
    ' genuine style difference is still reported.
    For lngIndex = 0 To newDoc.LineCount - 1
        If newDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Not newUsed(lngIndex) Then
                If Not IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_FIRST) And _
                   Not IsVerticalAtX(newDoc.Lines(lngIndex), TARGET_X_SECOND) Then

                    dblCandidate = BreaksLineGeometryDistance( _
                        oldDoc.Lines(oldIndex), _
                        newDoc.Lines(lngIndex))

                    If dblCandidate < distance Then
                        distance = dblCandidate
                        FindBreaksNearestLine = lngIndex
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    distance = 1E+30
    FindBreaksNearestLine = -1

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "oldIndex;pageNo", _
        oldIndex, _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    firstLine, secondLine
' Returns:       Double
' Description:   Returns a coordinate distance for two lines of the same orientation.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksLineGeometryDistance( _
    ByRef firstLine As tPdfLine, _
    ByRef secondLine As tPdfLine) As Double

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BreaksLineGeometryDistance"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblValue As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strFirstOrientation As String
    Dim strSecondOrientation As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    BreaksLineGeometryDistance = 1E+30
    strFirstOrientation = BreaksLineOrientation(firstLine)
    strSecondOrientation = BreaksLineOrientation(secondLine)

    If strFirstOrientation <> strSecondOrientation Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    Select Case strFirstOrientation
        Case "V"
            dblValue = _
                Abs(((firstLine.X1 + firstLine.X2) / 2) - ((secondLine.X1 + secondLine.X2) / 2)) + _
                Abs(MinDouble(firstLine.Y1, firstLine.Y2) - MinDouble(secondLine.Y1, secondLine.Y2)) + _
                Abs(MaxDouble(firstLine.Y1, firstLine.Y2) - MaxDouble(secondLine.Y1, secondLine.Y2))

        Case "H"
            dblValue = _
                Abs(((firstLine.Y1 + firstLine.Y2) / 2) - ((secondLine.Y1 + secondLine.Y2) / 2)) + _
                Abs(MinDouble(firstLine.X1, firstLine.X2) - MinDouble(secondLine.X1, secondLine.X2)) + _
                Abs(MaxDouble(firstLine.X1, firstLine.X2) - MaxDouble(secondLine.X1, secondLine.X2))

        Case Else
            dblValue = _
                Abs(firstLine.X1 - secondLine.X1) + _
                Abs(firstLine.Y1 - secondLine.Y1) + _
                Abs(firstLine.X2 - secondLine.X2) + _
                Abs(firstLine.Y2 - secondLine.Y2)
    End Select

    BreaksLineGeometryDistance = dblValue

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BreaksLineGeometryDistance = 1E+30

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    lineInfo
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns H, V, or D for a normalized PDF line.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksLineOrientation(ByRef lineInfo As tPdfLine) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BreaksLineOrientation"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Abs(lineInfo.X1 - lineInfo.X2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
        BreaksLineOrientation = "V"
    ElseIf Abs(lineInfo.Y1 - lineInfo.Y2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
        BreaksLineOrientation = "H"
    Else
        BreaksLineOrientation = "D"
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BreaksLineOrientation = "?"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    firstLine, secondLine
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Compares the parser-visible line style fields.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksLineStyleMatches( _
    ByRef firstLine As tPdfLine, _
    ByRef secondLine As tPdfLine) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BreaksLineStyleMatches"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    BreaksLineStyleMatches = _
        Abs(firstLine.LineWidth - secondLine.LineWidth) <= 0.01 And _
        Trim$(firstLine.PaintOperator) = Trim$(secondLine.PaintOperator) And _
        Trim$(firstLine.SourceOperator) = Trim$(secondLine.SourceOperator) And _
        NormalizeBreaksStrokeColor(firstLine.StrokeColor) = _
            NormalizeBreaksStrokeColor(secondLine.StrokeColor)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BreaksLineStyleMatches = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    lineInfo
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Formats one line style for OLD versus NEW diagnostics.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksLineStyle(ByRef lineInfo As tPdfLine) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BreaksLineStyle"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    BreaksLineStyle = _
        "width=" & NumTxt(lineInfo.LineWidth) & _
        ",paint=" & lineInfo.PaintOperator & _
        ",source=" & lineInfo.SourceOperator & _
        ",color=" & lineInfo.StrokeColor

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BreaksLineStyle = "error"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo, normalizedNeedle
' Returns:       Double
' Description:   Finds a fixed label baseline after punctuation and whitespace normalization.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindBreaksTextBaseline( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal normalizedNeedle As String) As Double

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindBreaksTextBaseline"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
