    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If NormalizeBreaksLabel(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue) = normalizedNeedle Then
                FindBreaksTextBaseline = pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y
                Exit For
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FindBreaksTextBaseline = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;needle", _
        pageNo, _
        normalizedNeedle

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo, bottomHeaderY, unpairedRows
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Counts Oracle two-row transactions and exposes leftover rows that could indicate a split transaction.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function CountBreaksTradePairs( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal bottomHeaderY As Double, _
    ByRef unpairedRows As Long) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CountBreaksTradePairs"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrY() As Double
    Dim dblStep As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngPairs As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    unpairedRows = 0
    lngCount = CollectBreaksDataBaselines(pdfDoc, pageNo, bottomHeaderY, arrY)
    lngIndex = 0

    Do While lngIndex < lngCount
        If lngIndex + 1 < lngCount Then
            dblStep = arrY(lngIndex) - arrY(lngIndex + 1)
            If dblStep >= ORACLE_MIN_DATA_ROW_STEP And _
               dblStep <= ORACLE_MAX_DATA_ROW_STEP Then

                lngPairs = lngPairs + 1
                lngIndex = lngIndex + 2
            Else
                unpairedRows = unpairedRows + 1
                lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
            End If
        Else
            unpairedRows = unpairedRows + 1
            lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
        End If
    Loop

    CountBreaksTradePairs = lngPairs

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    CountBreaksTradePairs = 0
    unpairedRows = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;bottomHeaderY", _
        pageNo, _
        bottomHeaderY

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo, bottomHeaderY, baselines
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Collects unique data baselines between the bottom header and the bottom full-width table rule.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function CollectBreaksDataBaselines( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal bottomHeaderY As Double, _
    ByRef baselines() As Double) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CollectBreaksDataBaselines"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrRaw() As Double
    Dim dblBottomY As Double
    Dim dblSwap As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngInner As Long
    Dim lngUnique As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    ReDim arrRaw(0 To pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount + 4)
    dblBottomY = FindBreaksTableBottomY(pdfDoc, pageNo)

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X >= 40# And pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X <= 1324# Then
                If bottomHeaderY = 0 Or pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y < bottomHeaderY - BREAKS_BASELINE_TOLERANCE Then
                    If dblBottomY = 0 Or pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y > dblBottomY + BREAKS_BASELINE_TOLERANCE Then
                        If InStr(1, UCase$(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue), "DIESE ABRECHNUNG", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                            arrRaw(lngCount) = pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y
                            lngCount = lngCount + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    If lngCount = 0 Then
        ReDim baselines(0 To 0)
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    For lngIndex = 0 To lngCount - 2
        For lngInner = lngIndex + 1 To lngCount - 1
            If arrRaw(lngInner) > arrRaw(lngIndex) Then
                dblSwap = arrRaw(lngIndex)
                arrRaw(lngIndex) = arrRaw(lngInner)
                arrRaw(lngInner) = dblSwap
            End If
        Next lngInner
    Next lngIndex

    ReDim baselines(0 To lngCount - 1)
    baselines(0) = arrRaw(0)
    lngUnique = 1

    For lngIndex = 1 To lngCount - 1
        If Abs(arrRaw(lngIndex) - baselines(lngUnique - 1)) > BREAKS_BASELINE_TOLERANCE Then
            baselines(lngUnique) = arrRaw(lngIndex)
            lngUnique = lngUnique + 1
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    ReDim Preserve baselines(0 To lngUnique - 1)
    CollectBreaksDataBaselines = lngUnique

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    ReDim baselines(0 To 0)
    CollectBreaksDataBaselines = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;bottomHeaderY", _
        pageNo, _
        bottomHeaderY

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo
' Returns:       Double
' Description:   Finds the lowest full-width horizontal table rule.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindBreaksTableBottomY( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As Double

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindBreaksTableBottomY"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblY As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If Abs(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1 - pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2) <= COORD_TOLERANCE Then
                If Abs(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X2 - pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).X1) >= BREAKS_FULL_WIDTH_MIN Then
                    dblY = (pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y1 + pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).Y2) / 2
                    If FindBreaksTableBottomY = 0 Or dblY < FindBreaksTableBottomY Then
                        FindBreaksTableBottomY = dblY
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FindBreaksTableBottomY = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo, footer fields
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Finds the machine-generated statement footer and returns its anchor and font signature.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindBreaksFooter( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByRef footerText As String, _
    ByRef footerX As Double, _
    ByRef footerY As Double, _
    ByRef footerFont As String) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindBreaksFooter"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If InStr(1, UCase$(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue), "DIESE ABRECHNUNG", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
                footerText = pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).TextValue
                footerX = pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).X
                footerY = pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y
                footerFont = pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontName & "@" & NumTxt(pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).FontSize)
                FindBreaksFooter = True
                Exit For
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FindBreaksFooter = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Counts unique visual text baselines with Oracle jitter tolerance.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function CountBreaksTextBaselines( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CountBreaksTextBaselines"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrY() As Double
    Dim dblSwap As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngCount As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngInner As Long
    Dim lngUnique As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    ReDim arrY(0 To pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).TextCount + 2)

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.TextCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            arrY(lngCount) = pdfDoc.Texts(lngIndex).Y
            lngCount = lngCount + 1
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    If lngCount = 0 Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    For lngIndex = 0 To lngCount - 2
        For lngInner = lngIndex + 1 To lngCount - 1
            If arrY(lngInner) > arrY(lngIndex) Then
                dblSwap = arrY(lngIndex)
                arrY(lngIndex) = arrY(lngInner)
                arrY(lngInner) = dblSwap
            End If
        Next lngInner
    Next lngIndex

    lngUnique = 1
    For lngIndex = 1 To lngCount - 1
        If Abs(arrY(lngIndex) - arrY(lngIndex - 1)) > BREAKS_BASELINE_TOLERANCE Then
            lngUnique = lngUnique + 1
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    CountBreaksTextBaselines = lngUnique

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    CountBreaksTextBaselines = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo", _
        pageNo

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo, targetX
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Counts vertical target-separator segments on one page.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function CountBreaksTargetOccurrences( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal targetX As Double) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "CountBreaksTargetOccurrences"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    For lngIndex = 0 To pdfDoc.LineCount - 1
        If pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex).PageNo = pageNo Then
            If IsVerticalAtX(pdfDoc.Lines(lngIndex), targetX) Then
                CountBreaksTargetOccurrences = CountBreaksTargetOccurrences + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next lngIndex

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    CountBreaksTargetOccurrences = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;targetX", _
        pageNo, _
        targetX

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

 '-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    pdfDoc, pageNo, textIndex
' Returns:       Boolean
' Description:   Identifies header/footer text that must not be ignored as a variable input value.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function IsBreaksFixedTextRole( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal textIndex As Long) As Boolean

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "IsBreaksFixedTextRole"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim dblBottomHeaderY As Double
    Dim dblTopHeaderY As Double
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If InStr(1, UCase$(pdfDoc.Texts(textIndex).TextValue), "DIESE ABRECHNUNG", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
        IsBreaksFixedTextRole = True
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    dblTopHeaderY = FindBreaksTextBaseline(pdfDoc, pageNo, "DEPOTNR")
    dblBottomHeaderY = FindBreaksTextBaseline(pdfDoc, pageNo, "TRANSAKTIONSNR")

    If dblTopHeaderY <> 0 Then
        If Abs(pdfDoc.Texts(textIndex).Y - dblTopHeaderY) <= BREAKS_GEOMETRY_TEXT_Y_TOLERANCE Then
            IsBreaksFixedTextRole = True
            GoTo ExitFunction
        End If
    End If

    If dblBottomHeaderY <> 0 Then
        If Abs(pdfDoc.Texts(textIndex).Y - dblBottomHeaderY) <= BREAKS_GEOMETRY_TEXT_Y_TOLERANCE Then
            IsBreaksFixedTextRole = True
        End If
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    IsBreaksFixedTextRole = False

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "pageNo;textIndex", _
        pageNo, _
        textIndex

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    value
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Normalizes horizontal whitespace without changing business characters.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksTextFontSemanticSignature( _
    ByRef pdfDoc As tPdfDocument, _
    ByVal pageNo As Long, _
    ByVal resourceName As String) As String

    Dim arrEntries() As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strEntry As String
    Dim strPrefix As String

    If pageNo < 1 Or pageNo > pdfDoc.PageCount Then
        BreaksTextFontSemanticSignature = resourceName
        Exit Function
    End If

    strPrefix = resourceName & "="
    arrEntries = Split(pdfDoc.Pages(pageNo).ResolvedFonts, ";")

    For lngIndex = LBound(arrEntries) To UBound(arrEntries)
        strEntry = Trim$(arrEntries(lngIndex))
        If Left$(strEntry, Len(strPrefix)) = strPrefix Then
            BreaksTextFontSemanticSignature = _
                NormalizeBreaksFontSignature( _
                    Mid$(strEntry, Len(strPrefix) + 1))
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    BreaksTextFontSemanticSignature = resourceName
End Function

Private Function BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature( _
    ByVal resolvedFonts As String) As String

    Dim arrEntries() As String
    Dim arrKeys As Variant
    Dim dictSemantic As Object
    Dim lngEquals As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strEntry As String
    Dim strSemantic As String
    Dim strResult As String

    Set dictSemantic = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    If Len(Trim$(resolvedFonts)) = 0 Or resolvedFonts = "(none)" Then
        BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature = "(none)"
        Set dictSemantic = Nothing
        Exit Function
    End If

    arrEntries = Split(resolvedFonts, ";")

    For lngIndex = LBound(arrEntries) To UBound(arrEntries)
        strEntry = Trim$(arrEntries(lngIndex))
        lngEquals = InStr(1, strEntry, "=", vbBinaryCompare)

        If lngEquals > 0 Then
            strSemantic = NormalizeBreaksFontSignature( _
                Mid$(strEntry, lngEquals + 1))
        Else
            strSemantic = NormalizeBreaksFontSignature(strEntry)
        End If

        If Len(strSemantic) > 0 Then
            dictSemantic(strSemantic) = strSemantic
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    If dictSemantic.Count = 0 Then
        BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature = "(none)"
    Else
        arrKeys = SortedDictionaryKeys(dictSemantic)
        For lngIndex = LBound(arrKeys) To UBound(arrKeys)
            If Len(strResult) > 0 Then strResult = strResult & ";"
            strResult = strResult & CStr(arrKeys(lngIndex))
        Next lngIndex
        BreaksSemanticPageFontSetSignature = strResult
    End If

    Set dictSemantic = Nothing
End Function

Private Function NormalizeBreaksStrokeColor(ByVal value As String) As String

    Dim arrParts() As String
    Dim lngColon As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strPrefix As String
    Dim strResult As String
    Dim strValues As String

    value = Trim$(value)
    lngColon = InStr(1, value, ":", vbBinaryCompare)

    If lngColon <= 0 Then
        NormalizeBreaksStrokeColor = value
        Exit Function
    End If

    strPrefix = UCase$(Trim$(Left$(value, lngColon - 1)))
    strValues = Mid$(value, lngColon + 1)
    arrParts = Split(strValues, ",")

    For lngIndex = LBound(arrParts) To UBound(arrParts)
        If Len(strResult) > 0 Then strResult = strResult & ","
        strResult = strResult & NumTxt(Val(Trim$(arrParts(lngIndex))))
    Next lngIndex

    NormalizeBreaksStrokeColor = strPrefix & ":" & strResult
End Function

Private Function NormalizeBreaksFontSignature(ByVal value As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "NormalizeBreaksFontSignature"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strHex As String
    Dim strResult As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    lngIndex = 1
    Do While lngIndex <= Len(value)
        If Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1) = "#" And lngIndex + 2 <= Len(value) Then
            strHex = Mid$(value, lngIndex + 1, 2)
            If IsBreaksHexPair(strHex) Then
                strResult = strResult & Chr$(CLng("&H" & strHex))
                lngIndex = lngIndex + 3
            Else
                strResult = strResult & Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1)
                lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
            End If
        Else
            strResult = strResult & Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1)
            lngIndex = lngIndex + 1
        End If
    Loop

    strResult = Replace(strResult, vbTab, " ")
    strResult = Replace(strResult, vbCr, " ")
    strResult = Replace(strResult, vbLf, " ")
    Do While InStr(1, strResult, "  ", vbBinaryCompare) > 0
        strResult = Replace(strResult, "  ", " ")
    Loop

    NormalizeBreaksFontSignature = Trim$(strResult)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    NormalizeBreaksFontSignature = value

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

Private Function IsBreaksHexPair(ByVal value As String) As Boolean
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strChar As String

    If Len(value) <> 2 Then Exit Function

    For lngIndex = 1 To 2
        strChar = UCase$(Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1))
        If InStr(1, "0123456789ABCDEF", strChar, vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    IsBreaksHexPair = True
End Function

Private Function BreaksVisibleWhitespace(ByVal value As String) As String
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strChar As String
    Dim strResult As String

    For lngIndex = 1 To Len(value)
        strChar = Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1)

        Select Case strChar
            Case " "
                strResult = strResult & "[SP]"
            Case vbTab
                strResult = strResult & "[TAB]"
            Case vbCr
                strResult = strResult & "[CR]"
            Case vbLf
                strResult = strResult & "[LF]"
            Case Chr$(160)
                strResult = strResult & "[NBSP]"
            Case Else
                strResult = strResult & strChar
        End Select

        If Len(strResult) > 180 Then
            strResult = Left$(strResult, 180) & "..."
            Exit For
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    If Len(strResult) = 0 Then strResult = "(empty)"
    BreaksVisibleWhitespace = strResult
End Function

Private Function BreaksLeadingSpaceCount(ByVal value As String) As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strChar As String

    For lngIndex = 1 To Len(value)
        strChar = Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1)
        If strChar = " " Or strChar = Chr$(160) Or strChar = vbTab Then
            BreaksLeadingSpaceCount = BreaksLeadingSpaceCount + 1
        Else
            Exit For
        End If
    Next lngIndex
End Function

Private Function BreaksTrailingSpaceCount(ByVal value As String) As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strChar As String

    For lngIndex = Len(value) To 1 Step -1
        strChar = Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1)
        If strChar = " " Or strChar = Chr$(160) Or strChar = vbTab Then
            BreaksTrailingSpaceCount = BreaksTrailingSpaceCount + 1
        Else
            Exit For
        End If
    Next lngIndex
End Function

Private Function NormalizeBreaksWhitespace(ByVal value As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "NormalizeBreaksWhitespace"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strValue As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strValue = Replace(value, vbTab, " ")
    strValue = Replace(strValue, vbCr, " ")
    strValue = Replace(strValue, vbLf, " ")
    strValue = Replace(strValue, Chr$(160), " ")

    Do While InStr(1, strValue, "  ", vbBinaryCompare) > 0
        strValue = Replace(strValue, "  ", " ")
    Loop

    NormalizeBreaksWhitespace = Trim$(strValue)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    NormalizeBreaksWhitespace = value

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    value
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Produces a punctuation-free uppercase key for fixed Oracle labels.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function NormalizeBreaksLabel(ByVal value As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "NormalizeBreaksLabel"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strCharacter As String
    Dim strResult As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    value = UCase$(Trim$(value))
    For lngIndex = 1 To Len(value)
        strCharacter = Mid$(value, lngIndex, 1)
        If (strCharacter >= "A" And strCharacter <= "Z") Or _
           (strCharacter >= "0" And strCharacter <= "9") Then

            strResult = strResult & strCharacter
        End If
    Next lngIndex

    NormalizeBreaksLabel = strResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    NormalizeBreaksLabel = UCase$(Trim$(value))

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    value
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Sanitizes and truncates text so the concise report stays readable.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksShortText(ByVal value As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BreaksShortText"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strValue As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strValue = SanitizeReportText(value)
    If Len(strValue) > 80 Then
        strValue = Left$(strValue, 77) & "..."
    End If
    BreaksShortText = strValue

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BreaksShortText = Left$(value, 80)

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    shiftX, shiftY
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Formats the exact NEW-to-OLD translation as a human-readable fix.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BreaksMoveFix( _
    ByVal shiftX As Double, _
    ByVal shiftY As Double) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BreaksMoveFix"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strResult As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strResult = "MOVE_NEW"
    If Abs(shiftX) > BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE Then
        If shiftX > 0 Then
            strResult = strResult & " RIGHT " & NumTxt(shiftX) & "pt"
        Else
            strResult = strResult & " LEFT " & NumTxt(Abs(shiftX)) & "pt"
        End If
    End If

    If Abs(shiftY) > BREAKS_POSITION_TOLERANCE Then
        If shiftY > 0 Then
            strResult = strResult & " UP " & NumTxt(shiftY) & "pt"
        Else
            strResult = strResult & " DOWN " & NumTxt(Abs(shiftY)) & "pt"
        End If
    End If

    If strResult = "MOVE_NEW" Then
        strResult = strResult & " 0pt"
    End If

    BreaksMoveFix = strResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BreaksMoveFix = "MOVE_NEW"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-07
' Parameters:    actionDictionary, fileNumber
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes grouped recurring fixes, useful for six-page and larger reports.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteBreaksActionSummary( _
    ByVal actionDictionary As Object, _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "WriteBreaksActionSummary"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim arrKeys As Variant
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim strKey As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If actionDictionary.Count = 0 Then
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, "NO_ACTIONS|All parser-sensitive checks in BREAKS_ONLY passed.")
        GoTo ExitSub
    End If

    arrKeys = SortedDictionaryKeys(actionDictionary)
    For lngIndex = LBound(arrKeys) To UBound(arrKeys)
        strKey = CStr(arrKeys(lngIndex))
        Call EmitLine(fileNumber, _
            "ACTION|count=" & CStr(actionDictionary(strKey)) & "|" & strKey)
    Next lngIndex

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    filePath As String
' Returns:       Integer
' Description:   Opens a new binary text report file and returns its file number.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function OpenNew(ByVal filePath As String) As Long
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "OpenNew"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngFileNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If Len(Dir$(filePath)) > 0 Then
        Kill filePath
    End If
    lngFileNumber = FreeFile
    Open filePath For Binary Access Write As #lngFileNumber
    OpenNew = lngFileNumber

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    OpenNew = 0

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "filePath", _
        filePath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    fileNumber As Long, textLine As String
' Returns:       ---
' Description:   Writes one CRLF-terminated line to a binary text report.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub EmitLine( _
    ByVal fileNumber As Long, _
    ByVal textLine As String)

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "EmitLine"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrBytes() As Byte

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    arrBytes = StrConv(textLine & vbCrLf, vbFromUnicode)
    Put #fileNumber, , arrBytes

ExitSub:
    Exit Sub

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "fileNumber", _
        fileNumber

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitSub
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    data As Byte array, fromIndex As Long, toIndex As Long
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Converts a byte range to a VBA string.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function BytesText( _
    ByRef data() As Byte, _
    ByVal fromIndex As Long, _
    ByVal toIndex As Long) As String

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "BytesText"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrTemporary() As Byte
    Dim lngIndex As Long
    Dim lngLength As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If fromIndex < 0 Then
        fromIndex = 0
    End If
    If toIndex > UBound(data) Then
        toIndex = UBound(data)
    End If
    If toIndex < fromIndex Then
        GoTo ExitFunction
    End If

    lngLength = toIndex - fromIndex + 1
    ReDim arrTemporary(0 To lngLength - 1)
    For lngIndex = 0 To lngLength - 1
        arrTemporary(lngIndex) = data(fromIndex + lngIndex)
    Next lngIndex
    BytesText = StrConv(arrTemporary, vbUnicode)

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    BytesText = vbNullString

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "fromIndex;toIndex", _
        fromIndex, _
        toIndex

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    data As Byte array, startAt As Long, pattern As String
' Returns:       Long
' Description:   Finds an ASCII byte pattern in a PDF byte array.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FindBytes( _
    ByRef data() As Byte, _
    ByVal startAt As Long, _
    ByVal pattern As String) As Long

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FindBytes"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim arrPattern() As Byte
    Dim lngDataIndex As Long
    Dim lngPatternIndex As Long
    Dim lngPatternLength As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    arrPattern = StrConv(pattern, vbFromUnicode)
    lngPatternLength = UBound(arrPattern) - LBound(arrPattern) + 1
    If startAt < 0 Then
        startAt = 0
    End If

    For lngDataIndex = startAt To UBound(data) - lngPatternLength + 1
        If data(lngDataIndex) = arrPattern(0) Then
            For lngPatternIndex = 1 To lngPatternLength - 1
                If data(lngDataIndex + lngPatternIndex) <> arrPattern(lngPatternIndex) Then
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next lngPatternIndex
            If lngPatternIndex = lngPatternLength Then
                FindBytes = lngDataIndex
                GoTo ExitFunction
            End If
        End If
    Next lngDataIndex

    FindBytes = -1

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FindBytes = -1

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "startAt;pattern", _
        startAt, _
        pattern

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    filePath As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Returns the file name without folder and extension.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function FileBaseName(ByVal filePath As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "FileBaseName"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim lngDotPosition As Long
    Dim strName As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strName = Mid$(filePath, InStrRev(filePath, "\") + 1)
    lngDotPosition = InStrRev(strName, ".")
    If lngDotPosition > 1 Then
        strName = Left$(strName, lngDotPosition - 1)
    End If
    FileBaseName = strName

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    FileBaseName = "pdf"

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription, _
        "filePath", _
        filePath

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    value As Double
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Formats a PDF coordinate with a dot decimal separator.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function NumTxt(ByVal value As Double) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "NumTxt"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    NumTxt = Replace(Format$(value, "0.##"), ",", ".")

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    NumTxt = CStr(value)

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    firstValue As Double, secondValue As Double
' Returns:       Double
' Description:   Returns the smaller of two Double values.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function MinDouble( _
    ByVal firstValue As Double, _
    ByVal secondValue As Double) As Double

    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "MinDouble"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    If firstValue < secondValue Then
        MinDouble = firstValue
    Else
        MinDouble = secondValue
    End If

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    MinDouble = firstValue

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

    ByVal secondValue As Double) As Double

    Dim errNumber As Long

        MaxDouble = firstValue

        MaxDouble = secondValue
    End If

    Exit Function

    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    MaxDouble = firstValue

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function

'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Author:        Pawel Ligezka
' Creation date: 2026-08-06
' Parameters:    textValue As String
' Returns:       String
' Description:   Removes line-breaking report delimiters from extracted PDF text.
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function SanitizeReportText(ByVal textValue As String) As String
    Const METHOD_NAME As String = "SanitizeReportText"
    Dim errorManager As New ErrorManager
    Dim errDescription As String
    Dim errNumber As Long
    Dim strResult As String

    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler

    strResult = Replace(textValue, vbCr, " ")
    strResult = Replace(strResult, vbLf, " ")
    strResult = Replace(strResult, "|", "/")
    SanitizeReportText = strResult

ExitFunction:
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    errNumber = VBA.Err.Number
    errDescription = VBA.Err.Description
    SanitizeReportText = textValue

    errorManager.addError _
        CLASS_NAME, _
        METHOD_NAME, _
        errNumber, _
        errDescription

    errorManager.save
    Resume ExitFunction
End Function
